## כ (id: BDB04305)

כ, כּ eleventh letter; used as numeral 20 in post B Heb.; כא \= 21, כב \= 22, etc.



## כְּ (id: BDB04306)

כְּ prop. **subst.** the like of, like, as (Aram. כ, Ph. כ, Ar. كَ, Sab. in כעד דא *as* till now DHMZMG xxix. 615, As. *Ki, kima*, DlHWB 325 f.)—before tone\-syll. כָּ, as כָּזֶה, כָּאֵ֫לֶּה, כָּחֵ֫מָּה; c. sf. כָּכֶם [Nu 15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15) \+ 7 t. ([Jb 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4) כָּכֵם: Köii. 1\. 285\), כָּהֶם † [2 K 17:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:15), כָּהֶן † Ez [18:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:14) \[also with nom. forms, כָּהֵ֫מָּה, כָּהֵם, כָּהֵ֫נָּה; v. הֵמָּה **8**; cf. Arab. كَأَنَاء, كَأَنْتَ, etc.], before light sf., in the form כְּמוֺ (q.v.), viz. כָּמֹנִי, כָּמוֺנִי [Gn 44:15](https://ref.ly/Gen44:15) \+ 16 t., כָּמוְֺ [Gn 41:39](https://ref.ly/Gen41:39) \+ 28 t., כָּמֹכָה † [Ex 15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11), כָּמֹהוּ [9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18) \+ 23 t., כָּמוֺהָ [11:6](https://ref.ly/Exod11:6) \+ 2 t., כָּמֹנוֺ [Gn 34:15](https://ref.ly/Gen34:15) \+ 3 t.; also כְּמוֺכֶם † [Jb 12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3), כְּמוֺהֶם † Ju [8:18](https://ref.ly/Judg8:18), [Psalm 115:8](https://ref.ly/Ps115:8), [135:18](https://ref.ly/Ps135:18). Prop. an undeveloped *subst.*, capable of standing in any case and followed (like كَ) always by a gen., as [1 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:3) כפשע ´כ for there is (nomin.) *the like of* a footstep (Ar. كَخَتْوَةِ) between me and death, [Gn 41:38](https://ref.ly/Gen41:38) הנמצא כזה shall be we find (accus.) *the like of* this one ? (v. esp. FleischerKl. Schrr. i. 376 ff. Mühlau, *ap.* Böii. 64 f., with many Arabic exx.; also WAG i. § 356 R. c.; ii. § 63 Ges§ 118\. 6; compare, for both usage and constr., the Lat. *instar:* this view criticized by Köii. 1, 279 ff., who treats כְּ as demonstr. adv.); but Heb. having no case\-endings, its proper sense was doubtless forgotten, and כְּ became practically equivalent to a prep. *like, as:*—**1\. a.** *quantitatively* (like the Ar. قدْر *measure, number, size, of…:* قَدْرُ مِييءَةِ *as many as* 100\), to express exact or approx. equality, in the latter case *the like of* is \= *about:* [Dt 1:11](https://ref.ly/Deut1:11) Yahweh add to you ככם אלף פעמים *the like of you* (Saad. مِثْلَكُم) 1000 times (cf. [2 S 24:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:3)), [Ex 12:37](https://ref.ly/Exod12:37) כשׁשׁ מאוֺת אלף רגלי *the like of* (i.e. *about*) 600,000 footmen, [32:28](https://ref.ly/Exod32:28) כשׁלשׁת אלפי אישׁ, [Jos 4:13](https://ref.ly/Josh4:13), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Josh7:3), [1 S 9:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:22), [25:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:38) ויהי כעשרת הימים (nomin.) and there was *the like of* (\= *about*) 10 days, and, etc. [Ru 2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17) כאיפה שערים, etc., cf. כַּמָּה *the like of* what ? \= *how many* ? (v. מָה); as accus. of space or time, [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31) כדרך יום *the like of* a day's journey, [Jos 3:4](https://ref.ly/Josh3:4), [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) כיום תמים *about* a whole day (Ar. نَحْوَ يَوْمٍ كَامِلٍ), [Ru 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:4) כעשר שׁגים. So (as adv. accus.) with words denoting a *point* of time: often with עֵת, as כָּעֵת הַזּאֹת lit. at *the like of* this time \= *about* this time, [Jos 11:6](https://ref.ly/Josh11:6) al., כָּעֵת מָחָר at *the like of* the time, (being) to\-morrow \= *about to\-morrow*, [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18) \+, כָּעֵת חַיָּה (v. חי), [Is 8:23](https://ref.ly/Isa8:23) כעת הראשׁין *at* the former time, כָּעֵת alone \= *at the* (present) *time, now* [Nu 23:23](https://ref.ly/Num23:23), Ju [13:23](https://ref.ly/Judg13:23), [Is 23:5](https://ref.ly/Isa23:5) (unusual) כְּשֵׁמַע חֹר; so כְּהַיּוֺם, כַּיּוֺם (v. יוֺם **7 h**), כִּמְעַט, כְּרֶגַע; and esp. with inf., v. **3 b. b.** *qualitatively* (like Ar. مَثَلُ *likeness of…*), to express resemblance in respect of some attribute, action, character, appearance, etc.: so very often; as subj. of a sentence, [Nu 9:15](https://ref.ly/Num9:15) כמראה אשׁ *the like of* (\= *as it were*) an appearance of fire was, etc. (cf. Ez [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:4), [1:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:26) a., [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27), [Dn 8:15](https://ref.ly/Dan8:15), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Dan10:18)), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Num23:22) כתועפות ראם לו, [Ex 24:10](https://ref.ly/Exod24:10), [Dt 4:32](https://ref.ly/Deut4:32) הנהיה כדבר הגדול הזה hath there been *the like of* this great thing ? [9:10](https://ref.ly/Deut9:10), [Jos 10:14](https://ref.ly/Josh10:14); [Ho 9:1](https://ref.ly/Hos9:1), [Is 23:15](https://ref.ly/Isa23:15), [Psalm 58:5](https://ref.ly/Ps58:5), [Pr 16:27](https://ref.ly/Prov16:27), [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20) בַּבַּיִת כַּמָּוֶת, [Ec 8:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:14); [Lv 14:35](https://ref.ly/Lev14:35) כְּנֶגַע נראה בביתי *the like of* (i.e. *a kind of*) mark; מִי כָמוֺכָה who is *the like of* thee ? [Ex 15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11) (Saad. مً مَثَلُكَ) \+; as gen. אֵין כָּמוְֺ [1 S 10:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:24), [2 S 7:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:22) \+; as pred., Ju [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21) כָּאִישׁ גְּבוּרָתוֺ, Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5) וְהוּא כַמָּוֶת, [Psalm 17:14](https://ref.ly/Ps17:14), [89:30](https://ref.ly/Ps89:30) וכסאו כימי שׁמים (of time: so v:[37](https://ref.ly/Ps89:37)), [125:1](https://ref.ly/Ps125:1), etc.; after a verb, היה כְּ to become *the like of* (*like*), [Gn 3:5](https://ref.ly/Gen3:5) \+ oft. (v. היה **II. 2 c**), cf. after שִים [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16) \+, נתן [42:30](https://ref.ly/Gen42:30) \+, שִׁית [Psalm 83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12) \+, שִׁוָּה [18:34](https://ref.ly/Ps18:34), נֶחְשַׁב [44:23](https://ref.ly/Ps44:23) \+, נִמְשַׁל [49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), הִרְבָּה [Gn 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17) \+; add כָּזאֹת, כָּאֵלֶּה *the like of* this (these), i.e. *tale, talia* (whether nom. or accus.) Ju [13:23](https://ref.ly/Judg13:23), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7), [20:30](https://ref.ly/Judg20:30), [Lv 10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19) \+ (v. זאֹת, אֵלֶּה). Cases such as to give or reward a man כמעשהו, כרעתו, etc., *the like of* his doings, etc., [2 S 3:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:39), [1 K 8:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:32), [Psalm 18:25](https://ref.ly/Ps18:25), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Ps28:4), to speak כדבר הזה or כדברים האלה [Gn 18:25](https://ref.ly/Gen18:25), [24:28](https://ref.ly/Gen24:28) \+ oft., form the transition to **c.** as an accus. of mode or limitation, *in* or *with the like of* (\= *like, as according to*, κατά);—(1\) expressing conformity to a standard or rule: [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26) let us make man כדמותנו *according to* our likeness, [9:3](https://ref.ly/Gen9:3), [Jos 19:47](https://ref.ly/Josh19:47), Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [1 S 13:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:14) hath looked out אישׁ כלבבו a man *according to* his own heart ([Je 3:15](https://ref.ly/Jer3:15)); to do a thing כמשׁפט הזה [Jos 6:15](https://ref.ly/Josh6:15), cf. [Gn 40:13](https://ref.ly/Gen40:13), [2 K 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:14), etc., ´כדבר פ [Gn 44:2](https://ref.ly/Gen44:2) \+ oft., כְּצִדְקִי [Psalm 7:9](https://ref.ly/Ps7:9), כְּחַסְדְּ [51:3](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3), בַּכָּתוּב *according to* what is written [Jos 8:31](https://ref.ly/Josh8:31), [8:34](https://ref.ly/Josh8:34) \+, בלא ככחיב (late) † [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18), etc. (2\) in comparisons: qualifying an adj., [Ex 16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14) דַּק כַּכְּפוֺר, Ct [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10) יָפָה כַלְּבָנָה, cf. [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10); a verb [Psalm 7:3](https://ref.ly/Ps7:3) פֶּן־יִטְרֹף כְּאַרְיֵה, 19:6 יָשִיש כְּגִבּוֺר, [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13), etc.; with the *tertium compare*. attached in a relative clause, [Dt 32:11](https://ref.ly/Deut32:11) בַּנֶּשֶׁר יָעִיר קִנִּי *like* the great vulture, (that) stirreth up its nest, [Jb 9:26](https://ref.ly/Job9:26), [11:16](https://ref.ly/Job11:16), [Is 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10) *like* the bride, who putteth on, etc., [62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1) כְּלַפִּיד יִבְעַר *like* a touch (that) burns (on the presence or absence of the art. in such cases, v. הַ **1 f**), [Psalm 42:2](https://ref.ly/Ps42:2), [83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15) (infr. **2 d**) \+ oft. (cf. Dr§ 34: כְּ is not in these cases to be construed as a conj. \[ \= כַּאֲשֶׁכ] with the verb: v. De:[Psalm 38:14](https://ref.ly/Ps38:14) Ges§ 155, 2 b, 1 R.). **d.** sometimes כְּ is used in partic. to compare an object with the *class* to which it belongs, and express its correspondence with the idea which it ought to realize: [Is 13:6](https://ref.ly/Isa13:6) ( \= [Jo 1:15](https://ref.ly/Joel1:15)) of the ´יום י, כְּשֹׁד מִשַּׁדֵּי יָבוֺא i.e. it comes as a *veritable*, or *ideal*, destruction from Shaddai, [29:2](https://ref.ly/Isa29:2) of Ariel (v:[1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1)) והיתה לי כאריאל i.e. shall be to me as a *true* Ariel (cf. Ges§ 118 x): so also, acc. to Hi De, [10:13](https://ref.ly/Isa10:13), Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10), [Zc 14:3](https://ref.ly/Zech14:3), [2 S 9:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:8), [Psalm 122:3](https://ref.ly/Ps122:3), Ct [8:10](https://ref.ly/Song8:10) (v. De), [Ec 10:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:5), [Ne 7:2](https://ref.ly/Neh7:2). The older grammarians called this the *Kaph veritatis* (a translations of Ki's ף הָאֲמִתּוּת“כ, ף לאמת הדבר“כ, Michl45 a (Lyck), Comm:[Jos 3:4](https://ref.ly/Josh3:4), [1 S 9:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:13), [Is 1:7](https://ref.ly/Isa1:7), [Ho 5:10](https://ref.ly/Hos5:10), or ף הָאֲמִתִּי“כ, Lex s.v. יוֺם): they extended it unduly (e.g. to [Nu 11:1](https://ref.ly/Num11:1), [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20), [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4)). **2\.** repeated, כְּ…כְּ, to signify the completeness of the correspondency between two objects (peculiar to Heb.): **a.** in a principal clause, [Gn 18:25](https://ref.ly/Gen18:25) וְהָיָה כַצַּדִּיק בָּרָשָׁע lit. that *the like of* the righteous be *the like of* the wicked, i.e. that the righteous be *as* the wicked, [44:18](https://ref.ly/Gen44:18) כמיך כפרעה *the like of* thee is *the like of* Pharaoh, i.e. *thou* art as Pharaoh, [Lv 24:22](https://ref.ly/Lev24:22) כַּגֵּר כָּאֶזְרָח יִהְיֶה, [1 K 22:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:4) כָּמֹנִי כָמוְֺ כְּעַמִּי כְעַמְּ (Van Dyck مَثَلِي مَثَلُكَ شَعْبِي كَشَأْبِكَ), [Hg 2:3](https://ref.ly/Hag2:3) הֲלוֺא כָמֹהוּ כְּאַיִן בְּעֵינֵיכֶם is not *the like of* it *the like of* nothing in your eyes ? i.e. is it not like nothing in your eyes ? [Psalm 139:12](https://ref.ly/Ps139:12) כַּחֲשֵׁיכָה כָּאוֺרָה i.e. the darkness (to thee) is *as* the light. Usu. the first term is the subj. and the second is the standard with which it is compared: but occas. the terms are inverted, as [Lv 7:7](https://ref.ly/Lev7:7), [Nu 15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), Ju [8:18](https://ref.ly/Judg8:18) כמוך כמוהם (Van Dyck مَثَلُهُم مَثَلُكَ), [Ho 4:9](https://ref.ly/Hos4:9), [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2), cf. [59:18](https://ref.ly/Isa59:18) *Note*, infr.) **b.** in a subordinate clause (to be regarded as subordinated in the accus. to the principal verb); (α) attached to the *subj.* of the principal vb., [Lv 24:16](https://ref.ly/Lev24:16) יומת…כגר כאזרח he shall be put to death, *the like of* the stranger (being) *the like of* the homeborn, [Jos 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33). (β) attached to its *object*, [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17) כקטן כגדל תשׁמעון lit. ye shall hear (them), *the like of* the small (being) *the like of* the great. **c.** occas., for כְּ…כְּ, there occurs וכ…בְּ: [Jos 14:11](https://ref.ly/Josh14:11) כְּכֹחִי אָז וּכְכֹחִי עָתָּה *the like of* my strength then, and *the like of* my strength now, i.e. they are similar (cf. וְ **1 j**) [1 S 30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24), Ez [18:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:4), [Dn 11:29](https://ref.ly/Dan11:29). **d.** yet more distinct than כְּ…כְּ, is כֵּן…כְּ; in a nominal sentence (Ges§ 140–1\), ‘כְּ with its genit. forming the predic., and כֵּן resuming it with emph. and connecting it with the subject;’ [1 S 25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25) כִּי כִשְׁמוֺ כֶּן־הוּא for *the like of* his name, *such* (or *so*) is he, [Gn 44:10](https://ref.ly/Gen44:10), [Jos 2:21](https://ref.ly/Josh2:21), [Je 18:6](https://ref.ly/Jer18:6), [Psalm 48:11](https://ref.ly/Ps48:11), [127:4](https://ref.ly/Ps127:4), [Pr 10:26](https://ref.ly/Prov10:26) \+; in a verbal sentence, ‘כְּ preceding, as an accusative of state, and giving the *secundum comparationis*, and כֵּן resuming it,’ [Jo 2:4](https://ref.ly/Joel2:4) כפרשׁים כן ירוצון ‘*instar* (accus.) equitum, *sic* currunt’ (Fll. c.), [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7) lit. *with* (or *in) the likeness of* their multiplying, *so* they sinned against me, [Gn 6:22](https://ref.ly/Gen6:22), [1 S 8:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:8), [Is 38:14](https://ref.ly/Isa38:14), Ez [22:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:22), [Psalm 42:2](https://ref.ly/Ps42:2), [83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15) \+ oft. **3\.** before an inf.: **a.** *like* (lit. *with* or *in the likeness of…*), Ju [14:6](https://ref.ly/Judg14:6) כְּשַׁסַּע הַגְּדִי *like* the rending of a kid, i.e. *as when* a kid is rent (by a lion), [2 S 3:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:34), [Is 5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24), [Psalm 66:10](https://ref.ly/Ps66:10), [68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3), [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10), etc.; \= *as if*, [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15) or shall the saw magnify itself against him that wieldeth it כהניף שׁבט את־מרימיו *with the like of* a staff's shaking (\= *as if* a staff were to shake) him that lifted it ! **b.** of time, *about, at*, whether of the past or of the future: [Gn 19:17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17) כהוציאם אותם *at* their bringing them forth, i.e. *as* or *when* they brought them forth [24:30](https://ref.ly/Gen24:30), [29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13), [39:10](https://ref.ly/Gen39:10), [39:13](https://ref.ly/Gen39:13) \+ very often; of the future, [44:30](https://ref.ly/Gen44:30), [44:31](https://ref.ly/Gen44:31), [Dt 20:2](https://ref.ly/Deut20:2), [20:9](https://ref.ly/Deut20:9), [2 S 13:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:28) \+; [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20) b (different from v:[29](https://ref.ly/Isa28:29) a) and the covering is (too) narrow *when one gathers oneself* in. Occasionally with the verbal noun, [Ho 13:6](https://ref.ly/Hos13:6) כמרעיתם *at the time of* their feeding, [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19) כְּשָׁמְעָתוֺ; וּכְחֶזְקָתוֺ † [2 Ch 12:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:1), [26:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16), [Dn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Dan11:2) (Ew§ 238 a, 239 a): cf. [Is 23:5](https://ref.ly/Isa23:5) b כְּשֵׁמַע חֹר. With the ptcp. † [Gn 38:29](https://ref.ly/Gen38:29) (si vera l.), perh. [40:10](https://ref.ly/Gen40:10); cf. Dr§ 135\. 6, *Obs.* 2\.

 *Note.*—כְּ is rarely prefixed to preps., the only exx. which occur being the idiomatic כְּבָרִאשֹׁנָה, † Ju [20:32](https://ref.ly/Judg20:32), [1 K 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:6), [Is 1:26](https://ref.ly/Isa1:26), [Jer 33:7](https://ref.ly/Jer33:7), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Jer33:11); כְּבַתְּחִלָּה in the ‖ clause † Isl. c.; כְּבַחֲצִי † [1 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:14) (apparently corrupt); כְּמִשְׁלשׁ חֳדָשִׁים † [Gn 38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24) (and prob. כְמֵחֹדֶשׁ [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27) LXX); כְּמִפְּנֵי † [Lv 26:37](https://ref.ly/Lev26:37); † כְּעַל (late): **a.** pleon. for כְּ (עַל \= *according to*), [Is 63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7) כְּעַל כָּל־אֲשֶׁר עָשָה, [59:18](https://ref.ly/Isa59:18), כְּעַל גְּמֻלוֺת יְשַׁלֵּם *the like of* their deeds is *the like of* (that which) he will repay (ellipse of rel., as [Jb 34:32](https://ref.ly/Job34:32), etc.); **b.** \= *as over* [Psalm 119:14](https://ref.ly/Ps119:14), \= *as concerning* [2 Ch 32:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:19). Where logically a prep. would seem to be required after כ, it is in Heb. regularly omitted (Ges§ 118\. 3 R), the nature of כְּ, as explained above, not in fact admitting it; thus [Is 1:25](https://ref.ly/Isa1:25) I will purge away thy dross כַּבֹּר \[not, as in Engl., *like as with* lye, but] (with) *the like of* lye (כ being an accus. defining the mode in which אצרף takes place); [5:17](https://ref.ly/Isa5:17) כְּדָבְרָם in Engl. as *in* their pasture, Ju [20:39](https://ref.ly/Judg20:39); כְּיוֺם as *in* day of, [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3), [Psalm 95:8](https://ref.ly/Ps95:8) \+, כִּימֵי [Jb 29:3](https://ref.ly/Job29:3) who will set me as *in* the days of old ! [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9) \+, כְּנֹקֶף זַיִת as *at*, etc., [17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6): so with לְ [Psalm 83:10](https://ref.ly/Ps83:10), [Jb 29:23](https://ref.ly/Job29:23), על [30:5](https://ref.ly/Job30:5). בְּ is used also pregnantly with substs., [Psalm 18:34](https://ref.ly/Ps18:34) who meketh my feet *like hinds* (for *like the feet of hinds*), [Is 29:4](https://ref.ly/Isa29:4), [63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2) בגדיך כדורך בגת, [Je 50:9](https://ref.ly/Jer50:9), [Jb 40:9](https://ref.ly/Job40:9) al. Cf. Ges§§ 118\. 6; 141\. 2 R. *n.* 

> \[Str834] כַּאֲשֶׁר conj. according as, as, when (cf. for the combin. Aram. כְּדִי, כַּד)—**1\.** *according to that which, according as, as*: **a.** [Gn 34:12](https://ref.ly/Gen34:12) I will give כאשׁר תאמרו אלי *according as* ye shall (*or* may) say unto me, [44:1](https://ref.ly/Gen44:1), [Ex 8:23](https://ref.ly/Exod8:23), [Nu 22:8](https://ref.ly/Num22:8), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16); [Gn 34:22](https://ref.ly/Gen34:22) if we are circumcised כאשׁר הם נמולים; [41:21](https://ref.ly/Gen41:21) כאשׁר בתחלה *as* at the beginning, so ´כ בראשׁונה [Jos 8:5](https://ref.ly/Josh8:5), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Josh8:6), [2 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:10); [Ex 5:13](https://ref.ly/Exod5:13) כאשׁר בהיות התבן: [Gn 7:9](https://ref.ly/Gen7:9) they came in two by two כאשׁר צוה אלהִים, *as* God commanded Noah; so, or similarly, very oft., esp. in P, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Gen7:16), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Gen8:21), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Gen12:4), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Gen17:23), [21:1](https://ref.ly/Gen21:1), [21:1](https://ref.ly/Gen21:1), [Ex 16:24](https://ref.ly/Exod16:24), [39:1](https://ref.ly/Exod39:1), [39:5](https://ref.ly/Exod39:5), [39:7](https://ref.ly/Exod39:7) [Nu 3:16](https://ref.ly/Num3:16), [3:42](https://ref.ly/Num3:42) etc.; ´כאשׁר דבר י [Dt 1:21](https://ref.ly/Deut1:21), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Deut2:1), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Deut6:3), [6:19](https://ref.ly/Deut6:19) \+ oft. Dt. **b.** answered, for increased emph., by כֵּן (cf. כְּ **2 d**), [Gn 50:12](https://ref.ly/Gen50:12) ויעשו כן כאשׁר צִוָּם, [Ex 7:10](https://ref.ly/Exod7:10), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Exod7:20); [Gn 18:5](https://ref.ly/Gen18:5) (J) כן חעשח כאשׁר דברת, [Ex 10:10](https://ref.ly/Exod10:10) (iron.), [Am 5:14](https://ref.ly/Amos5:14) (do.); in opp. order, Ju [1:7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7) כאשׁר עשיתי, כן שׁלם לי, [Ex 7:6](https://ref.ly/Exod7:6) כן עשו ´כאשׁר צוה י, cf. [12:28](https://ref.ly/Exod12:28), [12:50](https://ref.ly/Exod12:50), [39:43](https://ref.ly/Exod39:43), [Nu 5:4](https://ref.ly/Num5:4), [17:26](https://ref.ly/Num17:26), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Num36:10) (all P); with impf. (freq.) [Nu 2:17](https://ref.ly/Num2:17) (P) כאשׁר יחנו כן יסעו; of degree \= *the more… the more*, [Ex 1:12](https://ref.ly/Exod1:12) וכאשׁר יענּו אתו כן ירבה וכן יפרץ, cf. [17:11](https://ref.ly/Exod17:11) (JE) ´וגבר ישׁר…והיה כאשׁר ירים *according as* he held up, etc., Israel prevailed; in an oath or solemn promise, [Nu 14:28](https://ref.ly/Num14:28) אם לא כאשׁר דברתם כן אעשה, [Dt 28:63](https://ref.ly/Deut28:63) ([Je 31:28](https://ref.ly/Jer31:28)), [1 K 1:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:30), [Is 10:11](https://ref.ly/Isa10:11), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Isa14:24), [52:14](https://ref.ly/Isa52:14) f. (v. כֵּן **2 b**). **c.** answered by וַ (Dr§ 127 γ) † [Ex 16:34](https://ref.ly/Exod16:34), [Nu 1:19](https://ref.ly/Num1:19). **d.** oft. in *similes* (sq. impf. of habit) [Ex 33:11](https://ref.ly/Exod33:11) כאשׁר ידבר אישׁ אל רעהו, [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12), [Dt 1:44](https://ref.ly/Deut1:44), [Is 9:2](https://ref.ly/Isa9:2), [66:20](https://ref.ly/Isa66:20) \+, answered by כֵּן [Is 31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4), [55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10), [66:22](https://ref.ly/Isa66:22), [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12) \+; a second verb is, in such cases, in the pf. with וְ consec. (Dr§ 115\) [Dt 22:26](https://ref.ly/Deut22:26), [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8) והקיץ…כאשׁר יחלם, [65:8](https://ref.ly/Isa65:8), [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19). **e.** הָיָה כַּאֲשֶׁר (cf. הָיָה כְּ) to be *as if*, [Jb 10:19](https://ref.ly/Job10:19) כאשׁר לא הייתי אהיה, [Zc 10:6](https://ref.ly/Zech10:6) והיו כאשׁר לא זנחתים. **2\.** with a *casual* force, *in so far as, since* (Germ. *demgemäss dass*), [Gn 26:29](https://ref.ly/Gen26:29) if thou doest us no harm כאשׁר לא נגענוך *according as, in so far as*, we have not touched thee; [Nu 27:14](https://ref.ly/Num27:14) כאשׁר מריתם פי *inasmuch as* ye have defied my mouth, Ju [6:27](https://ref.ly/Judg6:27), [1 S 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:18) (answered by על כן), [2 K 17:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:26), [Mi 3:4](https://ref.ly/Mic3:4). **3\.** with a *temporal* force, *when*, [Gn 18:33](https://ref.ly/Gen18:33) and Y. went away כאשׁר כלה *when* he had finished, etc., [32:3](https://ref.ly/Gen32:3), [32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32), [1 S 8:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:6), [2 S 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:21) \+; answered by וַּ (Dr§ 127 β), 1 S [6:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:6), [12:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:8); …ויהי כאשׁר and it came to pass, when…[Gn 12:11](https://ref.ly/Gen12:11), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Gen20:13), [24:22](https://ref.ly/Gen24:22), [24:52](https://ref.ly/Gen24:52), [Ex 32:19](https://ref.ly/Exod32:19) \+ oft.; [Gn 43:14](https://ref.ly/Gen43:14) כאשׁר שׁכלתי שׁכלתי when I am bereaved, I am bereaved ! an expression of resignation, so [Est 4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16) כאשׁר אבדתי אבדתי. [Jos 2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7) אחרי כאשׁר is a ‘conflate’ reading, omit either אחרי or כ. Of future time, [Gn 27:40](https://ref.ly/Gen27:40), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) כאשׁר ייטב לך, [Ho 7:12](https://ref.ly/Hos7:12), [Ec 4:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:17), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:3), and without a verb [Is 23:5](https://ref.ly/Isa23:5) כאשׁר שׁמע למצרים.—[Mi 3:3](https://ref.ly/Mic3:3) כאשׁר is simply *as that which*, [Jb 29:25](https://ref.ly/Job29:25) *as one who*.

> \[Str3644] כְּמוֺ **adv.** and **conj.** like, as, when (lit. *the like of what* (viz.)…, a pleon. form of כְּ (v. מוֺ), and, like it, prop. a **subst.**: cf. Aram. כְּמָא *like* [Psalm 78:68](https://ref.ly/Ps78:68) Levy, usually כְּמָא דְּ *like as*; ܐܰܟ݂ܡܳܐ (i.e. ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܡܳܐ) *like what, like*, usu. sq. ܕ *like as*; Sam. ࠊࠌࠄ; Ar. كَمَا Eth. ከመ፡ *kama, like as*, DIGr:§ 63 a Sab. כמו:ZMG 1883, 394\)—used **1\.** (56 t.) as an indep. word (exc. [Gn 19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15), [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11), only in poetry); **2\.** as an equivalent for כְּ before sfs., always before light ones, and occasionally before heavy ones (v. כְּ *ad init.*):—**1\. a.** adv., as poet. syn. of כְּ, *like, as*, [Ex 15:5](https://ref.ly/Exod15:5) they went down כמו־אבן *like* a stone (hence in prose [Ne 9:11](https://ref.ly/Neh9:11)), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) ([Psalm 78:13](https://ref.ly/Ps78:13)), [Is 30:22](https://ref.ly/Isa30:22), [51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6) (v. כֵּן), [26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17) כְּמוֺ חָרָח תַּקְרִיב לָלֶרֶת (see כְּ **1 c** (2\), on the constr.: so [41:25](https://ref.ly/Isa41:25), [Psalm 58:5](https://ref.ly/Ps58:5), [58:8](https://ref.ly/Ps58:8) a, [58:9](https://ref.ly/Ps58:9)), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18) we have brought forth *the like of, as it were*, wind, [Je 13:21](https://ref.ly/Jer13:21), [15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18), [50:26](https://ref.ly/Jer50:26), Ez [16:57](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:57) (כמו עת as *at* the time of: v. כְּ *Note*), [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Hos8:12), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Hos13:7), Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14) (*as it were*), [Zc 9:15](https://ref.ly/Zech9:15), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Zech10:2), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Zech10:7) (as *with* wine: v. ib.) Elsewhere chiefly in PsalmPsalm (19 t.) and Jb (11 t.), as [Psalm 29:6](https://ref.ly/Ps29:6), [61:7](https://ref.ly/Ps61:7), [63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6), [78:69](https://ref.ly/Ps78:69), [79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5), [88:6](https://ref.ly/Ps88:6), [90:9](https://ref.ly/Ps90:9), [Jb 6:15](https://ref.ly/Job6:15), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Job10:22), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Job10:22), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3) כמו אלה *the like of these things*, [14:9](https://ref.ly/Job14:9), Ct [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2), [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6) כמו רגע (in prose כרגע). Repeated, like כְּ…כְּ, † [Psalm 58:10](https://ref.ly/Ps58:10); and answered by כֵּן [Is 26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17).—[Psalm 58:8](https://ref.ly/Ps58:8) b כמו יתמוללו, *as it were* is against usage of כמו (with a vb.), and yields a lame sense: read perh. ´ית \[חָצִיר] כמו (Lag Che); [Psalm 73:15](https://ref.ly/Ps73:15) if I said, אֲסַפְּרָה כְמָוֺ, the text is untranslateable, the rendering *thus* for כמו being indefensible: prob. הֵנָּה has dropped out before the following הִנֵּה: for כְּמוֺ הֵנָּה *the like of these things*, cf. [Jb 23:14](https://ref.ly/Job23:14) כָּהֵנָּה, and [12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3) supr. **b. conj.** \= כַּאֲשֶׁר: *(a)* of time, once, † [Gn 19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15) כְּמוֺ עָלָה הַשַּׁחַת *when* the dawn arose; (β) of mode, *according as*, † [Zc 10:8](https://ref.ly/Zech10:8) וְרָבוּ כְּמוֺ רָבוּ [Pr 23:7](https://ref.ly/Prov23:7) (answered by כֵּן).—For **2** (before sfs.), see עכּ.

## כָּאַב (id: BDB04309)

\[Str3510] † כָּאַב **vb.** be in pain (Aram. כְּאֵב, ܟܶܐܒ *id.;* Ar. كَيءِبَ *be sorrowful, sad;* As. in deriv., *ikkibu, pain* ZimBP 67, *kêbtu, ruin* Hpt in KAT2 Gloss. i.)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִכְאָ־ב [Pr 14:13](https://ref.ly/Prov14:13); יִכְאָב [Jb 14:22](https://ref.ly/Job14:22); *Pt.* כּוֺאֵב [Psalm 69:30](https://ref.ly/Ps69:30); כֹּאֲבִים [Gn 34:25](https://ref.ly/Gen34:25); —**1\.** *be in pain*, physical [Gn 34:25](https://ref.ly/Gen34:25) (J, as result of circumcision); [Jb 14:22](https://ref.ly/Job14:22) (subj. בָּשָר; poet. of body in grave ‖נַפְשׁוֺ תֶּאֱבָל). **2\.** of *mental pain* [Pr 14:13](https://ref.ly/Prov14:13) (subj. לֵב; prob. also [Psalm 69:30](https://ref.ly/Ps69:30) (‖עָנִי). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 s. sf. הִכְאַבְתִּיו Ez [13:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22); 2 fpl. הִכְאַבְתֶּן v:[22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22) LXX Targum Co (MT הַכְאוֺת v. כָּאָה); *Impf.* יַכְאִיב [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18); תַּכְאִ֫בוּ [2 K 3:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:19); *Pt.* מַכְאִב Ez [28:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:24); —*pain, mar;* **1\.** of enemies of Isr. under fig. of thorn causing pain (no object expr.) ´קוֺץ מַכ Ez [28:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:24) (‖מַמְאִיר סִלּוֺן); of שַׁדַּי, no obj. expr., [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18) (opp. חָבַשׁ, ‖מָחַץ. **2\.** of *mental pain*, obj. לֵב, Ez [13:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22) (subj. false prophetesses, v. supr.); cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22) (subj. ´י). **3\.** (si vera l.) of *marring* good land with stones [2 K 3:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:19), LXX άχρειώσετε (Klo תְּאַבְּדוּ).

> \[Str3511] † כְּאֵב **n.m.**:[Jb 2:13](https://ref.ly/Job2:13) pain;—´כ abs. [Jb 2:13](https://ref.ly/Job2:13), [Is 17:11](https://ref.ly/Isa17:11); cstr. [65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14); sf. כְּאֵבִי [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6) \+ 2 t.;—*pain*, mental and physical [Jb 2:13](https://ref.ly/Job2:13), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6) perh. also [Psalm 39:3](https://ref.ly/Ps39:3); אָנוּשׁ ´כּ (in disappointment and disaster) [Is 17:11](https://ref.ly/Isa17:11) (‖נַחֲלָה from חָלָה); mental, ־לֵב´כּ [Is 65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14) (‖שֵׁבֶר רוּחַ) so [Je 15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18) (‖מַכָּה fig.)

> \[Str4341] † מַכְאוֺב **n.m.**:[Psalm 32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10) pain;—´מ abs. [Ec 1:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:18) \+ 2 t.; cstr. [Psalm 69:27](https://ref.ly/Ps69:27); sf. מַכְאוֺבִי (מַכְאֹבִי) [Psalm 38:18](https://ref.ly/Ps38:18) \+ 3 t., etc.; pl. מַכְאוֺבִים [Psalm 32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10), [Ec 2:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:23); מַכְאֹבוֺת [Is 53:3](https://ref.ly/Isa53:3); sf. מַכְאֹבָיו [Ex 3:7](https://ref.ly/Exod3:7); מַכְאֹבֵינוּ [Is 53:4](https://ref.ly/Isa53:4); —**1\.** *pain*, physical, [Ex 3:7](https://ref.ly/Exod3:7) (‖עֳנִי; cf. מִפְּנֵי נֹגְשָיו in context); [2 Ch 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:29) (‖נֶגַע); [Jb 33:19](https://ref.ly/Job33:19). **2\.** of *mental pain*, [Psalm 32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10) (of troubles of wicked), of Babylon [Je 51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8); [Psalm 38:18](https://ref.ly/Ps38:18), [69:27](https://ref.ly/Ps69:27) (as result of sin; of ´י's servant); in ´י's word to Baruch [Je 45:3](https://ref.ly/Jer45:3) (‖יָגוֺן, אֲנָחָה), cf. of Israel in distress [Je 30:15](https://ref.ly/Jer30:15) (‖שֵׁבֶר), of Jerus. [La 1:12](https://ref.ly/Lam1:12), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Lam1:12), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Lam1:18), [Ec 1:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:18) (´יוֺסִיף דַּעַת יוֺסִיף מַכ); [2:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:23) (‖כַּעַם עִנְיָנוֺ); partic. of suffering servant of ´י [Is 53:3](https://ref.ly/Isa53:3), [53:4](https://ref.ly/Isa53:4) (both ‖חֳלִי).

## כָּאָה (id: BDB04312)

\[Str3512] † \[כָּאָה] **vb.** **Niph.** be disheartened, cowed (cf. Ar. كَأْكَأَ *draw back timidly*, كَآءَ *abstain, through timidity*)—**Niph.** *Pf.* וְנִכְאָה consec. [Dn 11:30](https://ref.ly/Dan11:30) *then shall he be cowed;* *Pt.* נִכְאֵה לֵבָב [Psalm 109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16) *downhearted* (‖עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֺן). **Hiph.** *Inf. cstr.* הַכְאוֺת לֵב צַדִּיק Ez [13:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22); but LXX Targum Co הכאבתן v. כָּאַב.

> \[Str2489] † \[כָּאֶה] **adj.** cowed, [Psalm 10:10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:10) Qr חֵל כָּאִים; *host of cowed ones*, but rd. Kt חלכאים; v. p. 319\.

> \[Str3738] \[כאר] v. II. כור

> \[Str2975] כָּאֹר [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8), v. יְאֹר. p. 384

## כבב (id: BDB04316)

כבב (√ of foll., cf. Sta§ 116:3; meaning not wholly clear; Ar. كَبَّ is *roll threads into a ball*, كَبَّ *ball* of thread, but also *troop* of horses, *band* of men, etc.; Eth. كُبَّةٌ is *in orbem circum\-sistere*, ከበበ፡ ከብኣብ፡ *orbis, circulus*, etc.; hence perh. *star*, as *round*, or as collected in *bands clusters, constellations*; As. *kabâbu* \= *burn*; *kabâbê shields*, is written *ḳabâbê* by DlHWB 578\).

> \[Str3556] † כּוֺכָב **n.m.**:[Gn 15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5) star (NH *id.*; Aram. כּוֺכְבָא, כּוֺכַבְתָּא ክብኣብ፡, ܟܰܘܟܒܳܐ, fem. form specifically of planet Venus, v. also WeSkizzen iii. 36, 38; Ar. ܟܰܘܟܰܒܬܳܐ gen. of *single stars* and prob. Aram. كَوْكَبٌ loan\-word, WeSkizzen iii. 173; Eth. ኮከብ፡; prob. for …כבכב v. Mahri *kubkob, kobkib*, etc., MaltzanZMG 1873, 227 and As. *kakkabu* DlHWB 336; Pun. κακαβουμ (Dioscorides), name of a plant, prob. from shape of seed\-cup, BlauZMG xxvii, 1873, 529; on formation cf. NöM § 109 BaNB § 138\. 1\)—´כ abs. [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17); cstr. כּוֺכַב [Am 5:26](https://ref.ly/Amos5:26); pl. כּוֺכָבִים [Gn 1:16](https://ref.ly/Gen1:16) \+ 19 t.; cstr. כּוֺכְבֵי (כֹּכְבֵי) [Gn 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17) \+ 13 t.; כֹּכְבֵיהֶם Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7); —sg. only אֱלֽהֵיכֶם ´כ [Am 5:26](https://ref.ly/Amos5:26) (where, however, ´כ is prob. a gloss, so GFM Dec. 19, 1890, and now We; v. further כִּיּוּן); and מִיַּעֲקֹב ´כ [Nu 24:12](https://ref.ly/Num24:12) (JE; ‖שֵׁבֶט בישראל, metaph. of future ruler); elsewhere pl. *stars*, ‖ sun and moon (cf. also infr.) [Gn 1:16](https://ref.ly/Gen1:16) (P) [Psalm 136:9](https://ref.ly/Ps136:9) (to rule the night, cf. [Gn 1:16](https://ref.ly/Gen1:16)), [Je 31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35), [Gn 37:9](https://ref.ly/Gen37:9) (E), Joseph's dream (´אַחַד עָשָר כ \= 11 brethren); [Dt 4:19](https://ref.ly/Deut4:19) obj. of idol. worship (cf. infr.); observed in augury ´הַחֹזִים בַּכּ [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13) (of Babylon); [Ec 12:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:2) (‖ (also) *light*; fig. of brightness of youth); ‖ sun [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7); ‖ moon [Psalm 8:4](https://ref.ly/Ps8:4); sign of evening [Ne 4:15](https://ref.ly/Neh4:15) (´מֵעֲלוֺת הַשַּׁחַר עַד צֵאת הַכּ) cf. כּוֺכְבֵי נִשְׁכּוֺ [Jb 3:9](https://ref.ly/Job3:9) (appar. sign of dawn); numbered by God [Psalm 147:4](https://ref.ly/Ps147:4); sim. of abundant posterity, [Gn 15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Gen26:4), [Ex 32:13](https://ref.ly/Exod32:13) (all JE); of Isr. [Dt 1:10](https://ref.ly/Deut1:10), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Deut10:22), [28:62](https://ref.ly/Deut28:62), [1 Ch 27:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:32), [Ne 9:23](https://ref.ly/Neh9:23); of number of merchants of Nineveh [Na 3:16](https://ref.ly/Nah3:16); as lofty, [Jb 22:12](https://ref.ly/Job22:12); so in fig. of haughty nations Ob:[4](https://ref.ly/Obad1:4), [Is 14:13](https://ref.ly/Isa14:13); cf. symbolic vision of Dn (‖צָבָא, צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם) [Dn 8:10](https://ref.ly/Dan8:10); sim. of brightness of righteous [Dn 12:3](https://ref.ly/Dan12:3); not pure (זַכּוּ) before God [Jb 25:5](https://ref.ly/Job25:5) (‖ moon); darkened in Yahweh's judgment [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/Joel2:10), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Joel3:15), Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) (‖ sun and moon; so) [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10) (‖ also כְּסִילֵיהֶם); personified: as fighting Ju [5:20](https://ref.ly/Judg5:20); shouting [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7) (בְּרָן־יַחַד כּוֺבֵי בֹקֶר, ‖בְּנֵי אֱלֽחִים); praising [Psalm 148:3](https://ref.ly/Ps148:3) (כּוֺכְבֵי אוֺר, ‖ sun and moon).—On stars in Arab. mythol. v. WeSkizzen iii. 173 f.

## כָּבֵד (id: BDB04318)

\[Str3513] † כָּבֵד **vb.** be heavy, weighty, burden\-some, honoured (NH Pi. *honour*, and deriv., כֹּבֶד *weight*; Ph. n.pr.f. כבדת \= *honoured one*, and in cpd. n.pr.; Eth. ከበደ፡ *be heavy*, etc.; As. *kabâdu* or *kabâtu, grow* or *be heavy*, DlHWB; cf. Ar. كَبَدٌ *difficulty, distress*; كَبَدَ III. *struggle with difficulties*, etc.; Zinj. כבדו *honour*, DHMSendsch. 58; Sab. כבודת *gifts of honour*, \= Heb. נדבה *freewill offering* DHMZMG 1883, 341 f.—**Qal** *Pf.* ´כ [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20); 3 fs. כָּֽבְדָה [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) \+ 4 t.; כָּבֵָדָה Ju [20:34](https://ref.ly/Judg20:34); 3 mpl. כָּֽבְדוּ [Gn 48:10](https://ref.ly/Gen48:10); *Impf.* יִכְבַּד [Ex 9:7](https://ref.ly/Exod9:7) [Is 66:5](https://ref.ly/Isa66:5); יִכְבָּד [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Job33:7) \+ 10 t. Impf. (כָּבֵד [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10) \+ is given as 3 m. Pf. by some; but it is prob. taken better as adj. SS make כָּבֵד [Psalm 38:5](https://ref.ly/Ps38:5) inf. abs.; \< Thes adj.).—**1\.** *be heavy*, in weight, of misfortune, heavier than sand [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3); of God's hand in punishment [1 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:11), with אֶל [1 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:6); עַל [Psalm 32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4); of man's hand in war Ju [1:35](https://ref.ly/Judg1:35); יָדִי כָּֽבְדָה עַל־אַנְחָתִי *my hand is heavy upon my groaning* [Jb 23:2](https://ref.ly/Job23:2) is explained by Targum Hrz Schlottm as *hand smiting me*; but read rather with LXX SyrVer Ew De Reu Di יָדוֺ of God's hand; of pressure אַכְפִּי [Jb 33:7](https://ref.ly/Job33:7) (LXX כַּפִּי); \= *vehement, sore*, of battle Ju [20:34](https://ref.ly/Judg20:34); c. אֶל [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3) \= עַל [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3); *burdensome, grievous*; וְלאֹ נִכְבַּד עָלֶ\+יְ *lest we be burdensome unto thee* [2 S 13:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:23); of work laid upon one, bondage, with עַל [Ex 5:9](https://ref.ly/Exod5:9) (JE) [Ne 5:18](https://ref.ly/Neh5:18); of sin conceived as a burden [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) (J), iniquities [Psalm 38:5](https://ref.ly/Ps38:5); transgressions, with עַל [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20). **2\.** *heavy, insensible, dull*, of the eyes, so as to be unable to see [Gn 48:10](https://ref.ly/Gen48:10) (JE); of the ears [Is 59:1](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1); of the לֵב, *hard, insensible* [Ex 9:7](https://ref.ly/Exod9:7) (J ; P and E's syn. is חָזַק, q. v.) **3\.** *be honoured* (cf. βαρύς) of a man [Jb 14:21](https://ref.ly/Job14:21) (opp. יצערו: cf. Hiph. **3** ), of Tyre Ez [27:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:25), of Yahweh [Is 66:5](https://ref.ly/Isa66:5).

 **Niph.** *Pf.* נִכְבַּד [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20), נִכְבָּ֔ד [2 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:19), [23:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:23), [1 Ch 11:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:21); נִכְבַּדְתָּ [Is 43:4](https://ref.ly/Isa43:4); \+ 2 t. Pf.; *Impf.* אֶכָּבֵד [Lv 10:3](https://ref.ly/Lev10:3), [Is 49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5); אִכָּֽבְדָה [Ex 14:4](https://ref.ly/Exod14:4), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Exod14:17); אִכָּבֵָדָה [2 S 6:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:22); אִכָּֽבְדָ֯ [Hg 1:8](https://ref.ly/Hag1:8); *Imv.* הִכָּבֵד [2 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:10); *Inf.* הִכָּֽבְדִי [Ex 14:18](https://ref.ly/Exod14:18), Ez [39:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:13); *Pt.* נִכְבָּד [Gn 34:19](https://ref.ly/Gen34:19) \+ 6 t.; pl. נִכְבָּדִים [Nu 22:15](https://ref.ly/Num22:15); cstr. נִכְבַּדֵּי [Is 23:8](https://ref.ly/Isa23:8), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Isa23:9), [Ps 8:24](https://ref.ly/Ps8:24); נִכְבַּדֶּיהָ [Na 3:10](https://ref.ly/Nah3:10); > \[Str3516] † כָּבֵד **n.m.**:[La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11) liver (explained by Thes (quoting Galen) as the *heavy* organ, *par excellence*, of the body; NH כָּבֵד; Aram. כַּבְדָא ܟܰܒܕܳܐ; Ar. كَبِدٌ, cf. أَكْبَدُ (anything) *large, thick* in the middle; Eth. ከብድ፡ As. *kabittu* DlHWB *temper, heart* (fig.), Aram. כְּבַד, ܐܶܬܟܰܒܰܕ *be angry*, because the liver was regarded as seat of emotions)—´כ [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13) \+ 11 t. ; כְּבֵדוֺ [Pr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Prov7:23); כְּבֵדִי [La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11); —*liver* of man, as pierced by arrow [Pr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Prov7:23); poured out in sorrow [La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11); of animals as consulted by divination ´רָאָה בַּכּ Ez [21:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:26); elsewhere in P, in phrases יֹתֶרֶת(ה) הַכָּבֵד (עַ־ל) [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Exod29:22), [Lv 3:4](https://ref.ly/Lev3:4), [3:10](https://ref.ly/Lev3:10), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Lev3:15), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Lev4:9), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Lev7:4), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Lev8:16), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Lev8:25), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Lev9:19); ´הַיֹּתֶרֶת מִן־הַכּ [Lv 9:10](https://ref.ly/Lev9:10), of the animals suited for sacrifice.

> \[Str3514] † כֹּ֫בֶד **n.\[m.]** **1\.** heaviness, *weight* of a stone [Pr 27:3](https://ref.ly/Prov27:3). **2\.** *mass, abundance*, of corpses [Na 3:3](https://ref.ly/Nah3:3). **3\.** *vehemence*, of war [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15), of storm [Is 30:27](https://ref.ly/Isa30:27).

(2\) *in historic and ideal* manifestations to the pious mind Yahweh's, name is a name of glory [Psalm 72:19](https://ref.ly/Ps72:19), [Ne 9:5](https://ref.ly/Neh9:5); his eyes eyes of glory [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8); in the temple his glory is seen [Psalm 63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3); it is על השׁמים [Psalm 113:4](https://ref.ly/Ps113:4); על כל הארץ [Psalm 57:6](https://ref.ly/Ps57:6), [57:12](https://ref.ly/Ps57:11); in a thunderstorm he is אֵל הַכָּבוֺד [Psalm 29:3](https://ref.ly/Ps29:3); his glory is לְעוֺלָם [Psalm 104:31](https://ref.ly/Ps104:31); it is great [Psalm 138:5](https://ref.ly/Ps138:5); above all the earth [Psalm 108:6](https://ref.ly/Ps108:6); the whole earth is full of it [Is 6:3](https://ref.ly/Isa6:3) the heavens are declaring כְּבוֺד אֵל [Psalm 19:2](https://ref.ly/Ps19:2); with reference to the divine reign הֲדַר מַלְכוּתוֺ ´כּ, [Psalm 145:12](https://ref.ly/Ps145:12); ´הֲדַר כ הוֺדְֶָ [Psalm 145:5](https://ref.ly/Ps145:5). (3\) he is מֶלֶח הַכָּבוֺד [Psalm 24:7](https://ref.ly/Ps24:7), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ps24:8), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Ps24:9), [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ps24:10), [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ps24:10); he will appear in his glory [Psalm 102:17](https://ref.ly/Ps102:17), his glory will be revealed in a march through the wilderness to the holy land [Is 40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), the land will see it [Is 35:2](https://ref.ly/Isa35:2), shine with it Ez [43:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:3), and it will dwell in the land [Psalm 85:10](https://ref.ly/Ps85:10); it will be to the rearward of Israel [Is 58:8](https://ref.ly/Isa58:8); it will arise and be seen upon Jerusalem [Is 60:1](https://ref.ly/Isa60:1), [60:2](https://ref.ly/Isa60:2); Yahweh will be the glory in the midst of her [Zc 2:9](https://ref.ly/Zech2:9); the temple will be filled with it [Hg 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hag2:7); the earth will be filled with a knowledge of it Hb [2:14](https://ref.ly/Hab2:14), and with it [Nu 14:21](https://ref.ly/Num14:21) (JE) [Psalm 72:19](https://ref.ly/Ps72:19); it will be declared among the nations and all will see it [Is 66:18](https://ref.ly/Isa66:18), [66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19), [66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19), [Psalm 97:6](https://ref.ly/Ps97:6) and peoples and kings revere it [Psalm 102:16](https://ref.ly/Ps102:16), [Is 59:19](https://ref.ly/Isa59:19); ´י will reign before his elders in glory [Is 24:23](https://ref.ly/Isa24:23); the resting\-place of the Messiah will be כָּבוֺד [Is 11:10](https://ref.ly/Isa11:10). **3\.** *honour, dignity* of position ´מנע מִכּ *withhold from honour* [Nu 24:11](https://ref.ly/Num24:11) (E); ´ירד מִכּ *descend from* [Je 48:18](https://ref.ly/Jer48:18); ´שׁלח אחר כ *send after* [Zc 2:12](https://ref.ly/Zech2:12); ´רוּם בכ [Psalm 112:9](https://ref.ly/Ps112:9); ´שׁכב בכ [Is 14:18](https://ref.ly/Isa14:18); ´תמך כ [Pr 29:23](https://ref.ly/Prov29:23); ´יַרְבֶּה כ [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39); ´לקח֔ כ *take me to honour* [Psalm 73:24](https://ref.ly/Ps73:24); not becoming to fools [Pr 26:1](https://ref.ly/Prov26:1); ´לפני כ ענוה *before honour* (goeth) *humility* [Pr 15:33](https://ref.ly/Prov15:33), [18:22](https://ref.ly/Prov18:22); antithesis קלון [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7), Hb [2:16](https://ref.ly/Hab2:16), [Pr 3:35](https://ref.ly/Prov3:35), קִיִקָלוֺן Hb [2:16](https://ref.ly/Hab2:16); כָּבוֺד is used as collective, of honoured men, *dignitaries, nobility* כבוד ישראל [Mi 1:15](https://ref.ly/Mic1:15); elsewhere as cstr. before various nations or with sfs. only [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Isa8:7), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Isa17:3), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Isa17:4), [21:16](https://ref.ly/Isa21:16), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Isa22:24). **4\.** *honour, reputation*, of character, of man ´וְלֹא־לְךָ לְכָבוֺד [2 Ch 26:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:18) *neither will it be for thine honour;* ‖חכמה [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1); צדקה [Pr 21:21](https://ref.ly/Prov21:21); antith. כְּלִמָּה [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3); וְחֵקֶר כְּבֹדָם כָּבוֺד [Pr 25:27](https://ref.ly/Prov25:27) *and searching out of their glory is* (not) *glory* לאישׁ ´כ [Pr 20:3](https://ref.ly/Prov20:3); אֱלֽהִים ´כ מְלָכִים חֲקֹר דָּבָר ´הַסְתֵּר דָּבָר וכ [Pr 25:2](https://ref.ly/Prov25:2) *the honour of God is to conceal a thing, but the honour of kings is to search out a thing.* **5\.** *my honour*, poet. of the seat of honour in the inner man, the noblest part of man ‖נפשׁי [Gn 49:6](https://ref.ly/Gen49:6) (poem) [Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6); לִבִּי [Psalm 16:9](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9), [108:2](https://ref.ly/Ps108:2); it is called upon to זמּר [Psalm 30:13](https://ref.ly/Ps30:13) (rd. כְּבוֺדִי for כָּבוֺד); עוּרָה [Psalm 57:9](https://ref.ly/Ps57:9). **6\.** *honour, reverence, glory*, as due to one or ascribed to one: **a.** of men, due to a father [Mal 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mal1:6); honour done to David by Nathan's prophecy [1 Ch 17:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:18); לְ ´עשה כ [2 Ch 32:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:33) *do honour to;* לְ ´נתן כ [Pr 26:8](https://ref.ly/Prov26:8); ‖ישׁע [Psalm 62:8](https://ref.ly/Ps62:8). **b.** of God, כְּבוֺדִי *the honour due to me* (Yahweh) [Is 42:8](https://ref.ly/Isa42:8), [43:7](https://ref.ly/Isa43:7), [48:11](https://ref.ly/Isa48:11) שְׁמְֶָ ´כ [Psalm 79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9); לְ ´נתן כ [1 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:5), [Je 13:16](https://ref.ly/Jer13:16), [Mal 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mal2:2), [Psalm 115:1](https://ref.ly/Ps115:1); לְ ´שים כ [Jos 7:19](https://ref.ly/Josh7:19) (J), [Is 42:12](https://ref.ly/Isa42:12); ´שים כ תהלתוֺ [Psalm 66:2](https://ref.ly/Ps66:2); ועז לְ ´יהב כ [Psalm 29:1](https://ref.ly/Ps29:1), [96:7](https://ref.ly/Ps96:7) \= [1 Ch 16:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:28); שְׁמוֺ לְ ´יהב כ [Psalm 29:2](https://ref.ly/Ps29:2), [96:8](https://ref.ly/Ps96:8) \= [1 Ch 16:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:29); שְׁמוֺ ´זמּר כ [Psalm 66:2](https://ref.ly/Ps66:2); ספּר כבודו בגוים [Psalm 96:3](https://ref.ly/Ps96:3) \= [1 Ch 16:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:24); אמר מלכותך ´כ [Psalm 145:11](https://ref.ly/Ps145:11); נתן כבודי בגוים Ez [39:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:21); אֹמֵר כָּבוֺד [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9) *say Glory;* ´יִעְלְזוּ בְּכ *exult with* (ascriptions of) *glory* [Psalm 149:5](https://ref.ly/Ps149:5). **7\.** *glory* as *the object* of honour, reverence and glorifying, כְּבוֺדִי [Psalm 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ps3:4) *my glory* (the one whom I glorify); כְּבוֺדָם [Psalm 106:20](https://ref.ly/Ps106:20) *their glory;* כְּבוֺדוֺ [Je 2:11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11).

## כָּבָה (id: BDB04326)

\[Str3518] † \[כָּבָה] **vb.** be quenched, extinguished, go out, of fire or lamp (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּבָא *id.* of light of eyes (rare))—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. כָּבוּ [Is 43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17); *Impf.* יִכְבֶּה [1 S 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:3), [Pr 21:18](https://ref.ly/Prov21:18); 3 fs. תִּכְבֶּה [Lv 6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5) \+ 10 t.; *Inf. cstr.* כְּבוֺתְ Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) LXX SyrVer Vulgate Co (MT ´כַּבּ Pi)—*be quenched, extinguished* (c. neg. exc. [1 S 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:3), [Pr 26:20](https://ref.ly/Prov26:20), [Is 43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17)), of lamp (נֵר אֱלֽהִים) in sanctuary [1 S 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:3); of altar\-fire [Lv 6:5](https://ref.ly/Lev6:5), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Lev6:6); of נֵרָהּ [Pr 31:18](https://ref.ly/Prov31:18); of bodies of renegade Israelites [Is 66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24) (cf. גֵּיא בֶן־הִנֹּם); subj. אֵשׁ fig. of contention [Pr 26:20](https://ref.ly/Prov26:20); fig., subj. fire kindled by ´י Ez [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:4); subj. wrath (חֵמָה) of ´י [2 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:17) \= [2 Ch 34:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:25), [Je 7:20](https://ref.ly/Jer7:20); wrath under fig. of fire (אֵשׁ) [Je 17:27](https://ref.ly/Jer17:27); so of burning land of Edom [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10); of annihilation of Yahweh's enemies [Is 43:17](https://ref.ly/Isa43:17) (כַּפִּשְׁתָּה; ‖דָּעַח), cf. Pharaoh Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) (v. supr.) **Pi.** *Pf.* וְכִבּוּ consec. [2 S 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:7); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְכַבֶּנָּה [Is 42:3](https://ref.ly/Isa42:3); 2 ms. תְּכַבֶּה [2 S 21:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:17); וַיְכַבּוּ [2 Ch 29:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:7); *Inf.* כַּבּוֺת Ct [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7); also MT כַּבּוֺתְ Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) (but cf. supr.); *Pt.* מְכַבֶּה [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) \+ 3 t.;—*quench, extinguish*, fig. except [2 Ch 29:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:7) (of extinguishing lamps, נֵרוֺת, in temple); sq. נֵר יִשְרָאֵל (fig. for life of David) [2 S 21:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:17); sq. גַּחַלְתִּי (*coal* \= family\-hope) [2 S 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:7) (of killing widow's only son); sq. פִּשְׁתָּה כֵהָה [Is 42:3](https://ref.ly/Isa42:3) *dimly\-burning* wick (fig. of spiritually weak); implied obj. ref. to wrath of ´י [Am 5:6](https://ref.ly/Amos5:6), [Je 4:4](https://ref.ly/Jer4:4), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Jer21:12); to people and idols [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31); sq. love אַהֲבָה Ct [8:7](https://ref.ly/Song8:7) (subj. מַיִם רַבִּים); Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) cf. supr.

> \[Str3521] † כָּבוּל **n.pr.loc. 1\.** city on border of Asher [Jos 19:27](https://ref.ly/Josh19:27), mod. *Kâbûl*, 4 h. (9 miles) SE. from Akko, RobBR iii, 88 GuêrinGal, i. 422 f. **2\.** אֶרֶץ כָּבוּל district in Galilee [1 K 9:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:13) containing 20 cities given by Sol. to Hiram (popular etymol. poss. כּ *as* \+ בֻּל \= בַּל *not*, i.e. *as good as nothing*, cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:12), so Ew Th; Klo conject. ארץ גָּלִיל), BuhlGeogr. § 116\.

Qal), only ´בֹּרִית מְכ [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2). † **2\.** *wash* person, only poet. and fig. אִם תְּכַבְּסִי בַּנֶּתֶר [Je 2:22](https://ref.ly/Jer2:22); כַּבְּסִי מֵרָעָה לִבֵח [4:14](https://ref.ly/Jer4:14); c. sf. [Psalm 51:4](https://ref.ly/Ps51:4) (‖טהר; \+ מֵעָוֺן); v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ps51:9) (‖חִטֵּא). † **Pu.** *Pf.* וְכֻבַּס consec. [Lv 13:58](https://ref.ly/Lev13:58), [15:17](https://ref.ly/Lev15:17) *be washed*, of garment. † **Hothp.** (Ges§ 54, 3\) *Inf.* אַחֲרֵי הֻכַּבֵּס אֶת־הַנֶּגַע [Lv 13:55](https://ref.ly/Lev13:55) *after the plague is washed out*, so v:[56](https://ref.ly/Lev13:56).

## I כָּבַר (id: BDB04335)

\[Str3527,4342] † I. \[כָּבַר] **vb.** be much, many (as. *kabâru, be great, mighty; kabru, great, huge;* Ar. كَبُرَ *be great*, in body, rank, or age, ܟܒܝܪܢ *great, noble, aged;* Eth. ከብረ፡ *be honoured, magnified;* ክብር፡ *honoured, glorious;* Sab. epith. כבר HalÉt. Sab. JAs. Dec. 1874, No. 90; Syr. ܟܒܰܪ, ܐܰܟܒܰܪ for Heb. רָבָה, הִרְבָּה, ܟܰܒܺܝܪ (rare) *much;* Zinj. *be abundant, numerous*)—**Hiph.** (only Elihu) *make many*, [Jb 35:16](https://ref.ly/Job35:16) בִּבְלִי דַעַת מִלִּין יַכְבִּר (cf. וְיֶרֶב אֲמָרָיו [34:37](https://ref.ly/Job34:37)) \> *make great*, BuBeitr. 138, comparing [8:2](https://ref.ly/Job8:2); מַכְבִּיר with the force of a subst. (Ew§ 160 c) [36:31](https://ref.ly/Job36:31) לְמַכְֶבִּיר יתן אכל giveth food *in abundance* (\= prose לָוֺב, e.g. [2 Ch 11:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:23)).

> \[Str3524] † כַּבִּיר **adj.** great, mighty, much only poet., and only Jb Is:1:—[Is 10:13](https://ref.ly/Isa10:13) Qr (but rd. as Kt כְּאַבִּיר), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Isa17:12) and [28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2) מים כבירים (usu. מַיִם רַבִּים), [Jb 8:2](https://ref.ly/Job8:2) ורוח כביר אמרי פיך a *mighty* wind, [15:10](https://ref.ly/Job15:10) כביר מאביך ימים (Ges§ 131, R 5a) \= *aged:* of God, [34:17](https://ref.ly/Job34:17) כַּבִּיר צַדִּיק the just *mighty* one, [36:5](https://ref.ly/Job36:5) a, vb כֹּחַ לֵב ´כ *mighty* in strength of understanding; [34:24](https://ref.ly/Job34:24) כבירים *mighty men;* of quantity \= *much*, [Is 16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14) שְׁאָר מְעַט מִזְעָר לוֺא כַבִּיר, [Jb 31:25](https://ref.ly/Job31:25) וכי כביר מצאה ידי had gotten *much.*

> \[Str3530] † \[כִּבְרָה] **n.f.** appar. distance, only in the phr. ארץ(ה)כִּבְרַת a *distance of land* or *length of way*, [Gn 35:16](https://ref.ly/Gen35:16) לָכוֺא אֶפְרָתָה ´ה ´וַיְהִי עוֺד כ, [48:7](https://ref.ly/Gen48:7) לָבאֹ אֶפְרָתָה ´א ´בְּעוֺד כ, [2 K 5:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:19) אָרֶץ ´וַיֵּלֶח מֵאִתּוֺ כ. The distance meant is uncertain, but fr. [2 K 5:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:19) it would seem to have been a short one; v. further Di:[Gn 35:16](https://ref.ly/Gen35:16) and Thes (As. *kibrâti* is a (widely extended) *territory, quarter of the world*, etc., DlHWB 315; according to Hoffm.:GGAbh. xxxvi (1890\), 23–25 perhaps the distance that one can see).

> \[Str3528] † I. כְּבָר **adv.** (late) already (freq. in Mish. and NH; Targum כבר (rare); Syr. ܟܒܰܪ usu. *perhaps*, occas. *already* ([Mt 11:21](https://ref.ly/Matt11:21) Hb 10:2\); Mand. כבאר *already*, NöM. p. 202\. Der. uncertain, connexion with √ כבר being dub.)—*already*, [Ec 1:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:10) כְּבָר הָיָה it hath *already* been, [2:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:12), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:16), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:15), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:15), [4:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:2), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:10), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:6), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:7).

> \[Str3529] † II. כְּבָר **n.pr.fl. Kĕ bār,** a river (or perhaps a canal) of Babylonia, not at present identified, by wh. the exiles, among whom Ez. ministered, were settled; always in the phr. נְהַר כְּבָר Ez [1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:1), [1:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:3), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:15), [3:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:23), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:15), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:20), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:22), [43:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:3) (cf. Sm: Ez [1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:1) DelPar. 47 f., 184\).

## II כבר (id: BDB04340)

II. כבר (√ of foll.; ? *to intertwine, net;* NH כָּבַר *sift* is denom. fr. כְּבָרָה).

> \[Str3523] † \[כָּבִיר] **n.\[m.]** prob. something *netted*, i.e. either a quilt or (EwH. 8\. 77 Ke) a **fly\-net** (κωνωπεῖον) spread over the face while a person was asleep; [1 S 19:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:13), [19:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:16) כְּבִיר הָעִזִּים a *quilt* (or *fly\-net*) of goats (’ hair). Vid. further Drad loc.

> \[Str3531] † כְּבָרָה **n.f.** a sieve (a *net*\-like implement), [Am 9:9](https://ref.ly/Amos9:9) כַּאֲשֶׁר יִנּוֺעַ בַּכְּבָרָה as it (corn) is shaken (i.e. sifted) in a *sieve.* Cf. WetzstZPV xiv. 1 ff.

> \[Str4345] † מַכְבֵּר **n.\[m.]** a netted cloth or coverlet; [2 K 8:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:15) of the cloth which Hazael dipped in water, and spread over Ben\-hand's face to smother him.

> \[Str4346] מִכְבָּר **n.m.** grating or lattice\-work, cstr. מִכְבַּר;—[Ex 27:4](https://ref.ly/Exod27:4) and thou shalt make for it (the altar of burnt\-offering in the tab.) מִכְבָּר מַעֲשֵה רֶשֶׁת נְהשֶׁת a *grating*, (even) a network of bronze, which surrounded the lower half of the altar (v:[5](https://ref.ly/Exod27:5) b): so [38:4](https://ref.ly/Exod38:4); מִכְבַּר הַנְּחשֶׁת [35:16](https://ref.ly/Exod35:16), [38:5](https://ref.ly/Exod38:5), [38:30](https://ref.ly/Exod38:30), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39).

## כָּבַשׁ (id: BDB04349)

\[Str3533] † \[כָּבַשׁ] **vb.** subdue, bring into bondage (NH *id., press, oppress;* Aram. כְּבַשׁ, ܟܒܰܫ *tread down, beat* or *make a path, subdue;* Ar. كَبَسَ *press, squeeze, knead* (body or limb, as in the bath, *message*), also *attack, assault*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְכָֽבְשׁוּ consec. [Zc 9:15](https://ref.ly/Zech9:15); *Impf.* יִכְבּוֺשׁ [Mi 7:19](https://ref.ly/Mic7:19); וַיִּכְבְּשׁוּם [Je 34:11](https://ref.ly/Jer34:11) Qr (Kt Hiph), etc.; *Imv.* pl. sf. כִּבְשֻׁהָ [Gn 1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28); *Inf.* כְּבוֺשׁ [2 Ch 28:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:10), [Est 7:8](https://ref.ly/Esth7:8); *Pt.* כֹּבְשִׁים [Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5); —**1\.** *bring into bondage*, sq. acc. \+ לַעֲבָדִים [Je 34:11](https://ref.ly/Jer34:11), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Jer34:16), [2 Ch 28:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:10), [Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5). **2\.** (late) *subdue, force*, a woman [Est 7:8](https://ref.ly/Esth7:8) (\= earlier עִנָּה).

 **3\.** *subdue, dominate*, the earth [Gn 1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28) (P), [Zc 9:15](https://ref.ly/Zech9:15) (of conquest); *tread down*, sq. אַבְנֵי־קֶלַע; fig. *subdue*, sq. עֲוֺנֹתֵינוּ [Mi 7:19](https://ref.ly/Mic7:19). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִכְבְּשָׁה [Jos 18:1](https://ref.ly/Josh18:1) \+ 3 t.; *Pt.* fpl. נִכְבָּשׁוֺת [Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5); —pass. of **Qal 1,** subj. בְּנוֺתֵינוּ [Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5); of **Qal 3,** subj. הָאָרֶץ (of conquest) [Nu 32:22](https://ref.ly/Num32:22), [32:29](https://ref.ly/Num32:29), [Jos 18:1](https://ref.ly/Josh18:1) (all P), [1 Ch 22:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:18). **Pi.** *Pf.* כִּבֵּשׁ [2 S 8:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:11), obj. גּוֺיִם (of conquest). **Hiph.** *Impf. bring into bondage*, sq. acc. \+ לַעֲבָדִים [Je 34:11](https://ref.ly/Jer34:11) Kt (Qr **Qal**). 

> \[Str3534] † כֶּ֫בֶשׁ **n.\[m.]** footstool (late) (NH *id., step, stair;* Aram. כִּבְשָׁא, etc., a rude *seat* (rare))—הַזָּהָב ´כ [2 Ch 9:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:18) (of Solomon's throne).

> \[Str3536] † כִּבְשָׁן **n.m.** kiln, for lime or pottery (so Ki, AW; MishnKel viii. 9; √ and original mng. dub.; acc. to Thes as *subduing, reducing* its contents, cf. *igne ferrum domatur* PlinNat. Hist. 36\. 27\)—alw. abs.; only in ´קִיטוֺר הַכּ [Gn 19:28](https://ref.ly/Gen19:28) (J; sim. of smoke of כִּכָּר, Sodom and Gom.); ´עֶשֶׁן הַכּ [Ex 19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18) (E; sim. of smoke of Sinai); ´פִּיחַ כ ashes of kiln [Ex 9:8](https://ref.ly/Exod9:8), cf. v:[10](https://ref.ly/Exod9:10) (both P).

* **Associated Passages:** Zechariah 9:15; Nehemiah 5:5; Exodus 19:18; Jeremiah 34:11; 2 Chronicles 9:18; Genesis 1:28; Joshua 18:1; 2 Chronicles 28:10; Numbers 32:22; Esther 7:8; Exodus 9:8; Genesis 19:28; Exodus 9:10; Micah 7:19; Numbers 32:29; 2 Samuel 8:11; 1 Chronicles 22:18; Jeremiah 34:16


## כדד (id: BDB04352)

כדד (√ of foll.; Ar. كَدَّ is *toil severely;* كَدَّ *fatigue, weary, harass, tread;* كَدٌّ *mortar* in which things are *pounded, bruised;* كُدَادَةٌ *what remains in bottom of cooking\-pot*).

> \[Str3537] † כַּד **n.f.** jar (NH *id.;* Aram. כַּדָּא)—כַּד abs. [1 K 17:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:12), [Ec 12:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:6); cstr. [1 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16); כַּדֵּח [Gn 24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), [24:43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43) etc.; pl. כַּדִּים Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16) \+ 3 t.;—water\-*jar*, carried on woman's shoulder [Gn 24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Gen24:15), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Gen24:16), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), [24:18](https://ref.ly/Gen24:18), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Gen24:20), [24:43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), [24:45](https://ref.ly/Gen24:45), [24:46](https://ref.ly/Gen24:46) (all J), [1 K 18:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:34), [Ec 12:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:6); empty (of water), containing lamps Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Judg7:19), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20); containing meal [1 K 17:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:12), [17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16). (On extra\-Palest. hist. of this word cf. LagBN 104\.)

> \[Str3590] † \[כִּידוֺד] **n.m.** spark (fr. above √, \= *strike out* a spark, acc. to Thes RobGes, cf. Sta§ 216 (doubtfully), LagBN 182 Köii. 147, § 71\. 1, NH כִּדּוֺד \= *rod* (of iron, etc.); כִּדּוֺדִיּוֺתְ שֶׁל אֵשׁ i.e. *red\-hot rods;* but BaNB § 142, 139 der. this and כַּדְכִּד fr. √ כיד \= Ar. كيد, كَادَ *emit fire* (said of a fire\-stick, زَنْدٌ, Lane2639 b, cf. 1257 b), and so Buhl)—only pl. cstr. כִּידוֺדֵי אֵשׁ [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11) of breath of crocodile (‖לַפִּידִים).

> \[Str3539] † כַּדְכֹּד **n.\[m].** a precious stone, perh. ruby (fr. *sparkle* ?; Aram. ‖ is כַּדְכְּדָנָא); ´כ Ez [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16) as article of commerce; כַּדְכַֹד [Is 54:12](https://ref.ly/Isa54:12) fig. in promise to Zion.

> \[Str1767] כְּדֵי v. דַּי

## כדר (id: BDB04357)

כדר (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. كَدَرَ VII. *shoot* or *rush down* (of hawk, star, etc.; also of an attacking force) so Thes Fl in De:[Job 15:24](https://ref.ly/Job15:24) Buhl Di al.; v. also mod. Ar. كدّر *annoy, vex, reprimand*, SpiroVocab.; كدر oftener *be dark, gloomy, turbid*, whence \> כידור \= *seething* tumult, of battle, cf. Köii, 147, § 71, 1\).

> \[Str3593] † כִּידוֺר **n.\[m.]** onset, ´מֶלֶח עָתִיד לַכּ [Jb 15:24](https://ref.ly/Job15:24) *a king ready for the onset.*

> \[Str1754] † כַּדּוּר **n.\[m.]** ball, acc. to BöNÄ ii, 134 De Or al. [Is 22:18](https://ref.ly/Isa22:18), as in Talm.; circle, cordon (As. *kudûru*), acc. to JastrPAOS, Oct. 1888, xcvi; v. also דּוּר p. 189 b.

> \[Str3540] † כְּדָרְלָעֹמֶר **n.pr.m.** king of Elam (\= Babylon. *Kudar\-Laga*\[*mar*] (*Lagamaru* \= n.pr.div.), v. PinchesVict. Inst. Jan. 20, 1896 SayPSBA, June 1896, 176 ScheilRev. Bibl. 1896, 600 f., cf. SchrCOT)—[Gn 14:1](https://ref.ly/Gen14:1), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Gen14:4), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Gen14:5), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Gen14:9), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Gen14:17).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 14:17; Isaiah 22:18; Genesis 14:9; Genesis 14:1; Genesis 14:4; Genesis 14:5; Job 15:24


## כֹּה (id: BDB04361)

\[Str3541] כֹּה **dmonstr. adv.** thus, here (BA כָּה; Targum SyrVerJerus. Mand. כָּא *here:* in Syr. with prefixes ܠܟܳܐ *hither*, ܡܶܟܳܐ (SyrVerJerus. ܡܶܢ ܟܳܐ) *hence*, ܐܰܝܟܳܐ *where?* cf. אֵיכָה, p. 32, and Köii. 1, 252\)—**1\.** of manner, *thus* (so most freq., and usually pointing to what is to follow, while כֵּן points commonly to what has preceded), [Gn 15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5) כה יהיה זרעך *thus* shall thy seed be, [Nu 6:23](https://ref.ly/Num6:23), [1 K 2:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:30) כֹּה דִבֶּר יוֺאָב וְכֹה עָנָנִי, [5:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:11), [Is 24:13](https://ref.ly/Isa24:13) (answered by בְּ, as [Je 23:29](https://ref.ly/Jer23:29)); with a subst. [Is 20:6](https://ref.ly/Isa20:6) הִנֵּה כֹה מַבָּטֵנוּ, [Je 9:21](https://ref.ly/Jer9:21) (si vera l.), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Jer23:29), [1 S 27:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:11). Mostly confined to particular phrases: thus **a.** with diff. forms of אמר, כה תאמר *thus* shalt thou say [Ex 3:14](https://ref.ly/Exod3:14), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Exod3:15), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Exod19:3), [20:22](https://ref.ly/Exod20:22) etc.; esp. י“כה אמר י thus saith (or said) ´י [Ex 4:22](https://ref.ly/Exod4:22), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Exod5:1), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [7:26](https://ref.ly/Exod7:26), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Exod8:16) etc.; and continually in the prophets, as [Is 7:7](https://ref.ly/Isa7:7), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Isa8:11), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Isa18:4) etc.; [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Jer2:5), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Jer4:27), [6:16](https://ref.ly/Jer6:16) etc.; also in the idiom. formula אִם כֹּה יאֹמַר *if he say* (or *used to say*) (the words spoken following) † [Gn 31:8](https://ref.ly/Gen31:8), [1 S 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:9), [14:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:10), [20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7), [2 S 15:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:26). **b.** with עשה to do *thus*, [Ex 5:15](https://ref.ly/Exod5:15), [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5); pointing back, [Nu 22:30](https://ref.ly/Num22:30), [Jos 6:14](https://ref.ly/Josh6:14), [1 S 27:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:11) al.; esp. in the phrase כה יעשה אלהים וכה יוסיף (with variations), [1 S 3:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:17) \+ (v. יסף), in which כֹּה points to an imprecation understood but not expressed. With בְּ prefixed † [1 K 22:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:20) ויאמר זה בְּכֹה וזה אמר בְּכֹה and one spake *in this wise*, and another spake *in that wise* (‖ [2 Ch 18:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:19) כָּכָה…כָכָה; prob. rightly). † **2\.** of place, *here* (rare, chiefly in E), [Gn 31:37](https://ref.ly/Gen31:37) שִים כֹּה place it *here* before my brethren, [Nu 23:15](https://ref.ly/Num23:15), [23:15](https://ref.ly/Num23:15), [2 S 18:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:30), [Ru 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8). Repeated, [Ex 2:12](https://ref.ly/Exod2:12) וַיִּפֶן כֹּה וָכֹה and he turned *this way* and *that way;* כֹּה…כֹּה *on this side…on that side* [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31). With עַד [Gn 22:5](https://ref.ly/Gen22:5) and we will go עַד־כֹּה *as far as here* (δεικτικῶς) i.e. *yonder.* † **3\.** of time, עַד כֹּה *hitherto*, [Ex 7:16](https://ref.ly/Exod7:16) (cf. עַד־הֵנָּה), [Jos 17:14](https://ref.ly/Josh17:14); עַד־כֹּה וְעַד־כֹּה till *now* and till *then*, i.e. meanwhile, [1 K 18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45).

> \[Str3602] כָּ֫כָה:37 **adv.** thus (NH abbrev. to כָּח, with preps. בֵּין כָּח *meanwhile*, לְפִיכָח *therefore;* prob. either from כָה and כְּ, lit. *as thus*, Ol:§ 223 h; or from כָּה כָה Köii. 1, 253\), somewhat more emph. than כֹּה, usu. prefixed to word which it qualifies:—[Ex 12:11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11) וככה תאכלו אתו and *thus* shall ye eat it, [29:35](https://ref.ly/Exod29:35), [Nu 8:26](https://ref.ly/Num8:26), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Num11:15) ואם ככה את עשה לי, [15:11](https://ref.ly/Num15:11), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Num15:12), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Num15:13), [Dt 25:9](https://ref.ly/Deut25:9), [29:23](https://ref.ly/Deut29:23) (cf. [1 K 9:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:8), [Je 22:8](https://ref.ly/Jer22:8)); so often with עשה: [1 S 19:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:17) למה ככה רמיתני why hast thou *thus* deceived me ? [2 S 17:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:21), [1 K 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:6), [1:48](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:48), [Je 13:9](https://ref.ly/Jer13:9), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Jer19:11), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Jer28:11), [51:64](https://ref.ly/Jer51:64); once with an adj. [2 S 13:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:4) מדוע אתה כָכָה דל why art thou *thus* lean ? [Ec 11:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:5) answering to כַּאֲשֶׁר; [Psalm 144:15](https://ref.ly/Ps144:15) העם שֶׁכָּכָה לוֺ the people to which it is *thus.* Once [2 Ch 18:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:19) repeated, *in this way…in that way* (‖ [1 K 22:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:20) בְּכֹה…בְּכֹה). Comp. the syn. כָּזאֹת. With a prep. once, in late Hebrew, [Est 9:26](https://ref.ly/Esth9:26) עַל־כָּ֫כָה concerning such a matter. With the interrog. אי, אֵיכָכָה *how* ? (p. 32\).

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 15:13; Exodus 3:14; Exodus 5:1; Jeremiah 19:11; 2 Samuel 17:21; 1 Kings 5:11; 2 Samuel 18:30; Jeremiah 13:9; 2 Samuel 13:4; Jeremiah 6:16; 2 Chronicles 18:19; Exodus 3:15; 2 Samuel 15:26; Exodus 2:12; Psalms 144:15; Exodus 8:16; Isaiah 7:7; Jeremiah 23:29; Jeremiah 51:64; Genesis 31:37; Exodus 19:3; Numbers 22:30; 1 Samuel 27:11; 1 Kings 9:8; Exodus 12:11; Exodus 7:17; Jeremiah 2:5; Genesis 22:5; Isaiah 24:13; Numbers 6:23; 1 Kings 18:45; Jeremiah 2:2; Deuteronomy 7:5; Numbers 11:31; 1 Kings 1:6; 1 Samuel 20:7; Exodus 5:15; 1 Kings 22:20; Numbers 15:11; Joshua 17:14; Deuteronomy 25:9; 1 Samuel 14:9; Genesis 15:5; Jeremiah 22:8; 1 Samuel 14:10; Exodus 20:22; Isaiah 18:4; 1 Samuel 19:17; 1 Samuel 3:17; 1 Kings 1:48; Exodus 29:35; Isaiah 8:11; Joshua 6:14; Exodus 7:16; Ruth 2:8; 1 Kings 2:30; Jeremiah 4:27; Deuteronomy 29:23; Genesis 31:8; Jeremiah 9:21; Jeremiah 28:11; Esther 9:26; Ecclesiastes 11:5; Isaiah 20:6; Numbers 8:26; Numbers 23:15; Exodus 4:22; Exodus 7:26; Numbers 15:12; Numbers 11:15

## I כָּהָה (id: BDB04363)

\[Str3543] † I. \[כָּהָה] **vb.** be or grow dim, faint (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּהָא; cf. Ar. كَهِىَ *be* أَكْهَى, i.e. *weak, cowardly*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. כָּֽהֲתָה [Dt 34:7](https://ref.ly/Deut34:7); *Impf.* יִכְהֶה [Is 42:4](https://ref.ly/Isa42:4); 3 fs. תִּכְהֶה [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); וַתֵּ֫כַהּ [Jb 17:7](https://ref.ly/Job17:7); וַתִּכְהֶיןָ [Gn 27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1); *Inf. abs.* כָּהֹה [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); —*grow dim*, of eye [Gn 27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1) (J), [Dt 34:7](https://ref.ly/Deut34:7), [Jb 17:7](https://ref.ly/Job17:7); of violent putting out of eye [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17); *he shall not grow dim* [Is 42:4](https://ref.ly/Isa42:4), of servant of ´י under fig. of lamp or fire (v. Kay Che De), or *grow faint* (cf. Di). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְכִהֲתָה consec. Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12) (subj. כָּל־רוּחַ; ‖וְנָמֵס כָּל־לֵב) *grow faint* (Pi intrans., Ges§ 52 *k*, Köi. 187; but rd. perh. כָּֽהֲתָה).

> \[Str3544] † \[כֵּהֶה] **adj.** dim, dull, faint; only f. כֵּהָה [Lv 13:6](https://ref.ly/Lev13:6) \+ 6 t.; pl. כֵּהוֺת [Lv 13:39](https://ref.ly/Lev13:39), [1 S 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:2); —*dim*, of eyes [1 S 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:2); of burning wick (´כִּשְׁתָּה כ) [Is 42:3](https://ref.ly/Isa42:3); *dull* (in colour, of plague\-spots) [Lv 13:6](https://ref.ly/Lev13:6), [13:21](https://ref.ly/Lev13:21), [13:26](https://ref.ly/Lev13:26), [13:39](https://ref.ly/Lev13:39), [13:56](https://ref.ly/Lev13:56); fig. \= *faint* ´רוּחַ כ [Is 61:3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3) (opp. מַעֲטֵה תְהִלָּה).

> \[Str3545] † כֵּהָה **n.f.** (dimming), lessenging, alleviation;—לְשִׁבְרְ ´אֵין כ of Nineveh [Na 3:19](https://ref.ly/Nah3:19) (read גֵּהָה, *healing* ?).

## כהן (id: BDB04367)

כהן (√ of foll.; mng. dub., v. Dr:[2 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:18); Ar. vb. كَهَنَ is *divine*, and كَاهِنٌ (Qor 52:29\) is *a seer*, the organ (mostly) of a jinn, rarely of a god: the كَاهِنٌ and the כֹּהֵן must have been orig. identical (both alike being guardians of an oracle, at a sanctuary); but their functions diverged: the كَاهِنٌ gradually lost his connexion with the sanctuary, and sank to be a mere diviner; the כהן acquired fuller sacrif. functions: v. RSEnc. Brit. ed. 9, xix. 727; WeSkizzen iii. 130 ff., 167; NowArch. ii. 89 f.; Ph. כהן is *priest*, כהנת *priestess*).

> \[Str3548] כֹּהֵן:750 **n.m.** priest (NH כֹּהֵן; Aram. כָּהֵין, כַּהֲנָא, ܟܳܗܶܢ, ܟܳܗܢܳܐ; Eth. ክኣህን፡ all *id.;* on Ph. and Ar. v. supr.);—´כ [Gn 14:18](https://ref.ly/Gen14:18) \+ 439 t.; pl. כֹּהֲנִים [Ex 19:6](https://ref.ly/Exod19:6) \+ 272 t.; cstr. כֹּהֲנֵי [1 S 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:5) \+ 13 t.; sf. כֹּהֲנַי [La 1:19](https://ref.ly/Lam1:19) \+ 22 t. sfs.—† **1\.** *priest\-king:* e.g. Melchizedek [Gn 14:18](https://ref.ly/Gen14:18) (E ?), cf. [Psalm 110:4](https://ref.ly/Ps110:4) (the Messianic priest\-king like Melchizedek); [Zc 6:13](https://ref.ly/Zech6:13) (Messianic priest and king); Israel מַמְלֶכֶת כֹּהֲנִים [Ex 19:6](https://ref.ly/Exod19:6) (E) *a kingdom of priests* (priests and kings at once in their relation to the nations); cf. [Is 61:6](https://ref.ly/Isa61:6) (of Israel ministering as a priest); or *a chieftain* (exercising priestly functions) כֹּהֵן מִדְיָן [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Exod3:1), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Exod18:1) (all JE); so also probably the sons of David [2 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:18), his grandson [1 K 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:5), and Ira the Jairite [2 S 20:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:26), who as princes performed priestly functions. The כהנים of the other cities of Judah, הבמות ´כ, who were not allowed to minister in Jerusalem [2 K 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8), [23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8), [23:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:9), [23:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:20), may be an attempt to enforce an exclusion from the priesthood of all but Levitical priests. † **6\.** *Zadokite priests:* Ezekiel distinguishes among הכהנים הלוים those of the seed of Zadok, בְּנֵי צָדוֺק; The adj. הַגָּדוֺל first appears of Jehoiada [2 K 12:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:11), then of Hilkiah [2 K 22:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:4), [22:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:8), [23:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:4), [2 Ch 34:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:9); after the exile, of Joshua [Hg 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hag1:1), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Hag1:12), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hag2:2), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hag2:4), [Zc 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zech3:1), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Zech3:8), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Zech6:11), and Eliashib [Ne 3:1](https://ref.ly/Neh3:1), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Neh3:20), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Neh13:28). (We also supposes הרואה in [2 S 15:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:27) to be a corruption of הראשׁ, which he regards as postexilic insertion. The test is corrupt \[see DrSm. l. c.]; but this correction is improbable. Lists of high priests occur [1 Ch 5:30–41](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:30-1Chr5:41), [6:35–38](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:35-1Chr6:38).) ## כָּוָה (id: BDB04373)

\[Str3554] † \[כָּוָה] **vb.** burn, scorch, brand (NH *id.;* Aram. ܟܘܳܐ esp. Pa.; Ar. كَوَى *cauterize*)—**Niph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּכָּוֶה [Is 43:2](https://ref.ly/Isa43:2) thou shalt not *be scorched* (fig. of Isr., ‖בָּעַר); 3 fpl. וְרַגְלָיו לאֹ תִּכָּוֶ\+ינָה [Pr 6:28](https://ref.ly/Prov6:28) shall a man walk on the coals *and his feet not be burned* ? (‖שָרַף of garments v:[27](https://ref.ly/Prov6:27)).

> \[Str3587,3588] † כִּי **n.\[m.]** burning, branding (for form, cf. רִי from רָוָה, אִי, חִי, עִי: Koii. 1\. 64\)—only כִּי תַּחַת \+יֹפִי [Is 3:24](https://ref.ly/Isa3:24) *branding instead of beauty*, in judgment on women of Jerusalem. p. 471

> \[Str3555] † כְּוִיָּה **n.f.** id.; ´תַּחַת כ ´כ [Ex 21:25](https://ref.ly/Exod21:25).

> \[Str4348] † מִכְוָה **n.f.** burnt\-spot, scar of a burn; מִכְוַת־אֵשׁ [Lv 13:24](https://ref.ly/Lev13:24); מִכְוָה v:[24](https://ref.ly/Lev13:24), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Lev13:25), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28) (all P).

> \[Str3556] כּוֺכָב v. sub כבב. p. 456

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 3:24; Isaiah 43:2; Proverbs 6:27; Leviticus 13:24; Leviticus 13:28; Exodus 21:25; Leviticus 13:25; Proverbs 6:28


## כּוּל (id: BDB04378)

\[Str3557] † \[כּוּל] **vb.** comprehend, contain (NH, Aram. *id., measure, measure out*, of dry or liquid measure; Syr. Aph. ܐܰܟܻܝܠ *id.;* Ar. كَالَ *measure* grain)—**Qal** *Pf.* only [Is 40:12](https://ref.ly/Isa40:12) כָּל בְּשָׁלִשׁ…מִי עֲפַר הָאָרֶץ *who hath comprehended the dust of the earth in a shalish\-measure*? **Pilp.** *Pf.* כִּלְכֵּל [2 S 19:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:33); sf. כִּלְכְּלָם [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4); 2 ms. sf. כִּלְכַּלְתָּם [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21), etc.; *Impf.* יְכַלְכֵּל [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) \+, etc.; *Inf. cstr.* כַּלְכֵּל [Je 20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9) \+, etc.; *Pt.* מְכַלְכֵּל [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2); —*sustain, maintain, contain:* **1\.** *sustain, support, nourish*, sq. acc. Pers. [Gn 45:11](https://ref.ly/Gen45:11), [50:21](https://ref.ly/Gen50:21) (both E), [2 S 19:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:33), [19:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:34), [20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3), [1 K 4:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:7) a (vb abs.), [5:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:7), [17:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:4), [17:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:9), [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21), [Ru 4:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:15), [Psalm 55:23](https://ref.ly/Ps55:23), [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) (of sheep); sq. acc. Pers. \+ rei (food) [Gn 47:12](https://ref.ly/Gen47:12) (J), [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4), [18:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:13). **2\.** *contain*, sq. acc. Pers. [1 K 8:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:27), [2 Ch 2:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:5), [6:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:18) (heavens cannot contain ´י); *hold in, restrain* Yahweh's word within one, [Je 20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9). **3\.** *support, endure*, sickness רוּחַ אִישׁ מַחֲלֵָהוּ ´יְכַל [Pr 18:14](https://ref.ly/Prov18:14), [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2) (obj. אֶת־יוֺם בּוֺאוֺ); of *sustaining* a cause, in court, דְּבָרָיו בְּמִשְׁפָּט ´יְכַל [Psalm 112:5](https://ref.ly/Ps112:5). **Polp.** *Pf.* 3 pl. כָּלְכְּלוּ *were supplied with food* [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27) (Klo prop. בַּגִּלְבֹּעַ). **Hiph.** *Impf.* יָכִיל [1 K 7:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:26) \+ 2 t. etc.; *Inf. cstr.* הָכִיל [Je 6:11](https://ref.ly/Jer6:11) \+ 5 t.;—*contain, hold, hold in, endure;* **1\.** *contain*, sq. acc. (of liquid) [1 K 7:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:26), [7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38), [2 Ch 4:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:5); in fig. [Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13), abs. Ez [23:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:32); (of burnt\-offering) [1 K 8:64](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:64), [2 Ch 7:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:7); *hold in* (wrath of ´י, obj. not expr., subj. Jeremiah) [Je 6:11](https://ref.ly/Jer6:11) (opp. שָׁפַח). **2\.** *sustain, endure*, sq. acc. [Am 7:10](https://ref.ly/Amos7:10), [Je 10:10](https://ref.ly/Jer10:10), [Jo 2:11](https://ref.ly/Joel2:11).

> \[Str3633] † כַּלְכֹּל **n.pr.m.** one of the wise men whom Solomon surpassed [1 K 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:11), [1 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:6) (on format. v. BaNB 206\).

## כום (id: BDB04380)

כום (√ of foll.; Ar. كَامَ II. is *heap up, accumulate;* كُومَةٌ *heap;* كُومٌ *herd* of camels, hence perh. כִּימָה as a *group, herd*, or *flock* of stars, but uncertain (v. כִּימָה)).

> \[Str3598] † כִּימָה **n.f.** perh. **Pleiades** (v. supr.; NH *id.;* Targum כִּימָא, SerVer ܟܻܝܡܳܐ)—as made by ´י, [Am 5:8](https://ref.ly/Amos5:8) (‖כְּסִיל), [Jb 9:9](https://ref.ly/Job9:9) (‖עָשׁ, כְּסִיל, חַדְרֵי תֵמָן); and under his control, ´מַעֲדַנּוֺת כ [Jb 38:31](https://ref.ly/Job38:31) (‖משְׁכוֺת כְּסִיל).—(SternJüd. Zeitschr. f. Wissen. u. Leben. iii. (1864–65\) 258 ff. Nö in SchenkelBL HoffmZAW iii. 107 ff. interpret עשׁ as Pleiades, and כימה as Sirius).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 38:31; Amos 5:8; Job 9:9

pl. נְכֹנִים [Ex 19:11](https://ref.ly/Exod19:11) \+ 2 t.; pass. or neut., and (less oft.) reflex.:—**1\.** *be set up, established, fixed:* **a.** lit., of house upon (עַל) pillars Ju [16:26](https://ref.ly/Judg16:26), [16:29](https://ref.ly/Judg16:29), so, in metaph., of temple\-mt., sq. בְּראֹשׁ [Is 2:2](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2) \= [Mi 4:1](https://ref.ly/Mic4:1); [Psalm 33:14](https://ref.ly/Ps33:14); מְכוֺנִי alone [Is 18:4](https://ref.ly/Isa18:4); fig. צֶדֶק וּמִשְׁפָּט מְכוֺן כִּסְאְֶָ [Psalm 89:15](https://ref.ly/Ps89:15), so [97:2](https://ref.ly/Ps97:2). **2\.** *foundation,* only pl., poet. יָסַד אֶרֶץ עַל־מְכוֺנֶיהָ [Psalm 104:5](https://ref.ly/Ps104:5).

> \[Str4350,4369] מְכוֺנָה, מְכֹנָה:25 **n.f.** fixed resting\-place, base;—abs. ´מ [1 K 7:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:27) \+ 5 t.; מְכֹנָה [1 K 7:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:34), [7:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:34), [7:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:35); sf. מְכֻנָתָתּ [Zc 5:11](https://ref.ly/Zech5:11) (Ges§ 27, 3\. R. 1\); pl. מְכוֺנוֺת [Je 27:19](https://ref.ly/Jer27:19), מְכֹנֹת [1 K 7:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:27) \+ 12 t.; sf. מְכוֺנֹתָ֯ו [Ezr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:3); —*base* or *stand* of laver כִּיּוֺד [1 K 7:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:27), [7:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:27) \+ 13 t. 1 K 7, 2 K [16:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:17), [25:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:13), [25:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:16) \= [Je 52:17](https://ref.ly/Jer52:17), [52:20](https://ref.ly/Jer52:20), [Je 27:19](https://ref.ly/Jer27:19), [2 Ch 4:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:14), [4:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:14); of altar [Ezr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:3); of the symbol. ephah [Zc 5:11](https://ref.ly/Zech5:11).

> \[Str5225] † נָכוֺן **n.pr.m.** only in ´גֹּרֶן נ [2 S 6:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:6); LXX Νωδαβ, A Ναχων, LXXL Ορνα τοῦ Ἰεβουσαίου; \= כִּידֹן [1 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:9).

> \[Str8498–99,4349] † תְּכוּנָה **n.f.** arrangement, preparation, fixed place;—**1\.** *arrangement, disposition* תְּכוּנָתוֺ Ez [43:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:11), i.e. the arrangement of the house (del. Co with LXX codd.) **2\.** *preparation,* וְאֵין קֶצֶה לַתְּכוּנָה [Na 2:10](https://ref.ly/Nah2:10) *and no end to the preparation* (i.e. things prepared, supply, store). **3\.** *fixed place,* i.e. dwelling\-place, of God (\= מָכוֺן) תְּכוּנָתוֺ [Jb 23:3](https://ref.ly/Job23:3).

> \[Str3561] † \[כַּוָּן] **n.\[m.]** cake, sacrificial cake (? √ כון; cf. Hiph. **2\. a.** *prepare* food, etc.)—only pl. abs. לַעֲשוֺת כַּוָּנִים לִמְלֶכֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18); עָשִינוּ לְהַעֲצִבָהַ ´לָהּ כ [44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19).

> \[Str3563] † I. כּוֺס **n.f.**:[La 4:21](https://ref.ly/Lam4:21) cup (√ unknown; perh. kindred with כִּיס, q.v.; NH *id.;* Ph. כסת; Aram. כָּס, ܟܳܣ; Ar. كَأْسٌ is Aram. loan\-wd. acc. to Frä171, but see DHMVOJ i. 27\)—´כ abs.
