[Gn 40:11](https://ref.ly/Gen40:11) \+; sf. כּוֺסִי [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5) etc.; pl. abs. כֹּסוֺת [Je 35:5](https://ref.ly/Jer35:5); —*cup* [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3); for wine [Gn 40:11](https://ref.ly/Gen40:11), [40:11](https://ref.ly/Gen40:11), [40:11](https://ref.ly/Gen40:11), [40:13](https://ref.ly/Gen40:13), [40:21](https://ref.ly/Gen40:21) (all E), [Pr 23:31](https://ref.ly/Prov23:31), [Je 35:5](https://ref.ly/Jer35:5); שְפַתאכּוֺס [1 K 7:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:26), [2 Ch 4:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:5); תַּנְחוּמִים ´כ [Je 16:7](https://ref.ly/Jer16:7) the *cup* offered to mourners; fig. (wine\-) *cup* of judgement of ´י, from which the nations, or Isr., must drink [Je 49:12](https://ref.ly/Jer49:12), [La 4:21](https://ref.ly/Lam4:21), [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9); חֲמָתוֺ ´כ [Is 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Isa51:22); הַיַּיִן ´כ הַחֵמָה [Je 25:15](https://ref.ly/Jer25:15), cf. v:[17](https://ref.ly/Jer25:17), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Jer25:28); הַתַּרְעֵלָה ´כ [Is 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Isa51:22); ´כ שַׁמָּה וּשְׁמָמָה Ez [23:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:33), cf. v:[31](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:31), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:32), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:33); ´יְמִין י ´כ Hb [2:16](https://ref.ly/Hab2:16); by bold metaph. Babylon is such a cup זָהָב ´כ ´בְּיַד י [Je 51:7](https://ref.ly/Jer51:7); so of wicked persons, fire, brimstone, and burning wind are מְנָתאכּוֺסָם [Psalm 11:6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:6) *the portion of their cup;* ´כ in good sense, *cup* of blessing [Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5) (overflowing); מְנָת חֶלְקִי וְכוֺסִי ´י [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5); יְשׁוּעוֺת ´כ [Psalm 116:13](https://ref.ly/Ps116:13).

> \[Str3563] † II. כּוֺס **n.\[m.]** a kind of owl (acc. to Vrss.; v. Kn\-Di [Lv 11:17](https://ref.ly/Lev11:17); wd. perh. onomatop.)—unclean bird [Lv 11:17](https://ref.ly/Lev11:17), [Dt 14:16](https://ref.ly/Deut14:16), dwelling in ruins חֳרָבוֺת ´כִ [Psalm 102:7](https://ref.ly/Ps102:7).

## I כור (id: BDB04396)

I. כור (√ of foll.; perh. *be* or *make round,* hence *furnace, pot, basin,* fr. shape; Ar. كَارَ *wind about* (e.g. a turban), كَوْرٌ *turn* or *twist* (of turban), كُورٌ *blacksmith's fire\-place, hornet's* or *bees' nest,* كَارَةٌ *bundle;* \> Thes comp. Syr. ܐܶܬܟܰܘܰܪ *incaluit* (Lexx. *aestuavit, calefactus est*)).

468:a. I. כור, 1\. 4: كُور means also a camel’s saddle (or ‘howdah’) with its apparatus, v. Frey Lane.

> \[Str3733,3733] † I. \[כָּר] **n.\[m.]** basket\-saddle, בְּכַר־הַגָּמָל [Gn 31:34](https://ref.ly/Gen31:34) *in the camel\-basket,* i.e. the basket\-saddle of the camel, a sort of palankeen bound upon the saddle proper (cf. Kn in Di and reff.) II. כַּר v. in alphabetical order. p. 499
> 
> —I. \[כָּר]: for *basket\-saddle* read \< *camelpalankeen;* the palankeen, or tent\-like erection, on the saddle (BurckhBedoulns and Wahábys, 266 DoughtyArab. Des. i. 437\. ii. 304 LaneMod. Egyptians (5\) ii. 158 al.), which seems intended here, is distinct from the basket or pannier (in which some women also traveled, PorterTravels ii. 232\.).

> \[Str3564] † כּוּר **n.\[m.]** smelting\-pot or furnace (Ar. كُور; NH *id.;* Aram. *id.,* ܟܽܘܪ; Eth. ከውር፡)—*pot* or *furnace* for smelting metals, but alw. in metaph. or sim. of human sufferings in punishment or discipline: iron (fig. of Egypt as place of bondage) הַבַּרְזֶל ´מִכּ [Dt 4:20](https://ref.ly/Deut4:20), [Je 11:4](https://ref.ly/Jer11:4), ´הַבּ ´מִתּוֺח כ [1 K 8:51](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:51); gold לַזָּהָב ´כ [Pr 17:3](https://ref.ly/Prov17:3) \= [27:21](https://ref.ly/Prov27:21) (‖מַצְרֵף); various metals Ez [22:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:18), [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:22); once בְּכוּר עָֹנִי [Is 48:10](https://ref.ly/Isa48:10) *in a smelting\-pot of affliction.*

> \[Str3565] כּוֺר־עָשָׁן **n.pr.loc.** v. בּוֺר עַשָׁן supr. p. 92:b.

> \[Str3600] † \[כִּיר] **n.\[m.]** cooking\-furnace (Mishn. כִּירָה)—frangible, only du. כִּירַיִם [Lv 11:35](https://ref.ly/Lev11:35) (c. תַּנּזּר), perh. as supporting two pots (Ki); LXX χυτρὀποδες.

> \[Str3595] † כִּיֹּר, כִּיּוֺר **n.m.**:[1 K 7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38) pot, basin (NH כִּיּוֺר *wash\-basin,* so Aram. כִּיּוֺרָא)—abs. כִּיֹּר [1 K 7:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:30) \+ 8 t.; כִּיּוֺר [1 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:14) \+ 5 t.; cstr. כִּיּוֺר [Ex 30:18](https://ref.ly/Exod30:18) \+ 2 t.; pl. abs. כִּיֹּרוֺת [1 K 7:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:40) (but v. infr.), [2 Ch 4:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:14); כְּיֹּרֹת [1 K 7:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:43); כִּיּוֺרִים [2 Ch 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:6); cstr. כְּיֹּרוֺת [1 K 7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38); —**1\.** *pot* for cooking [1 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:14) (‖דּוּד, קַלַּחַת, פָּרוּר). **2\.** *fire\-pot* כִּיּוֺר אֵשׁ [Zc 12:6](https://ref.ly/Zech12:6) (‖לַפִּיד אֵשׁ; fig. of chiefs of Judah). **3\.** *basin* of bronze for washing, *laver;* set on a bronze support **a.** before tabernacle [Ex 30:18](https://ref.ly/Exod30:18), [30:28](https://ref.ly/Exod30:28), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Exod31:9), [35:16](https://ref.ly/Exod35:16), [38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39), [40:7](https://ref.ly/Exod40:7), [40:11](https://ref.ly/Exod40:11), [40:30](https://ref.ly/Exod40:30), [Lv 8:11](https://ref.ly/Lev8:11) (all P). **b.** 10 in number, 5 at each front corner of temple [1 K 7:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:30), [7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38), [7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38), [7:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:38), [7:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:43) \= [2 Ch 4:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:14), [2 Ch 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:6) ([1 K 7:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:40) rd. הַסִּירוֺת as v:[45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45) and ‖ [2 Ch 4:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:11), [4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16), so Heb. Codd. LXX Vulgate Th Ke Sta Klo al.) **4\.** *plat form* or *stage* of bronze (prob. round, bowl\-like in shape) on which, acc. to Chr, Solomon stood and kneeled [2 Ch 6:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:13).

## II כוּר (id: BDB04402)

\[Str3738] † II. \[כוּר] **vb.** mng. dub., perh. *bore,* or *dig,* or *hew* (DlProl. 121 comp. As. *kâru, fell* trees (DlHWB 324\))—only **Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. כארו \= כָּארוּ for כָּרוּ [Psalm 22:17](https://ref.ly/Ps22:17) (MT כָּאֲרִי, v. אֲרִי) *they have bored* (*digged, hewn*) my hands and my feet (si vera l.; cf. Vrss De Pe Che Bae al.; some, however, deriving from כרה in this sense); hence perhaps following.

> \[Str4351] † \[מְכֹרָה, מְכוּרָה] **n.f.** origin (i.e. *place of digging* out?)—sg. sf. אֶרֶץ מְכוּרָתָם Ez [29:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:14); of a people, persons: pl. sf. בְּאֶרֶץ מְכֻרוֺתַיִךְ [21:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:30) (‖בִּמְקוֺם אֲשֶׁר נִבְרֵאת); of Jerus., מְכֹדֹתַיִךְ וּמֹלְדֹתַיִךְ מֵאֶרֶץ הַכְּנַעֲנִי [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3).

> \[Str4380] † \[מְכֵרָה] **n.\[f.]** prob. the name of a weapon, only pl. sf. כְּלֵי חָמָס מְכֵרֹתֵיהֶם [Gn 49:5](https://ref.ly/Gen49:5) *weapons of violence are their* ´מ (other conjectures v. in Di).

> \[Str3566] † כּ֫וֺרֶשׁ and ([Ezr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:1), [1:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:2)) כֹּ֫רֶשׁ **n.pr.m.** cyrus (Pers. *K’ur’u(š)*, SpiegAPK 215; Bab. *Kuraš* COT on [Ezr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:1), Dl in BaerDn. p. x)—king of (Anzan \= Susiana, TieleBab.\-Assyr. ## I כּוּשׁ (id: BDB04406)

\[Str3568] † I. כּוּשׁ **n. pr. Pers. m., gent.** et **terr.** (Egypt. *Kōš,* SteindorffBAS i, 593, As. *Kûsu,* Idib. DlPa 251; *Kaši* in Tel Amarna tablets, vid. Wkl Tel Am. 39\*);—**1\.** 1st ‘son’ of חָם [Gn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Gen10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) (P) \= [1 Ch 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:8), [1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9), LXX Χους, Vulgate *Chus,* from whom descended acc. to these vv. the southernmost peoples known to Hebrews. **2\.** land and people of southern Nile\-valley, or Upper Egypt, extending from Syene (Ez [29:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:10)) indefinitely to the south, LXX Αιθιοπια, Αιθιοπες: **a.** the land [Is 11:11](https://ref.ly/Isa11:11), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Isa18:1), [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10), Ez [29:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:10), [Jb 28:19](https://ref.ly/Job28:19), [Est 1:1](https://ref.ly/Esth1:1), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Esth8:9). **b.** the people [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4), [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9), Ez [38:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:5); personif. [Psalm 68:32](https://ref.ly/Ps68:32). **c.** indeterminate, either land or people, or including both: [Is 20:3](https://ref.ly/Isa20:3), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), [43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Isa45:14), [2 K 19:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:9) \= [Is 37:9](https://ref.ly/Isa37:9), [Na 3:9](https://ref.ly/Nah3:9), Ez [30:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:4), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:5), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9), [Psalm 87:4](https://ref.ly/Ps87:4) (LXX λαὸς Αιθιοπων). **3\.** in [Gn 10:8](https://ref.ly/Gen10:8) (J) \= [1 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:10) כּוּשׁ is err. for כַּשׁ \= Bab. *Kašsšu,* acc. to SchrCOT on [Gn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Gen10:6), DlPa 51 ff. 72 f. and most Assyriol.; so perh. also [Gn 2:13](https://ref.ly/Gen2:13) (J), yet v. Hpt: Über Ld. u. Meer, 1894–5, No. 15\.

> \[Str3568] † II. כּוּשׁ **n.pr.m.** a Benjamite, [Psalm 7:1](https://ref.ly/Ps7:1) (title), LXX Ξουσει υἱοῦ Ιεμενει.

## כָּזַב (id: BDB04414)

\[Str3576] † \[כָּזַב] **vb.** lie, be a liar (Ar. كَذَبَ; Aram. כְּדַב, ܟܕܰܒ *id.* chiefly Pa.; NH כָּזַב)—**Qal** *Pt.* כָּל־הָאָדָם כֹּזֵב [Psalm 116:11](https://ref.ly/Ps116:11) (cf. כָּזָּב [62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10)). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. תֹּחַלְתּוֺ נִכְָ\+זבָה [Jb 41:1](https://ref.ly/Job41:1) *his hope has been made deceptive,* i.e. been shewn to be so; 2 ms. consec. וְנִכְָ\+זבְתָּ [Pr 30:6](https://ref.ly/Prov30:6) lest he convict thee *and thou be proven a liar.* **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. כִּזֵּב [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְכַזֵּב Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3), [Pr 14:5](https://ref.ly/Prov14:5); וִיכַזֵּב [Nu 23:19](https://ref.ly/Num23:19), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* sf. כַזֶּבְכֶם (בְּ) Ez [13:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:19); —**1\.** *lie, tell a lie,* abs., [Nu 23:19](https://ref.ly/Num23:19) (JE), [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11), [Jb 6:28](https://ref.ly/Job6:28), [34:6](https://ref.ly/Job34:6), [Pr 14:5](https://ref.ly/Prov14:5), [Is 57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11); sq. לְ Pers. *lie to* Ez [13:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:9), [Psalm 78:36](https://ref.ly/Ps78:36), [89:36](https://ref.ly/Ps89:36); sq. בְּ Pers. *tell a lie with,* i.e. in intercourse, conversation with [2 K 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:16). **2\.** *disappoint, fail,* of a divine revelation Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3); waters of a spring [Is 58:11](https://ref.ly/Isa58:11). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַכְזִיבֵנִי [Jb 24:25](https://ref.ly/Job24:25) who *will make me a liar*? i.e. prove me to be so (cf. **Niph.**)

sf. כִּזְבֵיהֶם [Am 2:4](https://ref.ly/Amos2:4); —*lie, falsehood,* c. דִּבֶּר Ju [16:10](https://ref.ly/Judg16:10), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Judg16:13), [Ho 7:13](https://ref.ly/Hos7:13), [Zp 3:13](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:13), [Dn 11:27](https://ref.ly/Dan11:27), [Psalm 5:7](https://ref.ly/Ps5:7), [58:4](https://ref.ly/Ps58:4), so Ez [13:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:8) Co (by transp. ´כ with שָׁוְא, cf. v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:6), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:9)); c. יָפִיחַ (in Pr) [Pr 6:19](https://ref.ly/Prov6:19), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Prov14:5), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Prov14:25), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Prov19:5), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Prov19:9); of false prophecies (divinations) c. אָמַר, ´מִקְסַם כ Ez [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7); c. קָסַם v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:9), [21:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:34), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:28) \+ [13:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:6) LXX Co (´קָסוֺם כ, for MT ´קֶסֶם כ); c. שָׁמַע [13:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:19), בִּקֵּשׁ [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3); c. רָצָה *delight in* [62:5](https://ref.ly/Ps62:5); so in the phr. ´דְּבַר־כ [Pr 30:8](https://ref.ly/Prov30:8), ´אִישׁ כ [19:22](https://ref.ly/Prov19:22) \= *liar,* עֵד כְּזָבִים [21:28](https://ref.ly/Prov21:28) \= *false witness;* of idols. as a *lie* (*res ementita*) [Am 2:4](https://ref.ly/Amos2:4), so prob. ´שָטֵי כ *those turning aside to a lie* [Psalm 40:5](https://ref.ly/Ps40:5) (cf. אָוֶן, אֱלִיל, הֶבֶל, שֶׁקֶר); then of empty human pretensions וָשֹׁד יַרְבֶּה ´כ [Ho 12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2), [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15) (‖שֶׁקֶר), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17) (´מַחְסֵה כ), [Psalm 62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10) (‖הֶבֶל; cf. כֹּזֵב [116:11](https://ref.ly/Ps116:11)); לֶחֶם כְּזָבִים [Pr 23:3](https://ref.ly/Prov23:3) \= *deceptive* (disappointing) *bread*.

## כזר (id: BDB04421)

כזר (√ of foll.; Aram. Ethpe. *be cruel* (Targum [Jb 10:1](https://ref.ly/Job10:1)), cf. Syr. ܟܙܻܝܪܳܝܳܐ, *miles, vir strenuus* PS1718, who comp. Pers. كَزير *athleta, heros* (Vullers)).

> \[Str393] † אַכְזָר **adj.** cruel, flerce, of poison (ראֹשׁ) [Dt 32:33](https://ref.ly/Deut32:33); elsewh. of men, *fierce* [Jb 41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2); as subst. \= *a cruel one* [Jb 30:21](https://ref.ly/Job30:21) (of God), [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3).

> \[Str394] † אַכְזָרִי **adj.** cruel, always in this form;—[Pr 12:10](https://ref.ly/Prov12:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Prov17:11), [Je 6:23](https://ref.ly/Jer6:23), [50:42](https://ref.ly/Jer50:42), also [30:14](https://ref.ly/Jer30:14) (rd. ´מוּסָר א, for MT מוּסַר, Gf Gie); as subst. [Pr 5:9](https://ref.ly/Prov5:9), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Prov11:17), perhaps also, in appos. with ´יוֺם י, [Is 13:9](https://ref.ly/Isa13:9).

> \[Str395] † אַכְזְרִיּוּת **n.f.** cruelty, fierceness, only חֵמָה וְשֶׁטֶף אָף ´א [Pr 27:4](https://ref.ly/Prov27:4) *fierceness of rage and outpouring of anger*.

> \[Str3581] † I. כֹּחַ **n.\[m.]** a small reptile, prob. a kind of lizard, in list of unclean creeping things [Lv 11:30](https://ref.ly/Lev11:30); LXX Vulgate AV *chameleon;* on various opinions v. Di.

## כָּחַד (id: BDB04427)

\[Str3582] † \[כָּחַד] **vb.** not in **Qal; Pi. Hiph.** hide, efface; **Niph.** (usu.) pass. (Aram. אתכחדו *be effaced* † [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7); ܟܚܶܕ is *revere,* Pt. *venerable,* Pa. *put to shame,* Ethpa. *be ashamed;* Eth. ክሕደ፡ *deny, apostatize*)—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִכְחַד [Ho 5:3](https://ref.ly/Hos5:3) \+ 2 t.; 3 pl. נִכְחָדוּ [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7), [Psalm 69:6](https://ref.ly/Ps69:6); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִכָּחֵד [2 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:13), etc.; *Pt.* נִכְחֶדֶת [Zc 11:9](https://ref.ly/Zech11:9); pl. נִכְחָדוֺת [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16), [Jb 15:28](https://ref.ly/Job15:28); —**1\.** *be hidden,* sq. מִן Pers., [2 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:13), [Ho 5:3](https://ref.ly/Hos5:3), [Psalm 69:6](https://ref.ly/Ps69:6), [139:15](https://ref.ly/Ps139:15). **2\.** *be effected, destroyed* [Zc 11:9](https://ref.ly/Zech11:9), [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7), [15:28](https://ref.ly/Job15:28) (עָרִים נִכְחְדוֺת), [22:20](https://ref.ly/Job22:20), sq. מִ־ן הָאָרֶץ [Ex 9:15](https://ref.ly/Exod9:15) (J); *Pt.* intrans. of incomplete process \= *going to ruin* [Zc 11:9](https://ref.ly/Zech11:9), [11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16). **Pi.** *Pf.* כִּחֵד [1 S 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:18); 1 S. כִּחַדְתִּי [Jb 6:10](https://ref.ly/Job6:10), [Psalm 40:11](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11); 3 pl. כִּחֲדוּ [Jb 15:18](https://ref.ly/Job15:18); כִּחֵָדוּ [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9); *Impf.* 2 ms. תְּכַחֵד [Jos 7:19](https://ref.ly/Josh7:19) \+ 4 t.; 2 fs. תְּכַחֲדִי [2 S 14:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:18); 1 S. אֲכַחֵד [Jb 27:11](https://ref.ly/Job27:11); 2 mpl. תְּכַחֵָדוּ [Je 50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2); 1 pl. נְכַחֵד [Gn 47:18](https://ref.ly/Gen47:18), [Psalm 78:4](https://ref.ly/Ps78:4); —*hide,* sq. מִן Pers. [Gn 47:18](https://ref.ly/Gen47:18) (J), [Jos 7:19](https://ref.ly/Josh7:19) (JE), [1 S 3:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:17), [3:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:17), [3:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:18), [2 S 14:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:18), [Je 38:14](https://ref.ly/Jer38:14), [38:25](https://ref.ly/Jer38:25), [Psalm 78:4](https://ref.ly/Ps78:4); sq. לְ Pers. [Psalm 40:11](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11) *conceal toward, with ref. to,* i.e. *from;* without prep. [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9), [Je 50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2), [Jb 15:18](https://ref.ly/Job15:18), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Job27:11); \= *disown* [Jb 6:10](https://ref.ly/Job6:10). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 S. consec. sf. וְהִכְחַדְתִּיו [Ex 23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיַּכְחֵד [2 Ch 32:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:21), sf. יַכְחִידֶנָּה [Jb 20:12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12); 1 S. וָאַכְחִד [Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8); 1 pl. sf. וְנַכְחִידֵם [Psalm 83:5](https://ref.ly/Ps83:5); *Inf. cstr.* לְהַכְחִיד [1 K 13:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:34); —**1\.** *hide,* in fig. יַכְחִידֶנָּה תַּחַת לְשֹׁנוֺ [Jb 20:12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12) (obj. wickedness). **2\.** *efface, annihilate* [Ex 23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23) (E), [1 K 13:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:34) (‖הַשְׁמִיד), [Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8) 2 Ch [32:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:21); sq. מִגּוֺי [Psalm 83:5](https://ref.ly/Ps83:5), i.e. *from being* (so that they be not) *a nation.*

* **Associated Passages:** Hosea 5:3; Job 15:18; Isaiah 3:9; Zechariah 11:9; Job 20:12; Zechariah 11:8; Job 4:7; Psalms 40:11; 1 Samuel 3:17; Job 22:20; Job 15:28; Psalms 78:4; Jeremiah 38:14; Genesis 47:18; Psalms 69:6; 2 Samuel 14:18; Jeremiah 38:25; 2 Chronicles 32:21; Zechariah 11:16; Job 27:11; 1 Samuel 3:18; 1 Kings 13:34; Joshua 7:19; Exodus 23:23; Psalms 83:5; Job 6:10; Jeremiah 50:2; Psalms 139:15; Exodus 9:15; 2 Samuel 18:13


## כחח (id: BDB04428)

כחח (√ assumed by Thes Buhl al. for foll. BaNB 79 prop. √ כוח).

> \[Str3581] † II. כֹּחַ and († [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6)) כּוֺחַ **n.m.**:[Dt 4:37](https://ref.ly/Deut4:37) strength, power (NH *id.*);—´כ abs. [Dt 8:18](https://ref.ly/Deut8:18) \+; cstr. [Nu 14:17](https://ref.ly/Num14:17) \+; sf. כֹּחִי [Gn 31:6](https://ref.ly/Gen31:6) \+; כֹּחְֲ Ju [16:6](https://ref.ly/Judg16:6) \+, כֹּחְֶָ [Pr 5:10](https://ref.ly/Prov5:10), כֹּחֶָכָה [Pr 24:10](https://ref.ly/Prov24:10), etc.; alw. sg.;—**1\.** *human strength:* **a.** physical, of Samson Ju [16:5](https://ref.ly/Judg16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Judg16:6), [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Judg16:15), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17), [16:19](https://ref.ly/Judg16:19), [16:30](https://ref.ly/Judg16:30); strength in toil [Is 44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12) (זְרוֺעַ כֹּחוֺ), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12), [Lv 26:20](https://ref.ly/Lev26:20), [Ne 4:4](https://ref.ly/Neh4:4); of manly vigour (in procreation) אַתָּה כֹּחִי וְרֵאשִׁית אוֺנִי [Gn 49:3](https://ref.ly/Gen49:3) (poem in J; said to Reuben); vigour in gen. [Jos 14:11](https://ref.ly/Josh14:11), [14:11](https://ref.ly/Josh14:11) (JE), Ju [6:14](https://ref.ly/Judg6:14), [1 S 28:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:22), [Jb 6:11](https://ref.ly/Job6:11), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Job6:12), [Psalm 31:11](https://ref.ly/Ps31:11), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Ps38:11), [71:9](https://ref.ly/Ps71:9), [102:24](https://ref.ly/Ps102:24), [Pr 20:29](https://ref.ly/Prov20:29); as sustained by food [1 K 19:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:8); lack of such vigour is לאֹ־כֹחַ [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2), cf. מִכֹּחַ [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45) *without strength;* ´בְּלאֹ־כ [La 1:6](https://ref.ly/Lam1:6); ´יְגִיעֵי־כ [Jb 3:17](https://ref.ly/Job3:17); כֹּחַ לאֹ־הָיָה בוֺ [1 S 28:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:20), cf. [Gn 31:6](https://ref.ly/Gen31:6), [Pr 24:5](https://ref.ly/Prov24:5), [24:10](https://ref.ly/Prov24:10), [Ec 4:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:1), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:10), so prob. כֹּחַ יְדֵיהֶם [Jb 30:2](https://ref.ly/Job30:2); עָצַר כֹּחַ (late, cf. עצר) *retain ability* or *strength,* sq. inf.; לְהִתְנַדֵּב ´נַעְצֹר כ [1 Ch 29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14) that *we should retain* (have) *ability to make freewill\-oferings;* to build [2 Ch 2:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:5); c. neg., without inf., [Dn 10:8](https://ref.ly/Dan10:8), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Dan10:16) (cf. [11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6), **c**). **c.** *power* of a people, or king, [Jos 17:17](https://ref.ly/Josh17:17), [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2), Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11), [Is 49:4](https://ref.ly/Isa49:4) (of servant of ´י), [La 1:14](https://ref.ly/Lam1:14), [Dn 8:22](https://ref.ly/Dan8:22), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Dan8:24) a, also v:[24](https://ref.ly/Dan8:24) b (si vera l., v. in [Ho 7:9](https://ref.ly/Hos7:9) fig. of body seems combined with that of land yielding produce (v. **5** infr.) **d.** *power* opp. to that of God [Am 2:14](https://ref.ly/Amos2:14), [Is 10:13](https://ref.ly/Isa10:13) (יָדִי ´כ), [Dt 8:17](https://ref.ly/Deut8:17), [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9), [Zc 4:6](https://ref.ly/Zech4:6), [Jb 36:19](https://ref.ly/Job36:19), [Psalm 33:16](https://ref.ly/Ps33:16). **e.** *power* conferred by God [Is 40:29](https://ref.ly/Isa40:29), cf. v:[31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Isa41:1); of prophetic power [Mi 3:8](https://ref.ly/Mic3:8). **2\.** *strength* of angels [Psalm 103:20](https://ref.ly/Ps103:20). **3\.** *power* of God: in creation [Je 10:12](https://ref.ly/Jer10:12), [51:15](https://ref.ly/Jer51:15) (see also [32:17](https://ref.ly/Jer32:17) infr.), [Psalm 65:7](https://ref.ly/Ps65:7); in governing the world [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12), [2 Ch 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:6), [Psalm 29:4](https://ref.ly/Ps29:4); in acts of deliverance and judgment [Ex 9:16](https://ref.ly/Exod9:16), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Exod15:6), [32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11), [Nu 14:13](https://ref.ly/Num14:13), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Num14:17) (JE), [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8), [Jb 9:19](https://ref.ly/Job9:19), [24:22](https://ref.ly/Job24:22), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Job26:12), [30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18), [36:22](https://ref.ly/Job36:22); in combin. כֹּחַ גָּדוֺל [Dt 4:37](https://ref.ly/Deut4:37); ‖יָד חֲזָקָה [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11)(JE), [Ne 1:10](https://ref.ly/Neh1:10); ‖זְרוֺעַ נְטוּיָה [Dt 9:29](https://ref.ly/Deut9:29), [2 K 17:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:36), [Je 27:5](https://ref.ly/Jer27:5), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Jer32:17); he is רַב־כֹּחַ [Psalm 147:5](https://ref.ly/Ps147:5); cf. רֹב כֹּחוֺ [Is 63:1](https://ref.ly/Isa63:1), [Jb 23:6](https://ref.ly/Job23:6); also גדול־כֹּחַ [Na 1:3](https://ref.ly/Nah1:3); אַמִּיץ כֹּחַ [Is 40:26](https://ref.ly/Isa40:26), [Jb 9:4](https://ref.ly/Job9:4), שַגִּיא־כֹחַ [37:23](https://ref.ly/Job37:23); כֹּחַ מַעֲשָיו [Psalm 111:6](https://ref.ly/Ps111:6) *the power of his works;* בַּכֹּחַ ´קוֺל י [Psalm 29:4](https://ref.ly/Ps29:4) (of thunder); specif. of his wisdom (rather peculiarly, Elihu) כַּבִּיר כֹּחַ לֵב [Jb 36:5](https://ref.ly/Job36:5) *mighty in strength of mind.* **4\.** of animals: wild ox [Jb 39:11](https://ref.ly/Job39:11), horse v:[21](https://ref.ly/Job39:21), hippopot. ## כָּחַל (id: BDB04430)

\[Str3583] † \[כָּחַל] **vb.** paint (eyes) (NH *id,;* Aram. כְּחַל, ܟܚܰܠ; Eth. ኰሐለ፡ Ar. كَحَلَ)—only in ´רָחַצְתְּ כָּחַלְתְּ עֵינַיִח וגו Ez [23:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:40) (Co rds. 3 pl.);—v. also פּוּח.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 23:40


## כָּחַשׁ (id: BDB04431)

\[Str3584] † כָּחַשׁ **vb.** be disappointing, deceive, fail, grow lean (NH כָּחַשׁ *grow lean;* Hiph. *convict of falsehood, refute;* Aram. כְּחַשׁ *grow lean;* כַּחְשָׁא *leanness*, כְּחִישׁוּתָא *leanness, weakness*; Aph. as NH Hiph.)—**Qal.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וּבְשָרִי כָּחַשׁ מִשָּׁמֶן [Psalm 109:24](https://ref.ly/Ps109:24) *my flesh hath grown lean* (away) *fr. fatness* (‖בִּרְכַּי כָּֽשְׁלוּ מִצּוֺם). **Niph.** *Impf.* וְיִכָּֽחֲשוּ אֹיְבֶיְ לָח [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29) *thine enemies shall cringe* (come cringing, i.e. orig. *act deceptively, feigning obedience*) *to thee* (cf. also **Pi. 3, Hithp.**) **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. כִּחֵשׁ [1 K 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:18), Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17); וְכִּ֫חֶשׁ consec. (bef. monosyll.) [Lv 5:22](https://ref.ly/Lev5:22), [Jb 8:18](https://ref.ly/Job8:18), etc.; *Impf.* (בָּהּ)יְכַ֫חֶשּׁ [Ho 9:2](https://ref.ly/Hos9:2); 3 fs. וַתְּכַחֵש [Gn 18:15](https://ref.ly/Gen18:15), etc.; *Inf. abs.* כַּחֵשׁ [Ho 4:2](https://ref.ly/Hos4:2), [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13); *cstr.* [Zc 13:4](https://ref.ly/Zech13:4); —**1\.** *device,* abs. [Gn 18:15](https://ref.ly/Gen18:15) (J), [Jos 7:11](https://ref.ly/Josh7:11) (JE), Ho [4:2](https://ref.ly/Hos4:2) (אלה וכחשׁ together \= *false swearing* acc. to WeKl. Pr.; \+ וְרָצֹחַ וְגָנֹב וְנָאָֹף), cf. לאֹ תִגְ\+נֹבוּ וְל־אֹ תְכַחֲשׁוּ וְלאֹ תְשַׁקְּרוּ אִישׁ בַּעֲמִיתוֺ [Lv 19:11](https://ref.ly/Lev19:11) (H); ´לְמַעַן כ [Zc 13:4](https://ref.ly/Zech13:4) *in order to deceive;* sq. לְ Pers. [1 K 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:18), [Jb 31:28](https://ref.ly/Job31:28); sq. בְ Pers. et rei [Lv 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2), בְ rei v:[22](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3). **2\.** *act deceptively* against (i.e. seem to acknowledge, but not really do so), sq. בְ Pers. [Jos 24:27](https://ref.ly/Josh24:27) (E), [Je 5:12](https://ref.ly/Jer5:12), [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13), [Jb 8:18](https://ref.ly/Job8:18), appar. also בָּהּ ´וְתִירוֺשׁ יכ [Ho 9:2](https://ref.ly/Hos9:2) *and new wine shall deny her* (Isr.; i.e. refuse to acknowledge her as its mistress, not yield itself to her); abs. [Psalm 66:3](https://ref.ly/Ps66:3), [81:16](https://ref.ly/Ps81:16). **4\.** *disappoint, fail,* abs. כִּחֵשׁ מַעֲשֵה זַיִת Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17) *the product of the olive hath failed* (in sense comp. [Ho 9:2](https://ref.ly/Hos9:2) supr.)

 **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְכַּחֲשׁוּ לִי [2 S 22:45](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:45) *shall come cringing to me* (‖ [Psalm 18:45](https://ref.ly/Ps18:45). v. supr.) 

> \[Str3585] † כַּ֫חַשׁ **n.m.**:[Jb 16:8](https://ref.ly/Job16:8) **1\. lying. 2\. leanness**;—abs. ´כ [Ho 12:1](https://ref.ly/Hos12:1) \+ 2 t.; כָּחַשׁ [Ho 10:13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13); sf. כַּחֲשִׁי [Jb 16:8](https://ref.ly/Job16:8); pl. sf. כַּחֲשֵׁיהֶם [Ho 7:3](https://ref.ly/Hos7:3); —**1\.** *lying,* אֲכַלְתֶּם ´פְּרִי כ [Ho 10:13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13) *ye have eaten the fruit of lying;* [7:3](https://ref.ly/Hos7:3) (‖רָעָה), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Hos12:1); of Nineveh, פֶּרֶק מְלֵאָה ´כ [Na 3:1](https://ref.ly/Nah3:1) *of lying* (and) *robbery* it is *full;* [Psalm 59:13](https://ref.ly/Ps59:13) (‖אָלָה). **2\.** of Job's affliction [Jb 16:8](https://ref.ly/Job16:8) *my leanness hath risen up against me* (cf. [Psalm 109:24](https://ref.ly/Ps109:24); Targum J [Gn 41:27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27), [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5); Talm. כחשׁא; \> *my lying*, i.e. my affliction regarded as a lying witness, Di Buhl al.)

> \[Str3586] † \[כֶּחָשׁ] **adj, deceptive, false** (on format. v. BaNB 50 Ges§ 84b)—בָּנִים כֶּחָשִׁים [Is 30:9](https://ref.ly/Isa30:9) (‖עַם מְרִי).

## כִּי (id: BDB04434)

\[Str3588,3588\+,518] כִּי **conj.** that, for, when (Moab. *id,:* Ph. כ. Prob. from the same demonstr. basis found in كَي *here,* and in certain pronouns, as Aram. דֵּךְ *this* (WSG 110 f.); perh. also ultim. akin with ܟܰܝ *that, in order that,* and ܕ *then,* enclit., like Lat. *nam* in *quisnam?*)—**1\.** *that* (ὅτι, Germ. *dass*): **a.** prefixed to sentences depending on an active verb, and occupying to it the place of an accus.: so constantly, after vbs. of seeing, as [Gn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Gen1:10) וַיַּרְא אֱלֽהִים כִּי טוֺב and God saw *that* it was good, [3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6), [6:2](https://ref.ly/Gen6:2), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Gen6:5), [12:14](https://ref.ly/Gen12:14) \+ oft., hearing [14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14), [29:33](https://ref.ly/Gen29:33), knowing [22:12](https://ref.ly/Gen22:12), , [24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), telling [3:11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:11), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Gen12:18), repenting [6:6](https://ref.ly/Gen6:6), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), swearing [Gn 22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16), [Je 22:5](https://ref.ly/Jer22:5), believing [Ex 4:5](https://ref.ly/Exod4:5), [La 4:12](https://ref.ly/Lam4:12), remembering [Psalm 78:35](https://ref.ly/Ps78:35), forgetting [Jb 39:15](https://ref.ly/Job39:15); אָמַר \= *command* (late; in early Heb. the words said are quoted) [Jb 36:10](https://ref.ly/Job36:10), [36:24](https://ref.ly/Job36:24) (זְכֹר in a command) [37:20](https://ref.ly/Job37:20) b, [1 Ch 21:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:18) (contrast [2 S 24:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:18)) etc.; טוֺב כּי it is good *that* … [2 S 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:2) \+ (v. p. 374:b: usu. the inf. c., as [Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18); v. ibid.); [Gn 37:26](https://ref.ly/Gen37:26) מַה־בֶּצַע כִּי נַהֲרֹג what profit *that* we should slay (impf.)…? [Mal 3:14](https://ref.ly/Mal3:14) what proft כִּי שָׁמַרְנוּ *that* we have kept (pf.)…? [Jb 22:3](https://ref.ly/Job22:3) הַחֵפֶץ לְשַׁדַּי כִּי is it pleasure to Shaddai that…? after a pron., as [Psalm 41:12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:12) by *this* I know that thou hast pleasure in me, *that* my enemy cannot triumph over me, [42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5) *these* things will I remember…*that* (*or how*) I used to go, etc., [56:10](https://ref.ly/Ps56:10) *this* I know *that* God is for me [Jb 13:16](https://ref.ly/Job13:16) (הוא). And with כִּי repeated pleon. after an intervening clause [2 S 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7), [Je 26:15](https://ref.ly/Jer26:15) \+; וְכִי …כִּי [Gn 3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Gen29:12), [Ex 4:31](https://ref.ly/Exod4:31), [Jos 2:9](https://ref.ly/Josh2:9), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Josh8:21), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Josh10:1), [1 S 31:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:7), [2 S 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:12), [1 K 11:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:21), [Je 40:7](https://ref.ly/Jer40:7), [40:11](https://ref.ly/Jer40:11); וְכִי …לֵאמֹר [Gn 45:26](https://ref.ly/Gen45:26), Ju [10:10](https://ref.ly/Judg10:10). **b.** כִּי often introduces the *direct* narration (like أَنْ and the Gk. ὅτι *recitativum*, e.g. [Luke 4:21](https://ref.ly/Luke4:21)), in which case it cannot be represented in English (except by inverted commas), [Gn 21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30), [29:33](https://ref.ly/Gen29:33) and she said, ´כִּי שׁמע י Yahweh hath heard, etc.; [Ex 3:12](https://ref.ly/Exod3:12) \= Ju [6:16](https://ref.ly/Judg6:16) and he said, כִּי אֶהְיֶה עִמָּךְ I will be with thee, [Jos 2:24](https://ref.ly/Josh2:24), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16) (v. Dr) [10:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:19) and ye have said to him, כִּי מֶלָךְ תָּשִים עָלֵינוּ Thou shalt set a king over us, [2 S 11:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:23), [1 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:13), [20:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:5), [Ru 1:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:10), cf. [2:21](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:21) (but in reply to a qu. כִּי may \= *because,* v. sub **3;** and so also in sentences giving the expl. of a proper name, [Gn 26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22), [29:32](https://ref.ly/Gen29:32) (but De *surely:* v. infr.), [Ex 2:10](https://ref.ly/Exod2:10) (cf. [Gn 4:25](https://ref.ly/Gen4:25), [41:51](https://ref.ly/Gen41:51), [41:52](https://ref.ly/Gen41:52)); in כִּי מָה, introducing an expostulation, [1 S 29:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:8), [1 K 11:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:22), [2 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:13), it gives the reason for a suppressed ‘ Why do you say this? ’). **c.** esp. after an oath חַי אָ֫נִי, ´חַי י etc., introducing the fact sworn to, [Gn 42:16](https://ref.ly/Gen42:16) by the life of Pharaoh, כִּי מְרַגְּלִים אַתֶּם (I say) *that* ye are spies; but though Heb. usage prob. gave it an asseverative force, Engl. idiom does not require it to be expressed: [Nu 14:22](https://ref.ly/Num14:22), [1 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:3) as ´י liveth, כִּי כְפֶשַע בֵּינִי וּבֵין הַמָּוֶת there is but a step between me and death! [26:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:16), [29:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:6), [Is 49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18) \+; [1 S 14:44](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:44) כֹּה־יַעֲשֶה אֱלֽהִים וְכֹה יוֺסִיף כִּי מוֺת תָּמוּת thus may God do and more also: thou shalt surely die! [2 S 3:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:35), [1 K 2:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:23), [Ru 1:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:17) al.—Note that כִּי when thus used is oft. repeated after an intervening clause, in order that its force may be fully preserved: [Gn 22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16) f. [1 S 14:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:39) כִּי מוֺת יָמוּת (אִם־יֶשְׁנוֺ בְּיוֺנָתָן בְּנִי) כִּי ´חַי י [25:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:34), [2 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:27) ´כִּי אָז וג (לוּלֵא דִּבַּרְתָּ) כִּי, [3:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:9), [15:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:21) Qr [1 K 1:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:30), [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24). **d.** כִּי is used sts. with advs. and interjs. to add force or distinctness to the affirmation which follows: (*a*) so esp. in אַף כִּי (v. אַף); †הֲכִי *is it that…?* (as a neutral interrog.) [2 S 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:1), (expecting a neg. answer) [Gn 29:15](https://ref.ly/Gen29:15) *is it that* thou art my brother, and shalt (therefore) serve me for nothing? [Jb 6:22](https://ref.ly/Job6:22) *is it that* I have said, Give unto me? expressing surprise [Gn 27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36) *is it that* he is called Jacob, and has (hence) supplanted me twice? [2 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:19) an affirm. answer is required (wh. would imply הֲלאֹ כִי): rd. prob. with the ‖ [1 Ch 11:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:25) הִנּוֺ *be\-hold, he,* etc.; † אִם־לאֹ כִּי [Dt 32:30](https://ref.ly/Deut32:30) were it not *that*…; † אָמְנָם כִּי [Jb 12:2](https://ref.ly/Job12:2) of a truth (is it) *that* ye are the people, etc.; † אַךְ כִּי [1 S 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:9); אֶפֶם כִּי [Nu 13:28](https://ref.ly/Num13:28) \+; גַּם כִּי † [Ru 2:21](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:21); † הֲלאֹ כִי [1 S 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:1) (but v. LXX Dr), [2 S 13:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:28); † הִנֵּה כִי [Psalm 128:4](https://ref.ly/Ps128:4); cf. [Psalm 118:10–12](https://ref.ly/Ps118:10-Ps118:12) כִּי אֲמִילַֽם ´בְּשֵׁם י in the name of ´י (is it) *that*—or (I say) *that*—I will mow them down; [Jb 39:27](https://ref.ly/Job39:27) doth the vulture mount up at thy command, וְכִי יָרִים קִנּוֺ *and* (is it) *that* it (so) makes high its nest? [Is 36:19](https://ref.ly/Isa36:19) have the gods of the nations delivered each his land etc.?…וְכִי הִצִּילוּ i.e. (Hi) *and* (is it) *that* they have delivered Samaria out of my hand? \> (Ew§ 354 c De Di) *and that* they have delivered Samaria out of my hand! \= how much less (אַף כִּי) have they, etc.! (‖ [2 K 18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34) כִּי alone, perhaps conformed by error to v:[35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:35); [2 Ch 32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15) אַף כִּי, which however does not decide the sense of the orig. וְכִי). [1 Ch 29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14) וְכִי מִי rd. ומי or כי מי (*b*) in introducing the apodosis, esp. in כִּי עַתָּה (chiefly after לוּלֵא) *indeed* then…, [Gn 31:42](https://ref.ly/Gen31:42), [43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10) for unless we had tarried כִּי עַתָּה שַׁבְנוּ *surely* then we had returned twice, [Nu 22:33](https://ref.ly/Num22:33) (rd. לוּלֵי for אוּלַי); so [1 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:30) LXX (after לוּא), and [13:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:13) Hi We (לֻא for לאֹ); after אִם [Jb 8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6) *surely* then he will awake over thee, etc. (But elsewhere כִּי עַתָּה is simply *for now,* [Gn 29:32](https://ref.ly/Gen29:32), [Jb 7:21](https://ref.ly/Job7:21) \+; or *for then \= for in that case,* [Ex 9:15](https://ref.ly/Exod9:15), [Nu 22:29](https://ref.ly/Num22:29), [Jb 3:13](https://ref.ly/Job3:13), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3) \+). It is dub. whether כִּי אָז has the same sense: for [2 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:27), [19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7) the כִּי in כִּי אָז may be merely resumptive of the כִּי *recitat.* preceding (vid. **a, c**). Rare otherwise: [Ex 22:22](https://ref.ly/Exod22:22) if thou afflict him כִּי אִם־צָעֹק יִצְעַק ’tis *that* (\= *indeed*), if he cries unto me, I will hear him, [Is 7:9](https://ref.ly/Isa7:9) if ye believe not :כִּי לאֹ תֵאָמֵֽנוּ *indeed* ye will not be established. **e.** there seem also to be other cases in which כִּי, standing alone, has an intensive force, introducing a statement with emph., *yea, surely, certainly* (Germ. *ja*—a lighter particle than these Engl. words): see in AV RV [Ex 18:11](https://ref.ly/Exod18:11), [Nu 23:23](https://ref.ly/Num23:23), [1 S 17:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:25), [20:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:26), [2 K 23:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:22), [Is 32:13](https://ref.ly/Isa32:13), [60:9](https://ref.ly/Isa60:9), [Je 22:22](https://ref.ly/Jer22:22), [31:19](https://ref.ly/Jer31:19), [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Hos8:6), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Hos9:12), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Hos10:3), [Am 3:7](https://ref.ly/Amos3:7), [Psalm 76:11](https://ref.ly/Ps76:11), [77:12](https://ref.ly/Ps77:12) (Ew Che), [Pr 30:2](https://ref.ly/Prov30:2) (but not if construed as RVm), [Ec 4:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:16), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:7), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:20), [Jb 28:1](https://ref.ly/Job28:1) \+; [La 3:22](https://ref.ly/Lam3:22) (SyrVer Targum Ew Th Öt) the mercies of ´י, *surely* they are not consumed (rd. prob. תַמּוּ or תָֽמְמוּ for תמנו), [Ru 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:12) כִּי אָמְנָם *yea,* indeed. But it is doubtful whether כִּי has this force in all the passages for which scholars have had recourse to it, and whether in some it is not simply \= *for*. De:[Pr 30:1](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1) would restrict the usage to cases in which a suppressed clause may be understood. **f.** *that,* expressing consecution, esp. after a question implying surprise or deprecation: sq. perf., [Gn 20:9](https://ref.ly/Gen20:9) what have I sinned against thee כִּי הֵבֵאתָ עָלַי *that* thou hast brought upon me? [1 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1) what aileth thee, *that* thou art gone up, etc.? v:[16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16), [36:5](https://ref.ly/Isa36:5), [52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5), [Mic 4:9](https://ref.ly/Mic4:9), Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18); sq. ptcp. Ju [14:3](https://ref.ly/Judg14:3), [1 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:1), [1 K 18:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:9) how have I sinned *that* thou art giving, etc.? [2 K 5:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:7), Ez [24:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:19); usu. sq. impf. [Ex 3:11](https://ref.ly/Exod3:11) who am I כִּי אֵלֵךְ *that* I should go, etc.? [16:7](https://ref.ly/Exod16:7), Ju [8:6](https://ref.ly/Judg8:6), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Judg9:28), [2 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:13), [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13), [29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16) (also pf.), [Psalm 8:5](https://ref.ly/Ps8:5) what is man כִּי תִזְכְּרֶנּוּ? [Jb 3:12](https://ref.ly/Job3:12) or why the breasts :כִּי אִינָֽק *that* I should suck? [6:11](https://ref.ly/Job6:11) מַה־כֹּחִי כִּי אִַיַחֵל, [7:12](https://ref.ly/Job7:12), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Job7:17), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Job10:5) f., [13:25](https://ref.ly/Job13:25) f., [15:12](https://ref.ly/Job15:12) f, [15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14), [16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3), [21:15](https://ref.ly/Job21:15) \+; after a neg., [Gn 40:15](https://ref.ly/Gen40:15) here also I have done nothing כִּי שָמוּ *that* they should have placed me in the dungeon, [Psalm 44:19](https://ref.ly/Ps44:19) f. our heart has not turned backward, etc. כִּי דִכִּיתָנוּ *that* thou shouldst have crushed us, etc., [Is 43:22](https://ref.ly/Isa43:22) not *me* hast thou called on, כִּי יָגַעְתָּ בִּי *that* thou shouldst have wearied thyself with me, [Ho 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hos1:6) (v. RV), [Jb 41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2), [Ru 1:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12) I am too old to have an husband כִּי אָמַרְתִּי *that* I should have said, etc. (cf. Ew§ 337 a; Dr§ 39 δ). **g.** added to preps. כִּי converts them, like אֲשֶׁר, into conjs. ..., as יַעַן כִּי because *that*...: v. sub יַעַן, עַד, עַל, עֵקֶב, תַּחַת.

 **2\. a.** Of time, *when,* of the past וַיְהִי כִּי [Gn 6:1](https://ref.ly/Gen6:1) (cf. BuUrg. 6\), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Gen26:8), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1), [2 S 6:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:13), [7:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:1), [19:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:26) \+ (כַּאֲשֶׁר and esp. כְּ c. inf., are more freq.); וְהָיָה כִּי (simple וְ) [1 S 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:12), [17:48](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:48), [Jos 22:7](https://ref.ly/Josh22:7), Ju [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5) והיה כי יאמרו and it would be, whenever (freq.) they said, [Je 44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19) (ptcp.), [Ho 11:1](https://ref.ly/Hos11:1), [Psalm 32:3](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3) כי החרשׁתי *when* I was silent, [Jb 31:21](https://ref.ly/Job31:21); [31:26](https://ref.ly/Job31:26); [31:29](https://ref.ly/Job31:29); of present (usu. with impf.) as [Ex 18:16](https://ref.ly/Exod18:16) כִּי יִהְיֶה לָהֶם דָּבָר *when* they have a matter, [1 S 24:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:20), [Is 1:12](https://ref.ly/Isa1:12), [30:21](https://ref.ly/Isa30:21), [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12), [Zc 7:5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5), [7:6](https://ref.ly/Zech7:6), [Mal 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mal1:8), [Psalm 49:19](https://ref.ly/Ps49:19) and men praise thee כִּי תֵיטִיב לָךְ *when* thou doest well to thyself, [102:1](https://ref.ly/Ps102:1), [127:5](https://ref.ly/Ps127:5) \+, with pf. Ez [3:19–21](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:19-Ezek3:21), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:9), [Pr 11:15](https://ref.ly/Prov11:15), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22); esp. of future, as [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12) כִּי תַעֲבֹד אֶת־הָאֲדָמָה *when* thou shalt till the ground it shall not, etc., [24:41](https://ref.ly/Gen24:41), [30:33](https://ref.ly/Gen30:33), [31:49](https://ref.ly/Gen31:49), [32:18](https://ref.ly/Gen32:18), [Ex 7:9](https://ref.ly/Exod7:9) *when* Pharaoh shall speak unto you, [Dt 4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Deut6:20) \+; in phrase (´תאֹמַר וג) וְכִי תאֹמְרוּ [Lv 25:20](https://ref.ly/Lev25:20), [Dt 18:21](https://ref.ly/Deut18:21), [Is 8:19](https://ref.ly/Isa8:19), [36:7](https://ref.ly/Isa36:7), [Je 13:22](https://ref.ly/Jer13:22); and esp. in ... וְהָיָה כִּי [Gn 12:12](https://ref.ly/Gen12:12), [46:33](https://ref.ly/Gen46:33), [Dt 6:10](https://ref.ly/Deut6:10), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Deut15:16), [1 S 10:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:7), [25:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:30), [Is 8:21](https://ref.ly/Isa8:21), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12) \+ oft.; with pf. [Is 16:12](https://ref.ly/Isa16:12), [1 Ch 17:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:11) (altered fr. impf. [2 S 7:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:12)); with ptcp. (unusual) [Nu 33:51](https://ref.ly/Num33:51), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Num34:2), [Dt 11:31](https://ref.ly/Deut11:31), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Deut18:9). **b.** elsewhere כִּי has a force approximating to *if,* though it usu. represents a case as more likely to occur than אִם:—(mostly with impf.) [Gn 38:16](https://ref.ly/Gen38:16), [Nu 5:20](https://ref.ly/Num5:20), [10:32](https://ref.ly/Num10:32), [Dt 6:25](https://ref.ly/Deut6:25), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Deut7:17), [28:2](https://ref.ly/Deut28:2), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Deut28:13), [1 S 20:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:13), [2 S 19:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:8), [2 K 4:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:29), [18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22), [Je 38:15](https://ref.ly/Jer38:15), [Pr 4:8](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8), [Jb 7:13](https://ref.ly/Job7:13) (כי אמרתי *when* I say), [19:28](https://ref.ly/Job19:28); oft. in laws, as [Ex 21:14](https://ref.ly/Exod21:14), [21:33](https://ref.ly/Exod21:33), [21:35](https://ref.ly/Exod21:35), [21:37](https://ref.ly/Exod21:37), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Exod22:4), [22:5](https://ref.ly/Exod22:5) etc., [Dt 13:13](https://ref.ly/Deut13:13), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Deut14:24), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Deut15:7), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Deut15:12), [17:2](https://ref.ly/Deut17:2), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Deut18:6), [18:21](https://ref.ly/Deut18:21) etc.; sometimes, in particular, to state a principle broadly, after which special cases are introduced by אִם, as [Ex 21:2](https://ref.ly/Exod21:2) *when* (כִּי) thou buyest a Hebrew servant, he shall serve thee six years, after which v:[3–5](https://ref.ly/Exod21:3-Exod21:5) follow four special cases with אִם *if:* so [21:7](https://ref.ly/Exod21:7) (כי), v:[8–11](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8-Exod21:11) (אם); v:[18](https://ref.ly/Exod21:18) (כי), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19) (אם); v:[20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20) (כי), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod21:21) (אם); v:[22](https://ref.ly/Exod21:22) f; v:[28–32](https://ref.ly/Exod21:28-Exod21:32); [Lv 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lev1:2) (כי), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Lev1:3), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Lev1:10) (אם) [4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Lev4:3), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Lev4:13), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Lev4:27), [4:32](https://ref.ly/Lev4:32), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Lev13:2) ff. [Nu 30:3](https://ref.ly/Num30:3) ff. \+; though this distinction is not uniformly observed, contrast e.g. [Ex 21:5](https://ref.ly/Exod21:5) with [Dt 15:16](https://ref.ly/Deut15:16), [Nu 5:19](https://ref.ly/Num5:19) and v:[20](https://ref.ly/Num5:20).—N.B. with כִּי \= *when* or *if,* the subject is oft. prefixed for distinctness and emph.: [1 K 8:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:37) רָעָב ´כִּי־יִהְיֶה בָאָרֶץ דֶּבֶר כִּי יִהְיֶה וג, [Is 28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18), [Mi 5:4](https://ref.ly/Mic5:4) אַשּׁוּר כִּי־יָבאֹ בְאַרְצֵנוּ, [Psalm 62:11](https://ref.ly/Ps62:11), Ez [3:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:19) (וְאַתָּה), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:9), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:13), [18:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:5), [18:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:18), [18:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:21), [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6) (cf. v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:2)); and esp. in laws of P, as אָדָם …כִּי [Lv 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lev1:2); [13:2](https://ref.ly/Lev13:2), ... נֶפֶשׁ כִּי [2:1](https://ref.ly/Lev2:1), [4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Lev5:1), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Lev5:4), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Lev5:15), similarly [15:2](https://ref.ly/Lev15:2), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Lev15:16), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Lev15:19), [15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Lev22:12), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Lev22:13), [22:14](https://ref.ly/Lev22:14) etc., rather differently [Nu 5:20](https://ref.ly/Num5:20). **c.** *when* or *if*, with a concessive force, i.e. *though:*—(*a*) with impf. [Je 4:30](https://ref.ly/Jer4:30), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Jer4:30), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Jer4:30), [14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12), [49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16) כִּי־תַגְבִּיהַּ כַּנֶּשֶׁר קִֶ\+נְּ *though* thou make high like the vulture thy nest, I will bring thee down thence, [51:53](https://ref.ly/Jer51:53), [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15), [Zc 8:6](https://ref.ly/Zech8:6), [Psalm 37:24](https://ref.ly/Ps37:24), [49:19](https://ref.ly/Ps49:19) f. *though* in his lifetime he bless himself ... he shall come, etc., perh. also [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23) Ew (but Hi Gf Ke *for*), [50:11](https://ref.ly/Jer50:11) Ew Ke (Hi *yea*); and strengthened by גַּם, גַּם כִּי [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5), [Psalm 23:4](https://ref.ly/Ps23:4) (cf. Dr§ 143\); (*b*) with perf. (rare) [Mi 7:8](https://ref.ly/Mic7:8) כִּי נָפַלְתִּי קָמְתִּי *though* I have fallen, I rise, [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) (si vera 1\.), [Psalm 21:12](https://ref.ly/Ps21:12) (Hi Ew Now), [119:88](https://ref.ly/Ps119:88) (Ew De). **3\.** *Because, since* ὅτι—**a.** [Gn 3:14](https://ref.ly/Gen3:14) *because* thou hast done this, cursed art thou, etc., v:[17](https://ref.ly/Gen3:17), [18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) the cry of S. and G.—*because* it is great ... (subj. prefixed for emph.: cf. **2** N.B.) [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15); in answer to a qu., [Gn 27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20), [Ex 1:19](https://ref.ly/Exod1:19), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Exod18:15), [2 S 19:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:43) \+. Enunciating the conditions under which a fut. action is conceived as possible (Germ. *indem*) [Lv 22:9](https://ref.ly/Lev22:9), [Dt 4:29](https://ref.ly/Deut4:29) כי תדרשׁנו, [12:20](https://ref.ly/Deut12:20) (v.:Dr), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Deut12:25), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Deut12:28), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Deut13:19), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Deut14:24), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Deut16:15), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Deut19:9) \+, [1 K 8:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:35) (cf. v:[33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:33) אֲשֶׁר), v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:36) כִּי תוֺרֵם, [Pr 4:8b](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8). **b.** more commonly the causal sentence follows, as [Gn 2:3](https://ref.ly/Gen2:3) and God blessed the seventh day ´כִּי בוֺ שָׁבַת וג *because* on it he rested, etc., [4:25](https://ref.ly/Gen4:25) etc., in which case it may oft. be rendered *for,* [Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5), [2:28](https://ref.ly/Gen2:28), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Gen3:20), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Gen5:24), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Gen6:12), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13), [Psalm 6:3](https://ref.ly/Ps6:3) heal me כִּי נִבְהֲלוּ עֲצָמָי *for* my bones are vexed, [10:14](https://ref.ly/Ps10:14), [25:16](https://ref.ly/Ps25:16), [27:10](https://ref.ly/Ps27:10) \+ very oft. Spec. after vbs. expressive of mental emotions, as rejoicing [Is 14:29](https://ref.ly/Isa14:29), [Psalm 58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11), being angry [Gn 31:35](https://ref.ly/Gen31:35), [45:5](https://ref.ly/Gen45:5), fearing [43:18](https://ref.ly/Gen43:18), [Psalm 49:17](https://ref.ly/Ps49:17) etc. Iron. [1 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:27) *for* he is a god etc. (4 t.); [Pr 30:4](https://ref.ly/Prov30:4), [Jb 38:5](https://ref.ly/Job38:5) כִּי תֵדָע *for* or *since* thou knowest. With subj. prefixed [Psalm 128:2](https://ref.ly/Ps128:2). Repeated (with anacol.) [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19). **c.** the causal relation expressed by כִּי is sometimes subtle, esp. in poetry, and not apparent without careful study of a passage. Thus sts. it justifies a statement or description by pointing to a pregnant fact which involves it, as [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8) a, [Jb 6:21](https://ref.ly/Job6:21) (ground of the comparison v:[15–20](https://ref.ly/Job6:15-Job6:20)), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Job14:16) (*For*…: ground of the wishes expressed v:[13–15](https://ref.ly/Job6:13-Job6:15)), [16:22](https://ref.ly/Job16:22) (ground of v:[20](https://ref.ly/Job16:20) f.) [30:26](https://ref.ly/Job30:26) (*For* ...), or by pointing to a general truth which it exemplifies [Jb 5:6](https://ref.ly/Job5:6) (reason why complaining v:[2–5](https://ref.ly/Job5:2-Job5:5) is foolish), [15:34](https://ref.ly/Job15:34), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Job23:14); sts. it is *explicative,* justifying a statement by unfolding the particulars wh. establish or exemplify it [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5) a, [Is 1:30](https://ref.ly/Isa1:30), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Isa5:7), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Isa7:8), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Isa9:4), [10:8–11](https://ref.ly/Isa10:8-Isa10:11), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10) (development of v:[9a](https://ref.ly/Isa13:9)), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6) f. (developing the characters of the נָבָל and כִּילַי, and so explaining why they will no longer be esteemed v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5)); [Jb 11:16](https://ref.ly/Job11:16) ff. (explic. of v:[15b](https://ref.ly/Job11:15)), [18:8](https://ref.ly/Job18:8) ff. (justifying v:[7](https://ref.ly/Job18:7)), [22:26](https://ref.ly/Job22:26) ff. (justifying v:[25](https://ref.ly/Job22:25)); elsewhere the cause is expressed indirectly or figuratively [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6) (reason why invitation v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa2:5) is needed), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10) (sterility of the soil the cause of the desolation v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa5:9)), [18:5](https://ref.ly/Isa18:5), [28:8](https://ref.ly/Isa28:8) (proof of the intoxication v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7)), [31:7](https://ref.ly/Isa31:7) (reason for the exhortation v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa31:6): the certainty that the folly of idolatry will soon be recognized), [Jb 7:21](https://ref.ly/Job7:21) (for soon it will be too late to pardon), [27:8–10](https://ref.ly/Job27:8-Job27:10) (Job wishes his enemy the lot of the wicked, *because* this is so hopeless); or כִּי relates not to the v. which immed. precedes or follows, but to several, as [Is 7:16](https://ref.ly/Isa7:16) f. (v:[17](https://ref.ly/Isa7:17) specially the ground of the people being reduced to simple fare v:[15](https://ref.ly/Isa7:15)), [21:6](https://ref.ly/Isa21:6) ff. (ground of the statements v:[1–5](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1-Isa21:5)), [Jb 4:5](https://ref.ly/Job4:5) (ground of v:[2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2)), [14:7–12](https://ref.ly/Job14:7-Job14:12) (v:[10–12](https://ref.ly/Job14:10-Job14:12) specially the ground for the appeal in v:[6](https://ref.ly/Job14:6)), [23:10–13](https://ref.ly/Job23:10-Job23:13) (ground why God cannot be found v:[8](https://ref.ly/Job23:8) f.), [Psalm 73:21](https://ref.ly/Ps73:21) (ground not of v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20), but of the general train of thought v:[2–14](https://ref.ly/Ps73:2-Ps73:14)); similarly [Gn 4:24](https://ref.ly/Gen4:24), [Dt 18:14](https://ref.ly/Deut18:14), [Je 30:11](https://ref.ly/Jer30:11) the reason lies not in the words immed. after כִּי, but in the second part of the sentence; or, on the other hand, it may state the reason for a partic. word, [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20) (justifying ‘ nought but terror ’ v:[19](https://ref.ly/Isa28:19)), [Jb 23:17](https://ref.ly/Job23:17) (*God's* hostility v:[16](https://ref.ly/Job23:16) the cause of his misery, not the calamity as such). Sometimes also כִּי, in a poet. or rhet. style, gives the reason for a thought not expressed but implied, esp. the answer to a qu.; [Is 28:11](https://ref.ly/Isa28:11) (the mockeries of v:[10](https://ref.ly/Isa28:10) have a meaning) ‘ *for* with men of strange lips, etc. he will speak unto this people, ’ who will retort the mockeries, charged with a new and terrible meaning, upon those who uttered them (v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa28:13)); \= (no,) *for* [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28) (see RVm), [Jb 22:2](https://ref.ly/Job22:2) b *no*, he that is wise is profitable to himself, [31:18](https://ref.ly/Job31:18), [39:14](https://ref.ly/Job39:14) (see v:[13b](https://ref.ly/Job39:13)), [Psalm 44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24) (he cannot do this, v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ps44:23)) *for* for thy sake are we killed, etc., [130:4](https://ref.ly/Ps130:4) *no*, with thee is forgiveness; \= (yes,) *for* [Is 49:25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25) (see the qu. v:[24](https://ref.ly/Isa49:24)), [66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8). **d.** כִּי …כִּי ἀσυνδέτως sts. introduce the proximate and ultimate cause respectively, [Gn 3:19](https://ref.ly/Gen3:19), [26:7](https://ref.ly/Gen26:7), [43:32](https://ref.ly/Gen43:32), [47:20](https://ref.ly/Gen47:20), [Ex 23:33](https://ref.ly/Exod23:33) *for* \[else] thou wilt serve their gods, *for* it will be a snare to thee, [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Isa6:5) a, b [10:22](https://ref.ly/Isa10:22) f. [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3) f. [8:8](https://ref.ly/Job8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Job8:9), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Job8:9), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Job29:11) f.; sts. they introduce two co\-ordinate causes (where we should insert *and*), [Ex 23:21](https://ref.ly/Exod23:21), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Exod23:22), [Is 6:5](https://ref.ly/Isa6:5) b, c I am undone, *because* I am of unclean lips…, *because* mine eyes have seen ´י of hosts, [15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Isa15:9), [Zp 3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8) f. [Jb 15:25](https://ref.ly/Job15:25), [15:27](https://ref.ly/Job15:27), [20:19](https://ref.ly/Job20:19) f. [31:11](https://ref.ly/Job31:11) f.. But וְכִי …כִּי also occurs, [Gn 33:11](https://ref.ly/Gen33:11), [Nu 5:20](https://ref.ly/Num5:20) (if), Jo 7:15, Ju [6:30](https://ref.ly/Judg6:30), [1 S 19:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:4), [22:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:17), [1 K 2:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:26), [Is 65:16](https://ref.ly/Isa65:16) \+. **e.** after a neg. כִּי *for* becomes \= *but* (Germ. *sondern*): [Gn 17:15](https://ref.ly/Gen17:15) thou shalt not call her name Sarai, כִּי שָרָה שְׁמָהּ *for* (\= *but*) Sarah shall be her name, [24:3](https://ref.ly/Gen24:3) f. [45:8](https://ref.ly/Gen45:8), [Ex 1:19](https://ref.ly/Exod1:19), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Exod16:8) not against us are your murmurings, ´כי על־י *for* (they are) agst. ´י \= *but* agst. ´י, [Dt 21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17), [1 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:3) (אַל), [27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1) (v. Dr), [1 K 21:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:17), [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7), [28:27](https://ref.ly/Isa28:27), [29:23](https://ref.ly/Isa29:23), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Isa30:5), [Psalm 44:8](https://ref.ly/Ps44:8), [118:17](https://ref.ly/Ps118:17) \+ oft.; so in לא כי *nay, for* \= *nay, but,* as [Gn 18:15](https://ref.ly/Gen18:15) לאֹ כִּי צָחָֽקְתְּ *nay, but* thou didst laugh, [19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [42:12](https://ref.ly/Gen42:12), [Jos 5:14](https://ref.ly/Josh5:14), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16) MSS LXX (v. Dr), [12:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:12), [2 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:18), [24:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:24), [1 K 2:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:30), [3:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:22), [11:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:22), [Is 30:16](https://ref.ly/Isa30:16) *nay, but* we will flee upon horses. *Note.*—כִּי is sts. of difficult and uncertain interpretation, and in some of the passages quoted a different expl. is tenable. Authorities esp. read the Heb. differently, when the choice is between *for* and *yea*. E.g. [Is 8:23](https://ref.ly/Isa8:23) Ges Ew§ 330 b *doch* (*no, but*); Hi Di *for* (taking v:[22](https://ref.ly/Isa8:22) as RVm); Ch *surely:* [15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1) Ges Ew Hi Di *surely;* De *for:* [39:8](https://ref.ly/Isa39:8) Ges Hi De *surely;* Di *for* (expl. of טוֺב): Ez [11:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:16) Hi Ke Co *surely;* Ew Sm *because*.—In [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25) the tense of וַתְּחַלֲלֶהָ makes it prob. that כֹּי is *for* (Dr§ 153\). [Jb 22:29](https://ref.ly/Job22:29) is taken with least violence to usage (גַּאֲוָה) as Hi: *When* they humble thee, and thou sayest (\= complainest) Pride! he will save, etc. **2\.** (About 140 t.) the two particles being closely conjoined, and relating to the *same* clause—**a.** *limiting* the prec. clause, except (after a negative, or an oath, or question, the equivalent of a negative)—the most usual term for expressing this idea: sq. vb. [Gn 32:27](https://ref.ly/Gen32:27) I will not let thee go; כִּי אִם־בֵּרַכְתָּֽנִי lit. *but* (כִּי **3 e**) *if* thou bless me (sc. I will let thee go), i.e., subordinating the second clause to the first, ‘ I will not let thee go, *except* thou bless me; ’ [Lv 22:6](https://ref.ly/Lev22:6) he shall not eat of the holy things כִּי אִם־רָחַץ *except* he have washed his flesh, [Is 65:6](https://ref.ly/Isa65:6), [Am 3:7](https://ref.ly/Amos3:7), [Ru 3:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:18), [La 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lam5:21) f. (Ew Näg Ke Che Öt), turn thou us unto thee, etc., *unless* thou have utterly rejected us, (and) art very wroth with us (\= *Or* hast thou utterly rejected us? etc. Ew Öt); sq. a noun, *except, but,* [Gn 28:17](https://ref.ly/Gen28:17) this is nothing ´כִּי אִם־בֵּית הָאֱ *but* the house of God, [32:9](https://ref.ly/Gen32:9) he withholds from me nothing כִּי אִם־אוֺתָךְ *except* thee, [Lv 21:2](https://ref.ly/Lev21:2), [Nu 14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30) (after אִם), [26:65](https://ref.ly/Num26:65) (cf. [32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12) בִּלְתִּי), [Jos 14:4](https://ref.ly/Josh14:4), [1 S 30:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:17), [30:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:22), [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3) אֵין כֹּל כִּי אִ־ם (so [2 K 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:2)), [19:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:29), [1 K 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:1) (after אִם), [22:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:31), [2 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:15), [9:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:35), [13:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:7), [Je 22:17](https://ref.ly/Jer22:17), [44:14](https://ref.ly/Jer44:14) \+; after אַל, [2 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:6); sq. an adv. clause, [Gn 42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33), [2 S 3:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:13) (but כִּי אִם and לִפְנֵי are mutually exclusive: rd. prob. with LXX כִּי אִם־הֵבֵיאתָ); after an interrog. [Is 42:19](https://ref.ly/Isa42:19) who is blind כִּי אִם־עַבְדִּי *but* my servant ? (who is blind in comparison with him ?), [Dt 10:12](https://ref.ly/Deut10:12), [Mi 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8), [Ec 5:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:10), [2 Ch 2:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:5). **b.** the *if* being neglected, and treated as pleonastic (cf. אִם **1 c**), so that the clause is no longer a limitation of the preceding clause but a contradiction of it: but rather, but (\= a slightly strengthened כִּי), [Gn 15:4](https://ref.ly/Gen15:4) this man shall not be thy heir; ´כִּי אִם־אֲשֶׁר וג *but* one that shall come forth from thy own bowels, he shall be thy heir (cf. [1 K 8:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:19)), [32:29](https://ref.ly/Gen32:29) thy name shall no more be called Jacob כִּי אִם־יִשְרָאֵל *but* Israel (cf. כִּי alone [17:15](https://ref.ly/Gen17:15)), [47:18](https://ref.ly/Gen47:18) we will not hide it from my lord, *but* the money…is all made over to, etc., [Ex 12:9](https://ref.ly/Exod12:9) not boiled in water, *but* roast with fire, [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Deut12:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Deut16:6), Jo 23:8, [1 S 2:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:15) he will not take of thee boiled flesh :כִּי אִם־חָֽי *but* raw, [8:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:19) לאֹ כִּי אִם־מֶלֶךְ יִהְיֶה עָלֵינוּ nay, *but* a king shall be over us (cf. כי alone, [10:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:19), [12:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:12)), [21:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:5), [2 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6), [1 K 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:18), [2 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:23) (פֶּן), [Is 33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21), [55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10), [55:11](https://ref.ly/Isa55:11), [59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2), [Je 3:10](https://ref.ly/Jer3:10), [7:32](https://ref.ly/Jer7:32), [9:23](https://ref.ly/Jer9:23), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Jer16:15), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Jer20:3), Ez [36:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:22), [44:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:10), [Am 8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11), [Psalm 1:2](https://ref.ly/Ps1:2), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ps1:4), [Pr 23:17](https://ref.ly/Prov23:17) (אַל) Psalm \+; with the principal verb repeated (as [Gn 15:4](https://ref.ly/Gen15:4), [1 K 8:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:19)), [Lv 21:14](https://ref.ly/Lev21:14), Ez [44:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:22), [Nu 10:30](https://ref.ly/Num10:30), [2 K 23:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:23), [Je 39:12](https://ref.ly/Jer39:12) Kt (Qr om. אִם), cf. [7:23](https://ref.ly/Jer7:23). Occas. in colloq. language, the neg., it seems, is left to be understood: [1 S 26:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:10) as ´י liveth, (by no means,) כִּי אִם *but* ´י shall smite him, [2 S 13:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:33) Kt (by no means,) *but* Amnon alone is dead (Qr om. אִם). Sq. imv. [Is 65:18](https://ref.ly/Isa65:18), Ez [12:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:23), [Je 39:12](https://ref.ly/Jer39:12), [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8). Sts. also, though rarely (and not certainly), כִּי אִם appears to have the force of *only* even without a previous neg.: [Gn 40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) כִּי אִם־זְכַרְתָּנִי אִתְּ *only* have (?) me in remembrance with thyself (but rd. perh. אַךְ for כִּי; v Dr§ 119 δ n., the use of a bare pf., without לוּ, or even waw consec., to express a wish or command is unexampled), [Nu 24:22](https://ref.ly/Num24:22) כִּי אִ־ם :יִהְיֶה לְבָעֵר קָֽיִן *only, nevertheless,* the Kenite shall be for extermination (cf. Di), [Jb 42:8](https://ref.ly/Job42:8) (De Di) כִּי אִם־פָּנָיו אֶשָּא **c.** after an oath כִּי אִם appears to \= a strengthened כִּי (cf. בִּלְתִּי אִם, עַד אִם:אִם **1 c**), introducing the fact sworn to (v. כִּי **1 c**): [2 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20) as ´י liveth, כִּי אִם־רַצְתִּי *surely* I will run (pf. of certitude) after him, etc., [Je 51:14](https://ref.ly/Jer51:14) (Ges Hi Gf RV) *surley* I will fill thee with men (viz. assailants), etc. (but Ew Ke Ch treat the particles as separate (כִּי as כִּי **1 c**): though I have filled thee with men—i.e. increased thy population,—yet shall they—the assailants—lift up the shout against thee), [2 S 15:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:21) Kt (Qr omits אִם); after an assever. part. [Ru 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:12) Kt וְעַתָּה כִּי אָמְנָם כִּי אִם גּוֺאֵל אָנֹכִי and now, yea indeed, *surely* I am thy kinsman (Qr omits אִם); the oath being understood, Ju [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7) if ye do thus, כִּי אִם־נִקַּמְתִּי *surely* (Ges *hercle*) I will avenge myself, [1 S 21:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:6) כִּי אִם־אִשָּׁה עֲצֻרָה לָנוּ *of a truth* women have been kept from us, etc., [1 K 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:6) *surely* tomorrow I will send, etc., [Pr 23:18](https://ref.ly/Prov23:18) (v. De) *surely* there *is* a reward; perh. also [Jb 42:8](https://ref.ly/Job42:8). 

> \[Str3588\+5921\+3651] † כִּי עַל כֵּן *forasmuch as,* a peculiar phrase found [Gn 18:5](https://ref.ly/Gen18:5), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10), [38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26), [Nu 10:31](https://ref.ly/Num10:31), [14:43](https://ref.ly/Num14:43), Ju [6:22](https://ref.ly/Judg6:22), [2 S 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:20) Qr (rightly), [Je 29:28](https://ref.ly/Jer29:28), [38:4](https://ref.ly/Jer38:4)—lit. *for therefore,* emphasizing the ground pleonastically (Ew§ 353 a ). The orig. force of the phrase is traceable in some of the passages in which it occurs, as [Gn 18:5](https://ref.ly/Gen18:5) let me fetch a morsel of bread, and comfort your heart; כִּי־עַל־כֵּן עֲבַרְתֶּם עַל עַבְדְּכֶם *for therefore* (sc. to partake of such hospitality) are ye come to your servant, [Nu 14:43](https://ref.ly/Num14:43) the Amalekite and the Canaanite are there, and ye will fall by the sword, כִּי־עַל־כֵּן שַׁבְתֶּם *for therefore* (to encounter such a fate) have ye turned back from ´י etc.: but in process of time the distinct sense of its component parts was no doubt gradually obscured, and it thus came to be used conventionally, as a *mere* particle of causation, even where there was no preceding statement to which עַל כֵּן *therefore* could be explicitly referred. אֲשֶׁר עַל כֵּן appears to be used similarly (cf. אֲשֶׁר **8 c**) † [Jb 34:27](https://ref.ly/Job34:27).

> \[Str3587] I. כִּי, *branding,* v. sub כוה. p. 465

## כיד (id: BDB04438)

כיד (√ of foll.; cf. perh. Ar. كَادَ in sense *labour, take pains, strive,* or *struggle* with, كَيْدٌ *war*).

> \[Str3591] † I. כִּידוֺן **n.\[m.]** dart, javelin (NH *id.*)—abs. ´כ [Jos 8:18](https://ref.ly/Josh8:18) \+ 6 t.; כִּידֹן [Je 50:42](https://ref.ly/Jer50:42); cstr. כִּידוֺן [1 S 17:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:6); —*dart, javelin* (distinct fr. חֲנִית *spear, lance,* q.v.), (ך)אשׁר ביד ´נטה בַּכּ [Jos 8:18](https://ref.ly/Josh8:18), [8:18](https://ref.ly/Josh8:18), cf. v:[26](https://ref.ly/Josh8:26); ´רַעַשׁ כּ [Jb 41:21](https://ref.ly/Job41:21) *the rushing sound of a dart;* בְּהֶרֶב ´וּבַחֲנִית וּבְכ [1 S 17:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:45) (weapons of Goliath), ´כ נְחשֶׁת בֵּין כְּתֵפָיו v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:6) (חֲנִית in v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:7)); \+ חֲנִית [Jb 39:23](https://ref.ly/Job39:23) also; ´קֶשֶׁת וְכ [Je 6:23](https://ref.ly/Jer6:23), [50:42](https://ref.ly/Jer50:42).

> \[Str3592] † II. כִּידֹן **n.pr.m.** [1 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:9), LXXL Χεδων, A Χειλω; \= נָכוֺן [2 S 6:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:6) (LXX Νωδαβ, A Ναχων, LXXL Ορνα).

> \[Str3590,3593] כִּידוֺד v. sub כדד; כִּידוֺר v. sub כדר. p. 461

> \[Str3594] † כִּיּוּן **n.pr.dei** [Am 5:26](https://ref.ly/Amos5:26), prob. \= As. *kaivânu,* planet *Saturn* (Ar. and Pers. كَيْوَانٌ Syr. ܟܶܐܘܳܢ), regarded as god; orig. pronunc. כֵּיוָן SchrCOT ad loc., cf. Köii. 151 (pointing כִּיּוּן intended to suggest √ כון as something *established, firm*);*kaimâ̆nu* \= *kaiânu,* acc. to JenCosm. iii. 502 who der. fr. כון (cf. Thes; so ZimBP 17\); \> HptZA ii. 266, 281 f. rds. כַּיָּוָן (for כְּאָמָן…), reading the Babyl. name *Ka’âmânu;* v. also M\-AJBL 1892, xi. 86 n. 39\.

> \[Str3595] כִּיּוֺר, v. sub I. כור. p. 468

> \[Str3596] כִּילַי, כֵּלַי [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Isa32:7) v. נכל (Köii. 118\). p. 647

> \[Str3597] † כֵּילַפּוֺת **n.\[f.]** axe (loan\-word (cf. Frä74) fr. As. *kalappatu* v. DlHWB 333\)—יַהֲלֽמָוּן ´בְּכַשִּׁיל וְכ [Psalm 74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6) (‖קַרְדֻּמּוֺת v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps74:5)).

> \[Str3598] כִּימָה v. sub כום. p. 465

> \[Str3599] † כִּיס **n.m.**:[Pr 1:14](https://ref.ly/Prov1:14) bag, purse (perh. kindred with כּוֺס, q.v.; Ar. كِيسٌ (prob. fr. Pers. كِيسَةٌ Lane2640); Eth. ኪሰ፡ NH כִּיס; Aram. כִּיסָא, ܟܻܝܣܳܐ Plam. מן כיסה *from his purse* \= at his own expense, Vogp. 13 al.)—*bag,* in which were carried: **a.** weights (v. אֶבֶן **5**) [Dt 25:13](https://ref.ly/Deut25:13), [Mi 6:11](https://ref.ly/Mic6:11), [Pr 16:11](https://ref.ly/Prov16:11); **b.** money, i.e. *a purse* [Is 46:6](https://ref.ly/Isa46:6), [Pr 1:14](https://ref.ly/Prov1:14).

> \[Str3595,3600] כִּיִּר, \[כִּיר], כִּירַיִם v. sub I. כור. p. 468

> \[Str3601] כִּישׁוֺר v. sub כשׁר. p. 507

> \[Str3602] כָּ֫כָה v. sub כה p. 462 supr.

> \[Str3603] כִּכָּר v. sub כרר; כֹּל v. sub I. כלל. p. 503

## כָּלָא (id: BDB04452)

\[Str3607] † \[כָּלָא] **vb.** shut up, restrain, withhold (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּלָא, ܟܠܴܐ; Eth. ከልአ፡ *restrain, hinder;* As. *Kâlû, id.;* Ar. كَلَأَ *protect* Qor 21:43 cf BaZMG 1887, 605 Anm. 4\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. כְּלָאוֺ [Je 32:3](https://ref.ly/Jer32:3); 3 fs. כָּֽלְאָה [Hg 1:10](https://ref.ly/Hag1:10); 2 fs. sf. כְּלִתִ֫נִי [1 S 25:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:33); 1 S. כָּלִאתִי [Psalm 119:101](https://ref.ly/Ps119:101); 3 pl. כָּֽלְאוּ [Hg 1:10](https://ref.ly/Hag1:10), כָּלוּ [1 S 6:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:10); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִכְלֶה [Gn 23:6](https://ref.ly/Gen23:6); 2 ms. תִּכְלָא [Psalm 40:12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:12); 2 fs. תִּכְלָאִָי [Is 43:6](https://ref.ly/Isa43:6); 1 S. אֶכְלָא [Psalm 40:10](https://ref.ly/Ps40:10); *Imv.* sf. כְּלָאֵם [Nu 11:28](https://ref.ly/Num11:28); *Inf. cstr.* לִכְלוֺא [Ec 8:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:8); *Pt. pass.* כָּלוּא [Je 32:2](https://ref.ly/Jer32:2), כָּלֻא [Psalm 88:9](https://ref.ly/Ps88:9); (on the metapl. forms, as if fr. כלה, v. Ges§ 75, 21 c Köi. 611\);—**1\.** *shut up,* c. acc. [1 S 6:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:10) (\+ בְ loc.), [Je 32:3](https://ref.ly/Jer32:3); pt. pass. [Je 32:2](https://ref.ly/Jer32:2) (בְ loc.), fig. [Psalm 88:9](https://ref.ly/Ps88:9) (opp. יצא). **2\.** *restrain,* c. acc. [1 S 25:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:33) (\+ מִבּוֺא בְדָמִים), [Psalm 119:101](https://ref.ly/Ps119:101) (\+ מִכָּ־ל); כְּלָאֵם (sc. מֵהִתְנַבֵּא) [Nu 11:28](https://ref.ly/Num11:28); שְפָתַי ´כ [Psalm 40:10](https://ref.ly/Ps40:10); אֶת־הָרוּחַ ´כ [Ec 8:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:8). On [Psalm 74:11](https://ref.ly/Ps74:11) v. כלה **Pi. 2\. 3\.** *withhold,* אֶת־קִבְרוֺ ´כ מִמְּ מִקְּבֹר מֵתְֶָ [Gn 23:6](https://ref.ly/Gen23:6) (P), רַחֲמֶיְ מִמֶָּנּי ´כ [Psalm 40:12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:12), [Hg 1:10](https://ref.ly/Hag1:10) b, prob. also va (rd. טָל for MT LXX מִטָּל, We); abs. אַל־תִּכְלָאִי (sc. exiles) [Is 43:6](https://ref.ly/Isa43:6) (‖תְּנִי). **Niph.** *Impf.* וַיִּכָּלֵא הַגֶּשֶׁם מִן־הַשָּׁמַיִם [Gn 8:2](https://ref.ly/Gen8:2) (J), *and the rain from heaven was restrained,* cf. וַיִּכָּֽלְאוּ מַיִם רַבִּים Ez [31:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:15) (in fig.; ‖מנע); וַיִּכָּלֵא הָעָם מֵהָבִיא [Ex 36:6](https://ref.ly/Exod36:6) (P). **Pi.** \[Pf. כִּלָּא and] *Inf. cstr.* לְכַלֵּא v. כלה **Pi.**

> \[Str3608,1004] † כֶּ֫לֶא **n.\[m.]** confinement, restraint, imprisonment;—abs. ´כ [1 K 22:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:27) \+ 6 t.; sf. כִּלְאוֺ [2 K 25:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:29), [Je 52:33](https://ref.ly/Jer52:33); pl. כְּלָאִים [Is 42:22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22); —*confinement,* in combin. ´כ(ה)בֵּית (cf. As. *bit kili, prison,* DlHWB 328\) [1 K 22:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:27) \= [2 Ch 18:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:26), [2 K 17:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:4), [Je 37:15](https://ref.ly/Jer37:15), [37:18](https://ref.ly/Jer37:18), [Is 42:7](https://ref.ly/Isa42:7); בָּֽתֵּי כְלָאִים v:[22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22) (cf. בַּיִת **1** (ε) (2\) p. 109 supr.; v. also כלוא infr.); בִּנְדֵי כִלְאוֺ [2 K 25:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:29) \= [Je 52:33](https://ref.ly/Jer52:33) *garments of imprisonment* \= prison\-garb.

> \[Str4356,4357] † I. מִכְלָה (incorrect for מכלא) **n.\[m.]** enclosure, fold;—צאֹן ´גָּזַר מִמּ Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17); pl. cstr. לקח מִמִּכְלְאֹת צאֹן [Psalm 78:70](https://ref.ly/Ps78:70) (obj. David); pl. sf. לקח מִמִּכְלְאֹתֶיְ [50:9](https://ref.ly/Ps50:9) (obj. he\-goats).—II. מִכְלָה v. sub כלה. p. 479

> \[Str3610] † כִּלְאַיִם **n.\[m.] du.** two kinds (Ar. كِلَانِ, كِلَا *both;* Eth. ክልኤ፡ *two;* perh. also כלאי MI23 *both, two,* NöLCB 1889, 61, cf DrSm xcii; Sm u. SoMI, 1886 suggest *reservoirs;* v. also כְּלִי)—בְּהֶמְתְּ לאֹ ´תַּרְבִּיעַ כ [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) *not cause thy cattle to breed* (in) *two kinds;* ´שָֽדְ לאֹ תִּזְרַע כ v:[19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) (H); לאֹ תִּזְרַע ´כַּרְמְ כ [Dt 22:9](https://ref.ly/Deut22:9) (both כִּלְאָיִם); ´בֶּגֶד כ [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) (H);—only in law of H and D against mixtures.

## כלב (id: BDB04458)

כלב (√ of foll.; meaning unknown; Thes regards כֶּלֶב as onomatop.)

> \[Str3611] † כֶּ֫לֶב **n.m.**:[1 S 24:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:15) dog (NH *id.;* Ph. כלב; Aram. כַּלְבָּא, ܟܰܠܒܳܐ, Ar. كَلْبٌ; Eth. ከልብ፡ As. *kalbu* DlHWB 328\)—abs. ´כ Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5) \+ 16 t., כָּלֶב [Psalm 59:7](https://ref.ly/Ps59:7), [59:15](https://ref.ly/Ps59:15); pl. כְּלָבִים [1 K 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:11) \+ 12 t.; cstr. כַּלְבֵי [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1); sf. כְּלָבֶיְ [Psalm 68:24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24); —*dog:* **a.** lit. [1 K 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:11), [16:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:4), [21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19), [21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19), [21:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:23), [21:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:24), [22:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:38), [2 K 9:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:10), [9:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:36) (all of fierce, hungry dogs, devouring dead bodies and licking blood); cf. [Je 15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3) and (לְשׁוֺן כְּלָבֶיְ) [Psalm 68:24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24); eating torn flesh, [Ex 22:30](https://ref.ly/Exod22:30) (E), כַּלְבֵי צאֹנִי \= *my sheep\-dogs,* only [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1) (in both these with implied inferiority); dog\-sacrifice was a heathen rite [Is 66:3](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3) (Di al.); v. esp. RS Sem. i, 273 (291\), 325 (343\); in various sim. Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), [Pr 26:11](https://ref.ly/Prov26:11), [26:17](https://ref.ly/Prov26:17), [Psalm 59:7](https://ref.ly/Ps59:7), [59:15](https://ref.ly/Ps59:15); in proverbial sayings: לאֹ לִשֹׁנוֺ ´יֶחֱרַץ־כ [Ex 11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7) (v. I. חרץ); מֵת טוֺב מִ־ן ´לְכ הָאַרְיֵה הַמֵּת [Ec 9:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:4). **b.** applied, fig., to men, in contempt [1 S 17:43](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:43), so of psalmist's enemies [Psalm 22:17](https://ref.ly/Ps22:17), [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ps22:21), or in excessive humility [2 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:13); still more emphatically מֵת ´כ *a dead dog,* הַמֵּת ´הַכּ [2 S 9:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:8), [16:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:9); also ´ראֹשׁ כ [3:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:8); כְּלָבִים אִלְּמִים [Is 56:10](https://ref.ly/Isa56:10) (of misleading prophets), עַזֵּי נֶפֶשׁ ´הַכּ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11) (*id.*); ´כ was name given to male temple\-prostitutes [Dt 23:19](https://ref.ly/Deut23:19) (v. Dr; cf. קָדֵשׁ).

> \[Str3612] † כָּלֵב **n.pr.m.** (Nab. n.pr. כלבא Eut (Nö):p. 55\. כלבו VogNab. 6; cf. also WeSkizzen u. Vorarbeiten iii. 217; on כָּלֵב \= *dog\-clan,* v. RSK 17, 190 f., 219, 254\);—son of יְפֻנֶּה (LXX Χαλεβ; cf. LagBN 78\), called הַקְּנִזִּי (v. קְנַז) in JE, [Nu 32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12), [Jos 14:6](https://ref.ly/Josh14:6), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Josh14:14), cf. [15:17](https://ref.ly/Josh15:17) \= Ju [1:13](https://ref.ly/Judg1:13), Ju [3:9](https://ref.ly/Judg3:9) (where קְנַז is brother of ´כ), also [1 Ch 4:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:15); treated as full Judahite only in P [Nu 13:6](https://ref.ly/Num13:6) (v. GFMJu p. 30 f.) the one of the spies who (with Joshua) reported well of Canaan [14:6](https://ref.ly/Num14:6), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Num14:24), [14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30), [14:38](https://ref.ly/Num14:38); also [26:65](https://ref.ly/Num26:65), [34:19](https://ref.ly/Num34:19), [Dt 1:36](https://ref.ly/Deut1:36), [Jos 14:13](https://ref.ly/Josh14:13), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Josh15:13), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Josh15:14), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Josh15:16), [15:18](https://ref.ly/Josh15:18), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Josh21:12) (P\= [1 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:41)) Ju [1:12](https://ref.ly/Judg1:12), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Judg1:14), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Judg1:15), [1:20](https://ref.ly/Judg1:20) (‖ with Jos 15); \= clan of Caleb [1 S 30:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:14) (disting. from Judah). Caleb is called בֶּן־חֶצְרוֺן [1 Ch 2:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:18), cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:19), v:[42](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:42), v:[46](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:46), v:[48](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:48), v:[49](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:49), v:[50](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:50) \= כְּלוּבָי v:[9](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:9); בְּכָלֵב אֶפְרָתָה [1 Ch 2:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:24), rd. perh ´בָּא כ, so LXX Vulgate, cf. KauAT (v. אֶפְרָתָה p. 68 supr.)
