## I כָּלָה (id: BDB04465)

\[Str3615,3607] I. כָּלָה:206 **vb.** be complete, at an end, finished, accomplished, spent (NH *id.*, Pi.; Ph. כלה; As. *kalû, put and end to,* and *cease, vanish* DlHWB 329; late Aram. כְּלָא *cease, perish* (not Targum); on root\-meaning *enclose* cf. כלא and BaZMG 1887, 605\)—† **Qal.** *Pf.* ´כ [1 K 6:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:38) \+; 3 fs. כָּֽלְתָה [1 S 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7) \+; 1 S. כָּלִיתִי [Psalm 39:11](https://ref.ly/Ps39:11); 3 pl. כָּלוּ [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27) \+; 2 mpl. כְּלִיתֶם [Mal 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mal3:6); וּכְלִיתֶם consec. Ez [13:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:14); 1 pl. כָּלִינוּ [Psalm 90:7](https://ref.ly/Ps90:7), etc.; *Impf.* יִכְלֶה [Pr 22:8](https://ref.ly/Prov22:8); juss. יִ֫כֶל [Jb 33:21](https://ref.ly/Job33:21) (v. Dr§ 171–173 Ges§ 109\. 2 b Anm. DaSynt. § 51 R. 5; 64 R. 6\); 3 fs. תִּכְלָה [1 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14); וַתֵּ֫כֶל [Ex 39:32](https://ref.ly/Exod39:32) \+ [2 S 13:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:39) (v. infr.); 3 mpl. יִכְלוּ [Is 1:28](https://ref.ly/Isa1:28) \+, יִכְלָ\+יוּן [Is 31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* כְּלוֺת [Ru 2:23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23) \+, etc.;—**1\. a.** *be complete, at an end,* of a period of time [Gn 41:53](https://ref.ly/Gen41:53) (E; opp. III. חלל Hiph. *begin* v:[54](https://ref.ly/Gen41:54)), [Je 8:20](https://ref.ly/Jer8:20) (‖עבר); so of action or event, with emphasis on time [Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Isa24:13), [Ru 2:23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23), [2 Ch 29:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:28) (opp. הֵחֵל v:[27](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:27)), v:[34](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:34), [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) (‖קֵץ v:[6](https://ref.ly/Dan12:6), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Dan12:9));—vid. also **2 b** infr. **b.** *be completed, finished,* of a work: temple [1 K 6:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:38), [1 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:20), [2 Ch 8:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:16) (‖שָׁלֵם), tabernacle [Ex 39:32](https://ref.ly/Exod39:32) (P). **c.** *be accomplished, fulfilled,* only of purposed wrath, or predicition of ´י, Ez [5:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:13) (‖כַּלּוֺתִי vb, see **Pi.**), [Dn 11:36](https://ref.ly/Dan11:36), [2 Ch 36:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:22) \= [Ezr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:1). **d.** be ideally complete, *be determined,* always in bad sense, *plotted,* subj. הָרָעָה: sq. מֵעִם of agent [1 S 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7), also v:[9](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:9) (\+ לָבוֺא עַל), \+ v:[33](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:33) (rd. כָּֽלְתָה, for MT כָּלָה הִיא, LXX We Dr Kit Bu); הָרָעָה אֶל־אֲדֹנֵינוּ וְעַל־כָּל־בֵּיתוֺ ´כ [25:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:17); sq. מֵאֵת of agent [Est 7:7](https://ref.ly/Esth7:7) (\+ אֵלָיו). **2\. a.** *be spent, used up* (prop. *come to an end*), of water [Gn 21:15](https://ref.ly/Gen21:15) (E), meal [1 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16). **b.** *waste away, be exhausted, fail,* lit. of fading grass [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6) (‖יָבֵשׁ, לאֹ היה), of vintage [32:10](https://ref.ly/Isa32:10) (opp. בּוֺא), of vanishing cloud [Jb 7:9](https://ref.ly/Job7:9) (in sim., ‖הלך); fig. of fleeting days of life v:[6](https://ref.ly/Job7:6) (‖קלל), cf. [Psalm 102:4](https://ref.ly/Ps102:4) and כָּלוּ בְיָגוֺן חַיַּי [Psalm 31:11](https://ref.ly/Ps31:11), [Je 20:18](https://ref.ly/Jer20:18); v. also **1 a** supr.; of flesh [Jb 33:21](https://ref.ly/Job33:21), cf. of vital strength [Psalm 71:9](https://ref.ly/Ps71:9), בָּשָר וּשְׁאֵר [Pr 5:11](https://ref.ly/Prov5:11), ´כ שְׁאֵרִי וּלְבָבִי [Psalm 73:26](https://ref.ly/Ps73:26), subj. רוּחִי [143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7); once of ´י's compassion לאֹ־כָלוּ רַחֲמָיו [La 3:32](https://ref.ly/Lam3:32) (‖תמם, on txt. see VB); esp. of eyes exhausted by weeping [La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11), strained by looking (fig.) for relief or refreshment, *pine, languish* [4:17](https://ref.ly/Lam4:17), [Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4), [Jb 11:20](https://ref.ly/Job11:20), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Job17:5), [Je 14:6](https://ref.ly/Jer14:6) (of wild asses); sq. לְ [La 4:17](https://ref.ly/Lam4:17) and, in spiritual sense, [Psalm 119:82](https://ref.ly/Ps119:82), [119:123](https://ref.ly/Ps119:123); similarly (sq. לְ) of נפשׁ exhausted by *longing* [Psalm 84:3](https://ref.ly/Ps84:3) (‖נכסף), [119:81](https://ref.ly/Ps119:81), כָּלוּ כִלְיֹתַי בְּחֵקִי [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27); so also [2 S 13:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:29) where rd. וַתֵּכֶל רוּחַ הַמֶּלֶח (for MT ´וַתְּכַל דָּוִד הַמּ) *and the spirit of the king pined* to go forth unto Absalom, cf. LXXL We Klo Dr Kit Bu. **c.** *come to an end, vanish* \= *perish, be destroyed,* by ´י's judgment [Is 1:28](https://ref.ly/Isa1:28) (‖שֶׁבֶר), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Isa16:4) (‖אָפֵס, תָּמַם), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Isa29:20) (‖אָפֵס, נִכְרַת), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3) (‖כשׁל, נפל), וּבַחֶרֶב וּבָרָעָב יִכְלוּ [Je 16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4), cf. [44:27](https://ref.ly/Jer44:27) (‖תָּמַם), Ez [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:12) (‖מוּת, נָפַל), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:14), [Mal 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mal3:6), [Psalm 37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20), [37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20); (‖אבד), [Jb 4:9](https://ref.ly/Job4:9) (‖ *id.*), [Psalm 71:13](https://ref.ly/Ps71:13) (‖בּוֺשׁ); hyperbol., of severe discipline [39:11](https://ref.ly/Ps39:11), [90:7](https://ref.ly/Ps90:7) (‖נבהל); of prosperity of a people [Is 21:16](https://ref.ly/Isa21:16); שֵׁבֶט עֶבְרָתוֺ יִכְלֶה [Pr 22:8](https://ref.ly/Prov22:8) *the rod of his wrath perishes* (\> Ew al. *is accomplished,* of God's wrath).—[Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) rd. perh. וְכִכְלוֺת יַד נֹפֵץ עַם קֹדֶשׁ *and when the power of the shatterer of the holy people should come to an end,* so Bev after LXX (for difficult MT וּכְכַלּוֺת נַפֵּץ יַד־עַם־קֹדֶשׁ) †.

## II כלה (id: BDB04475)

II. כלה (√ of foll.; meaning unknown).

> \[Str3629] † \[כִּלְיָה † **n.f.** only pl. kidneys (NH. כִּלְיָה (pl.); Aram. כּוּלְיָא or כּוֺלְיָא (only pl.), ܟܽܘܠܢܳܐ; Eth. ኵሊት፡ LXX οἱ νεφροί);—abs. pl. כְּלָיוֺת [Je 11:20](https://ref.ly/Jer11:20) \+ 5 t.; כְּלָיֹת [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13) \+ 13 t.; cstr. כִּלְיוֺת [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14), [Is 34:6](https://ref.ly/Isa34:6); sf. כִּלְיוֺתַי [Jb 16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3) \+ 2 t.; כִּלְיֹתַי [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27); כִּלְיוֺתָי [Psalm 16:7](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7), [Pr 23:16](https://ref.ly/Prov23:16); כִּלְיֹתָי [Psalm 139:13](https://ref.ly/Ps139:13), [La 3:13](https://ref.ly/Lam3:13); כִּלְיוֺתֵיהֶם [Je 12:2](https://ref.ly/Jer12:2); —*kidneys:* **1\.** lit., as physical organ, **a.** of man, only poet., as created by ´י [Psalm 139:13](https://ref.ly/Ps139:13); as the most sensitive and vital part, in metaph. of one wounded by ´י's arrows [Jb 16:13](https://ref.ly/Job16:13), [La 3:13](https://ref.ly/Lam3:13). **b.** of sacrificial animals, offered as choice part to ´י [Lv 3:4](https://ref.ly/Lev3:4), [3:10](https://ref.ly/Lev3:10), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Lev3:15), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Lev4:9), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Lev7:4), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Lev9:10), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Lev9:19); שְׁתֵּי ´הַכּ [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Exod29:22), [Lv 3:4](https://ref.ly/Lev3:4), [3:10](https://ref.ly/Lev3:10), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Lev3:15), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Lev4:9), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Lev7:4), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Lev8:16), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Lev8:25) (all P); in fig. of sacrif. Edomites חֵלֶב כִּלְיוֺת אֵילִים [Is 34:6](https://ref.ly/Isa34:6); transferred to wheat, חֵלֶב כִּלְיוֺת חִטָּה [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14) *kidney\-fat* (i.e. the choicest, richest) *of wheat*. **2\.** fig., as seat of emotion and affection [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27), [Pr 23:16](https://ref.ly/Prov23:16), [Psalm 16:7](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7), [73:21](https://ref.ly/Ps73:21); קָרוֺב אַתָּה בְּפִיהֶם ´וְרָחוֺק מִכּ [Je 12:2](https://ref.ly/Jer12:2) *near art thou in their mouth, and far from their affections;* hence, as involving character, the obj. of God's examination, alw. ‖לֵב:וָלֵב ´כֹּחֵן כ [Je 11:20](https://ref.ly/Jer11:20) cf. [Psalm 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10), ´חֹקֵר לֵב כֹּחֵן כ [Je 17:10](https://ref.ly/Jer17:10), וָלֵב ´בֹּחֵן צַדִּיק רֹאֶה כ [20:12](https://ref.ly/Jer20:12), וְלִבִּי ´צָרְו֯פָה כ [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2).

> \[Str3618,3623] כַּלָּה, כְּלוּלָה v. sub II. כלל. p. 483

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 8:16; Psalms 16:7; Leviticus 9:19; Psalms 26:2; Isaiah 34:6; Leviticus 3:15; Job 16:3; Leviticus 3:10; Job 16:13; Lamentations 3:13; Deuteronomy 32:14; Jeremiah 17:10; Proverbs 23:16; Jeremiah 20:12; Leviticus 4:9; Leviticus 7:4; Leviticus 8:25; Exodus 29:13; Leviticus 9:10; Leviticus 3:4; Jeremiah 11:20; Job 19:27; Psalms 73:21; Exodus 29:22; Psalms 139:13; Jeremiah 12:2; Psalms 7:10


## כלח (id: BDB04478)

כלח (√ of I. כֶּלַח, mng. dub.; Ar. كَلَحَ is *contract the face, look hard, stern;* كَلَاحِ كُلَاحٌ *a hard year* (though dearth, etc.)).

> \[Str3624] † I. כֶּ֫לַח **n.m.** poss. firm or rugged strength
> 
>  (v. √ supr.), אֱלֵי־קָבֶר ´תָּבוֺא בְכ [Jb 5:26](https://ref.ly/Job5:26) *thou shalt come in firm strength* (with thy body vigorous, powers unimpaired) *unto the grave;* עָלֵימוֺ אָבַד כָּלַךְ [30:2](https://ref.ly/Job30:2) *upon whom vigour has perished*.

> \[Str3625] † II. \[כֶּ֫לַח], כָּלַח **n.pr.loc.** city in Assyria [Gn 10:11](https://ref.ly/Gen10:11), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Gen10:12) \= As. *kalḫu,* SchrCOT ad loc. DlPar 261, mod. *Nimrûd*, at NE. angle of Upper Zab and Tigris (v. Billerbeck u. Jeremias BASiii. 1895, 130 ff.)

> \[Str3626] † כָּל־חֹזֶה **n.pr.m. 1\.** father of one of the builders, Shallun [Ne 3:15](https://ref.ly/Neh3:15). **2\.** a name in Judah [Ne 11:5](https://ref.ly/Neh11:5) (relation to **1** unknown).

> \[Str3627,3631] כְּלִי, כִּלָּיוֺן, etc., v. sub I. כלה. p. 479

> \[Str3629] כִּלְיָה v. sub II. כלה. above

> \[Str3557,3633] כִּלְכֵּל **vb.**, כַּלְכֹּל **n.pr.m.** v. sub כול. p. 465

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 3:15; Genesis 10:12; Job 30:2; Job 5:26; Nehemiah 11:5; Genesis 10:11


## I כָּלַל (id: BDB04485)

\[Str3634] † I. כָּלַל **vb.** complete, perfect (NH *comprehend, include,* Pi. *complete,* כְּלָל *a general rule,* בִּכְלָל *in general;* BAram. And Syr. Shaph. שׁכלל, ܫܰܟܠܶܠ *complete, finish;* As. *kalâli* III. I. 2\. *complete;* Ar. كَلَّ is *be wearied, fatigued;* Aram. כְּלִילָא, ܟܠܻܝܠܴܐ is *a crown,* hence den. אַכְלֵיל, ܟܰܠܶܠ *to crown;* Eth. I. 2 ከለለ፡ *to crown,* አክሊል፡ *a crown,* ተክሊል፡ *crowning* (of bride), *nuptials;* Ar. إِكْلِيلٌ *a crown,* loan\-word (Frä62)):—Ez [27:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:4) בֹּנַיִח כָּֽלְלוּ יָפְיֵח thy builders have *perfected* thy beauty (of Tyre, under fig. of ship), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:11).

> \[Str3605] כֹּל once כּוֺל ([Je 33:8](https://ref.ly/Jer33:8) Kt.), **n.m.** the whole, all (Moab., Ph., *id.;* Aram. כּוֺל, ܟܽܠ; Ar. كُلٌّ; Sab. כל, cf. DHMEpigr. Denk. 36–38; Eth. ኩል፡ As. *kullatu*)—abs. כֹּל, cstr. כֹּל [Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Gen2:16), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Gen2:20) \+ oft., once כֹּ־ל [Psalm 138:2](https://ref.ly/Ps138:2) (v. Ba), but more usu. כָּ־ל (with makk.: without it, † [Psalm 35:10](https://ref.ly/Ps35:10), [Pr 19:7](https://ref.ly/Prov19:7); Köi. 84, 95\); sf. 2 ms. in p. כֻּלָּךְ † [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12); 2 fs. כֻּלֵּךְ † [Is 14:29](https://ref.ly/Isa14:29), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), כֻּלָּךְ † [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1), Ct [4:7](https://ref.ly/Song4:7) (perh. for assonance with accompanying לָךְ, בָּךְ); 3 ms. כֻּלּֽה [2 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:9) (v. Dr) \+ 17 t. (never in Pent.), כֻּלּוֺ [Gn 25:25](https://ref.ly/Gen25:25) \+ 16 t.; 3 fs. כֻּלָּהּ [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10) \+ 15 t., כֻּלָּא † Ez [36:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:5); כֻּלָּנּו (16 t.); כֻּלְּכֶם (18 t.); כֻּלָּם (oft.), כּוּלָּם † [Je 31:34](https://ref.ly/Jer31:34), כֻּלָּהַם † [2 S 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:6) (and prob. [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10) כֻּלְּהֶם); 3 fpl. כֻּלָּ֫נָה † [Gn 42:36](https://ref.ly/Gen42:36), [Pr 31:29](https://ref.ly/Prov31:29); :כֻּלָּֽהְנָה † [1 K 7:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:37):—*the whole,* **1\.** with foll. gen. (as usually) *the whole of,* to be rendered, however, often in our idiom, to avoid stiffness, *all* or *every:* **a.** [Gn 2:2](https://ref.ly/Gen2:2) כָּל־צְבָאָם *the whole of* their host, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen2:13) כָּל־אֶרֶץ כּוּשׁ *the whole of* the land of Kush; כָּל־הַלַּיְלָה *the whole of* the night; כָּל־יִשְרָאֵל *the whole of* Israel \= *all* Israel; [Dt 4:29](https://ref.ly/Deut4:29) בְּכָל־לְבָֽבְ with *the whole of* thy heart \= with *all* thy heart; \+ very oft. With a *plural* noun, usu. determined by the art. or a genitive: [Gn 5:5](https://ref.ly/Gen5:5) כל־ימי אדם *the whole of* (\= *all*) the days of Adam, [37:35](https://ref.ly/Gen37:35) כל־בניו *the whole of* (\= *all*) his sons, [Is 2:2](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2) כל־הגוים *all* the nations; [Gn 43:9](https://ref.ly/Gen43:9) \+ oft. כל הימים \= *continually*. In poetry, however, the noun may remain undetermined, כָּל־יָדַיִם *the whole of* hands \= *every* hand, [Is 13:7](https://ref.ly/Isa13:7), [Jer 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37), Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12); כל־פנים i.e. *every* face [Is 25:8](https://ref.ly/Isa25:8), [Joel 2:6](https://ref.ly/Joel2:6); כל שׁלחנות [Is 28:8](https://ref.ly/Isa28:8); כל חוצות [51:20](https://ref.ly/Isa51:20), [La 2:19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19) al. Before an inf. † [Gn 30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41), [Dt 4:7](https://ref.ly/Deut4:7), [1 K 8:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:52), [1 Ch 23:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:31). Freq. with sfs., as כֻּלּוֺ (כֻּלּֽה) *the whole of him* [Gn 25:25](https://ref.ly/Gen25:25), [Jb 21:23](https://ref.ly/Job21:23), Ct [5:16](https://ref.ly/Song5:16), *the whole of it* [Lv 13:13](https://ref.ly/Lev13:13), [Je 2:21](https://ref.ly/Jer2:21), [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1), [Pr 24:31](https://ref.ly/Prov24:31); כֻּלָּהּ *the whole of it* [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10), [Ex 19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18), [25:36](https://ref.ly/Exod25:36), [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8); כֻּלָּךְ *all of thee* Ct [4:7](https://ref.ly/Song4:7) \+ (v. ad init.); כֻּלָּנוּ *the whole of us* [Gn 42:11](https://ref.ly/Gen42:11), [Dt 5:3](https://ref.ly/Deut5:3), [Is 53:6](https://ref.ly/Isa53:6), [53:6](https://ref.ly/Isa53:6); כֻּלְּכֶם [Dt 1:22](https://ref.ly/Deut1:22), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Deut4:4), [1 S 22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7), [22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7); כֻּלָּם [Gn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6), [43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34), [Jos 8:24](https://ref.ly/Josh8:24) ויפלו כֻּלָּם, Ju [11:6](https://ref.ly/Judg11:6) בְּיַד כֻּלָּם, [Is 7:19](https://ref.ly/Isa7:19), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3) \+ oft.—Twice, strangely, with hyperb. intensive force, [Psalm 39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6) כָּל־הֶבֶל *the whole of* vanity are all men (? om. כל, as v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12)), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14) כָּל־כְּבוּדָּה *the whole of* gloriousness is the king's daughter. **b.** followed often by a singular, to be understood *collectively,* whether with or without the art.: [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21) את כל נפשׁ החיה *the whole of* living souls \= every living soul, [2:9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9) כל עץ נחמד למראה *the whole of* trees (every kind of tree) pleasant to view, [6:12](https://ref.ly/Gen6:12) \+ כָּל־בָּשָר, [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14) כל צפור כל כנף *all* birds of *every kind of* wing (so Ez [17:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:23)), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:21) כל האדם the whole of mankind (so [Nu 12:3](https://ref.ly/Num12:3), [16:29](https://ref.ly/Num16:29), Ju [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17) al.); poet. כל־אדם [Psalm 39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6), [64:10](https://ref.ly/Ps64:10) \+; [1 S 14:52](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:52) כל אישׁ גבור וכל־בן־חיל, [17:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:19), [17:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:24) כל אישׁ ישראל, [22:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:2); [Is 9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16) כל פה *the whole of* mouths \= every mouth, [15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [24:10](https://ref.ly/Isa24:10) כָּל־בַּיִת \+ oft. (in [2:12](https://ref.ly/Isa2:12)–16 the sg. and pl. interchange); [Psalm 7:12](https://ref.ly/Ps7:12) \+ בכל־יום, [10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5) \+ בכל־עת \= at *all* seasons. So כל־העץ [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29), כל־הבן [Ex 1:22](https://ref.ly/Exod1:22) \= *all* the sons, כל־המקום [20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24), [Dt 11:24](https://ref.ly/Deut11:24) \= *all* the places, כל־המרכב [Lv 15:9](https://ref.ly/Lev15:9), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Lev15:26), [Dt 4:3](https://ref.ly/Deut4:3) כל־האישׁ אשׁר \= all the *men* who… , [15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19) כל־הבכור, [Je 4:29](https://ref.ly/Jer4:29) כל־העיר עזובה *all* the cities (notice the foll. בָּהֶן); כל־היום \= *all* the days (v. יוֺם **7 f**), etc. In late Heb. extended to such phrases as בְּכָל־דּוֺר וָדוֺר † [Psalm 45:18](https://ref.ly/Ps45:18), [145:13](https://ref.ly/Ps145:13), [Est 9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28); בכל־עיר ועיר † [2 Ch 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:12), [28:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:25), [31:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:19), [Est 8:11](https://ref.ly/Esth8:11), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Esth8:17), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28); † [2 Ch 32:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:28), [Est 2:11](https://ref.ly/Esth2:11), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Esth3:14), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Esth4:3), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Esth8:13), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Esth8:17), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Esth9:21), [9:27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28) (cf. וְ **1 i** b). **c.** the gen. after כל is oft. a rel. sentence, introduced by אשׁר: [Gn 1:31](https://ref.ly/Gen1:31) את כל אשׁר עשה *the whole* of what he had made, [7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Gen13:1) \+ very oft. Sts., with a prep., כל אשׁר has the force of *wheresover, whithersoever,* as [Jos 1:7](https://ref.ly/Josh1:7) בְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵךְ *wheresoever* thou goest, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Josh1:16) אֶל־כָּל־אֲשֶׁר *whithersoever* (see אֲשֶׁר **4 b** γ). Very rarely in such cases is there ellipse of the rel., as [Gn 39:4](https://ref.ly/Gen39:4) וְכָל־יֶשׁ־לוֺ נָתַן בְּיָדִי (contrast v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Gen39:8)), [Ex 9:4](https://ref.ly/Exod9:4) מכל־לבני ישראל, [Is 38:16](https://ref.ly/Isa38:16) וּלְכָּל־בָּהֶן חַיֵּי רוּחִי, [Psalm 71:18](https://ref.ly/Ps71:18) לְכָל־יָבוֺא ([74:3](https://ref.ly/Ps74:3), v. **2 a**), [2 Ch 32:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:31); peculiarly also in Chr (DrIntr. 505\), [1 Ch 29:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:3) מִכָּל־הֲכִינוֺתִי, [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18) f., [Ezr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:6); cf. with כֹּל (**2 a**) [1 Ch 29:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:11) a, [2 Ch 30:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:17), [Ezr 1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:5). **d.** with a *suffix* two idiomatic uses of כל have to be noticed: (*a*) כל is often made more independent and emphatic by being placed with a suffix *after* the word which it qualifies, to which it then stands in apposition (cf. in Syr., Ar., Eth.), as [2 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:9) יִשְרָאֵל כֻּלּהֹ, [Jer 13:19](https://ref.ly/Jer13:19), [48:31](https://ref.ly/Jer48:31), [Is 9:8](https://ref.ly/Isa9:8), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Isa14:29), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31) פלשׁת כלך Philistia, *all of* thee ! [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12), Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6), [Jb 34:13](https://ref.ly/Job34:13), [Psalm 67:4](https://ref.ly/Ps67:4), [67:6](https://ref.ly/Ps67:6); esp. in Ezek., as [14:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:5), [29:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:2) מצרים כֻּלָּהּ [32:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:12), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30); with change of person (cf. the idiom in [Is 22:16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16), [48:1](https://ref.ly/Isa48:1), [54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1) etc.), [1 K 22:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:28) \= [Mi 1:2](https://ref.ly/Mic1:2) שׁמעו עמים כלם Hear, nations, *all of* them ! [Mal 3:9](https://ref.ly/Mal3:9) הַגּוֺי כֻּלּוֺ. So even with כל preceding: [Nu 16:3](https://ref.ly/Num16:3) כָּל־הָעֵדָה כֻּלָּם, [Is 14:18](https://ref.ly/Isa14:18), [Jer 30:16](https://ref.ly/Jer30:16), Ez [11:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:15) כל בית ישראל כֻּלּֽה *the whole of* the house of Israel, *the whole of* it (so [20:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:40), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:10)), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:15), [36:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:5), [Psalm 8:8](https://ref.ly/Ps8:8) (cf. Sab. DHMl. c.); (*b*) with the sf. of 3 ms., understood as referring to the *mass* of things or persons meant, כֻּלּֽה or כֻּלּוֺ, lit. *the whole of it,* is equivalent to *all of them, every one,* † [Ex 14:7](https://ref.ly/Exod14:7) and captains עַל־כֻּלּוֺ upon *the whole of it* (the רֶכֶב collectively) \= *all of them,* [Is 1:23](https://ref.ly/Isa1:23) *the whole of* it (the people) loveth bribes, [9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3), [Jer 6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Jer8:6), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Jer8:10), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Jer8:10), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Jer20:7), [Hab 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hab1:9), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Hab1:15), [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9) and in his temple כֻּלּוֺ אֹמֵר כָּבוֺד *the whole of* it (\= every one there) says, Glory ! [53:4](https://ref.ly/Ps53:4) (‖ [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:3) הַכֹּל); perh. [Is 16:7](https://ref.ly/Isa16:7), [Je 48:38](https://ref.ly/Jer48:38); \+ [Pr 19:6](https://ref.ly/Prov19:6) Ew Hi (וְכֻלּהֹ רֵעַ): [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10) rd. כֻּלְּהֶם קִלֲלוּנִי. **e.** Heb. idiom in certain cases affirms, or denies, of an *entire* class, where Engl. idiom affirms, or denies, of an *individual* of the class; thus in a compar. or hypoth. sentence כל is \= *any,* and with a neg. \= *none:* (*a*) [Gn 3:1](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1) the serpent was more subtil מכל חית השדה than *all* beasts of the field (in our idiom: than *any* beast of the field), [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), [1 S 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:2); (*b*) [Lv 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2) a soul when it sins through ignorance י“מכל מצות י in *all* the commandments of Jehovah (\= in *any* of the commandments, etc.), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Lev19:23) when ye…plant כל־עץ מאכל \= *any* tree for food, [Nu 35:22](https://ref.ly/Num35:22) or if he have cast upon him כָּל־כְּלִי \= *any* weapon, [1 K 8:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:37) b; joined with a ptcp. in a hypoth. sense (Dr§ 121 n. Ges§ 116\. 5 R. 5\), [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) כל מצאי *all my finders* (\= if any one find me), he will slay me, v:[15](https://ref.ly/Gen4:15) a, [Nu 21:8](https://ref.ly/Num21:8) כָּל־הַנָּשׁוּךְ \= *whosoever* (\= if *any one*) is bitten, [1 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:13); (*c*) with a neg., [Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5) *all* plants of the field טֶרֶם יִהְיֶה were not as yet \= no plant of the field as yet was, [4:15](https://ref.ly/Gen4:15) b לבלתי הכות־אתו כל־מצאו for the not\-smiting him of all finding him \= that none finding him should smite him, [Ex 10:15](https://ref.ly/Exod10:15) ולא־נותר כל ירק \= and no green things were left, [12:16](https://ref.ly/Exod12:16) כל־מלאכה לא יעשה *all* work shall not be done \= no work shall be done, [Dt 28:14](https://ref.ly/Deut28:14), Ju [13:4](https://ref.ly/Judg13:4) אַל־תּאֹכְלִי כָּל־טָמֵא eat not of *all that is* unclean, [19:19](https://ref.ly/Judg19:19) אֵין מַחְסוֺר כָּל־דָּבָר there is no lack of *all things* i.e. of *any thing,* [Psalm 143:2](https://ref.ly/Ps143:2) כי לא־יצדק לפניך כל־חי, \+ very oft. (so οὐ πᾶς, as a Hebraism, in the N. T., e.g. [Mk 13:20](https://ref.ly/Mark13:20) οὐκ ἄν ἐσώθη πᾶσα σάρξ, [Lk 1:37](https://ref.ly/Luke1:37) οὐκ ἀδυνατήσει…πᾶν ῥῆμα, as [Jer 32:17](https://ref.ly/Jer32:17) לאֹ־יִפָּלֵא מִמְּ כָּל־דָּבָר, [Gal 2:16](https://ref.ly/Gal2:16) οὐ δικαιωθήσεται…πᾶσα σάρξ, etc.) Usu., in such cases, כל (or its gen.) is without the art., being left purposely indef.: in [Psalm 49:18](https://ref.ly/Ps49:18) (**2 b** *a*) הַכֹּל is emph. (In [Nu 23:13](https://ref.ly/Num23:13) וְכֻלּוֺ לאֹ תִרְאֶה the context shews that כֹּל is opp. to a part). **f.** very anomalously, severed from its gen., † [2 S 1:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:9) כִּי־כָל־עוֺד נַפְשִׁי בִי, [Jb 27:3](https://ref.ly/Job27:3) כִּי־כָל־עוֺד נִשְׁמָתִי בִי, [Ho 14:3](https://ref.ly/Hos14:3) (si vera l.) כָּל־תִּשָּא עָוֺן. On [Ec 5:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:15) ´כָּל־עֻמַּת־שֶ v. עֻמָּה.
> 
>  *Note*.—When the gen. after כל is a noun fem. or pl., the pred. usu. agrees with this (as being the really important idea), e.g. [Gn 5:5](https://ref.ly/Gen5:5) ויהיו כל ימי אדם, [Nu 14:1](https://ref.ly/Num14:1) וַתִּשָּא כָל־הָעֵדָה, [Nah 3:1](https://ref.ly/Nah3:1), [Psalm 150:6](https://ref.ly/Ps150:6) כָֹּל הַנְּשָׁמָה תְּהַלֵּל exceptions being very rare, [Is 64:10](https://ref.ly/Isa64:10) b, [Pr 16:2](https://ref.ly/Prov16:2) (Ges§ 141\. 1 R. 2\). **2\.** Absolutely: † **a.** without the art., *all things, all* (mostly neuter, but sts. m.), the sense in which ‘ all ’ is to be taken being gathered fr. the context, [Gn 9:3](https://ref.ly/Gen9:3) נתתי לכם את כֹּל, [16:12](https://ref.ly/Gen16:12) וְיַד כֹּל בּוֺ, [20:16](https://ref.ly/Gen20:16) ואת כֹּל ונוכחת, [33:11](https://ref.ly/Gen33:11) וכי ישׁ לי כֹל, [Nu 8:16](https://ref.ly/Num8:16) בכור כֹּל מבני ישראל, [11:6](https://ref.ly/Num11:6) אֵין כֹּל *nought of all things !* \= *there is nothing* (so † [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3), [Pr 13:7](https://ref.ly/Prov13:7), cf. [2 K 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:2)), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Num13:2) כֹּ֭ל נשיא בהם (cf. [2 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:28), [1 Ch 3:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:9): usu. so הַכֹּל), [Dt 28:47](https://ref.ly/Deut28:47) מֵרֹב כֹּל, v:[48](https://ref.ly/Deut28:48), v:[37](https://ref.ly/Deut28:37) בְּחֹסֶר כֹּל (cf. [Je 44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18)), [Is 30:5](https://ref.ly/Isa30:5) כֹּל הֹבִאישׁ *all* exhibit shame, [44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24) עשֶֹׁה־כֹּל ´י, [Je 44:12](https://ref.ly/Jer44:12) וְתַמּוּ כֹל (unusual), [Zp 1:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:2), [Psalm 8:7](https://ref.ly/Ps8:7), [74:3](https://ref.ly/Ps74:3) (rd. כֹּל הֵרַע), [145:15](https://ref.ly/Ps145:15) עיני כֹל, [Pr 16:4](https://ref.ly/Prov16:4), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Prov26:10), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Prov28:5), [Jb 13:1](https://ref.ly/Job13:1) הן כֹּל ראתה עיני, [42:2](https://ref.ly/Job42:2), [1 Ch 29:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:11) b, [2 Ch 32:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:22) (m.), [Dn 11:37](https://ref.ly/Dan11:37) (v. also **1 c** *end*); מִכֹּל [Gn 6:19](https://ref.ly/Gen6:19), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Gen6:20) b שְׁנַיִם מִכֹּל, [14:20](https://ref.ly/Gen14:20), [27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33), [Je 17:9](https://ref.ly/Jer17:9) עקוב הלב מִכֹּל, [Dn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Dan11:2) (m.) After a neg. \= *anything*, [Dt 4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25) תְּמוּנַת כֹּל the likeness of *anything,* [8:9](https://ref.ly/Deut8:9), [28:55](https://ref.ly/Deut28:55), [Pr 30:30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30). In the gen. also, very rarely, to express the idea of *all* as comprehensively as possible: Ez [44:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:30) כָּל־בִּכּוּרֵי כֹל וְכָל־תְּרוּמַת כֹּל; [Psalm 119:128](https://ref.ly/Ps119:128) (si vera l.) כָּל־פִּקּוּדֵי כֹל all the statutes *about everthing*. † **b.** with art. הַכֹּל: (*a*) where the sense is limited by the context to things (or persons) just mentioned, [Ex 29:24](https://ref.ly/Exod29:24) ושמת הַכֹּל ביד אהרן, [Lv 1:9](https://ref.ly/Lev1:9) והקטיר הכהן את הַכֹּל, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Lev1:13), [8:27](https://ref.ly/Lev8:27), [Dt 2:36](https://ref.ly/Deut2:36) לפנינו ´את הַכֹּל נתן י, [Jos 11:19](https://ref.ly/Josh11:19) (cf. [2 S 19:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:31), [1 K 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:26) \= [2 Ch 12:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:9)), [21:43](https://ref.ly/Josh21:43) הַכֹּל בָּא (cf. [7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33), [2 K 25:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:17) \= [Je 52:22](https://ref.ly/Jer52:22)), [2 K 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:16) הַכֹּל גבורים, [Is 65:8](https://ref.ly/Isa65:8) לבלתי השׁחית הַכֹּל, [Psalm 14:3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:3); or implied, [Gn 16:12](https://ref.ly/Gen16:12) יָרוֺ בַכֹּל, [24:1](https://ref.ly/Gen24:1) ברך את אברהם בַּכֹּל [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5) (poet.) עֲרֻכָה בַכֹּל, [Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11) (peculiarly) חָזוּת הַכֹּל the vision *of the whole,* [Je 13:7](https://ref.ly/Jer13:7), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Jer13:10) לא יצלח לַכֹּל, Ez [7:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:14) וְהָכִין הַכֹּל (but Co וְהָכִינוּ הָכֵן), [Psalm 49:18](https://ref.ly/Ps49:18) לא במותו יקח הַכֹּל: more freq. later, viz. [1 Ch 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:5) (as regards *all*), [28:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:19), [29:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:19), [2 Ch 28:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:6), [29:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:28), [31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5), [35:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:7), [36:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:17), [36:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:18), [Ezr 1:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:11), [2:42](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:42), [8:34](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:34), [8:35](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:35), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:17) (וַיְכַלּוּ בַכֹּל: v. BeRy), [Ec 5:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:8) (בַּכֹּל, appar. \= *in all respects*), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:19), [12:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:13). (*b*) in a wider sense, *all,* whether of all mankind or of all living things, the universe (τὸ πᾶν), or of all the circumstances of life (chiefly late), [Je 10:16](https://ref.ly/Jer10:16) \= [51:19](https://ref.ly/Jer51:19) כי יוצר הַכֹּל הוא, [Psalm 103:19](https://ref.ly/Ps103:19) (cf. [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12)), [119:21](https://ref.ly/Ps119:21) הַכֹּל עֲבָדֶיְ, [145:9](https://ref.ly/Ps145:9) לַכֹּל ´טוֺב י, [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12), [29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14), [29:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:16), [Dn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Dan11:2), and esp. in Ec., as [1:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:2), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:14), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:17), [3:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:19), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:8) הַכֹּל הֶבֶל, [2:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:16) הכל נשׁכח, [3:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:1) לַכֹּל זְמָן, v:[11](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:11), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:19), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:20), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:6), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:15), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:1), [9:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:2), [9:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:2), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:3), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:3), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:19), [11:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:5). כַּכֹּל, † [Jb 24:24](https://ref.ly/Job24:24) (si vera l.) כַּכֹּל יִקָָּֽפְצוּן like *all* men (i.e. like men in general).

> \[Str3632] † כָּלִיל **adj.** and **subst.** entire, whole, holocaust, cstr. כְּלִיל, fem. constr. כְּלִילַת:—**1\. adj.** Ez [16:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:14) (of Jerus.) the report בְּיָפְיֵח כִּי כָּלִיל הוּא, [28:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:12) :כְּלִיל יֹֽפִי (of the king of Tyre) *entire, perfect* in beauty; [27:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:3) :אֲנִי כְּלִילַת יֹֽפִי (of Tyre), [La 2:15](https://ref.ly/Lam2:15) (of Jerus.) הֲזאֹת הָעִיר שֶׁיּאֹמְרוּ כְּלִילַת יֹ֔פִי. **2\. subst. a.** *entirety, whole,* [Ex 28:31](https://ref.ly/Exod28:31) thou shalt make the robe of the ephod כְּלִיל תְּכֵלֶת a *whole* of purple (i.e. wholly purple); so [39:22](https://ref.ly/Exod39:22); [Nu 4:6](https://ref.ly/Num4:6) בֶּגֶד כְּלִיל תְּכֵלֶת; Ju [20:40](https://ref.ly/Judg20:40) והנה עלה כְּלִיל־הָעִיר השׁמימה the *whole* of the city (perh. with allusion to mng. **b**); as adv. acc., [Is 2:18](https://ref.ly/Isa2:18) וְהָאֱלִילִים כָּלִיל יַחֲלֽף will pass away *in entirety, wholly*. **b.** as a sacrif. term, *entire\-* or *whole*\-offering, *holocaust,* of a sacrifice consumed *wholly* on the altar (cf. Ph. כלל CISI. i, 165, 167; RSRel. Sem. 237\), usu. a descriptive syn. of עוֺלָה: [Dt 33:10](https://ref.ly/Deut33:10) יָשִימוּ קְטוֺרָה בְּאַפְֶּ֔ :וְכָלִיל עַל מִזְכְּחֶֽן, [Psalm 51:21](https://ref.ly/Ps51:21) אז תחפץ זבחי צדק עוֺלָה וְכָלִיל, in app. [1 S 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:9) ויעלה עוֺלָה כָּלִיל ליהוה; of the priests ’ מִנְחָה, [Lv 6:15](https://ref.ly/Lev6:15) ליהוה כָּלִיל תָּקְטָֽר, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev6:16) וְכָל־מִנְחַת כֹּהֵן כָּלִיל תִּהְיֶה לאֹ תֵאָכֵל; fig, [Dt 13:17](https://ref.ly/Deut13:17) (of idol. city) ושרפת באשׁ את־העיר ואת־כל שׁללהּ אלהיך ´כָּלִיל לי and thou shalt burn the city and all its spoil as *a whole\-offering to* ´י.

> \[Str4358] † מִכְלוֺל **n.m.** perfection, i.e. (prob.) gorgeous attire, Ez [23:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:12), [38:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:4) (of warriors) לְבֻשֵׁי מִכְלוֺל.

> \[Str4360] † \[מַכְלֻל] **n.m.** a thing made perfect, i.e. (prob.) gorgeous garment (or stuff), Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24) ´הֵמָּה רֹכְלַיִח בְּמַכְלֻלִים בִּגְלֽמֵי תְּכֵלֶת וְרִקְמָה וג.

> \[Str4359] † מִכְלָל **n.m.** completeness, perfection, [Psalm 50:2](https://ref.ly/Ps50:2) מִצִּיּוֺן מִכְלַל \+יֹפִי out of Zion, the *perfection* of beauty (cf. כָּלִיל **1**), hath God shined.

## II כלל (id: BDB04491)

II. כלל (assumed as √ of foll., but dubious; cf NH כַּלָּה; Aram. כַּלְּתָא, ܟܰܠܬܳܐ; Ar. كَنَّةٌ As. *kallâtu,* expl. by DlProl. 130 f. (cf. IdHWB 330\) as prop. *closed bridal chamber* (ideogr. \= *closed chamber*), fr. √ כלא or כלה, thence *bride* (cf. *harem*); acc. to RSK 136 f. 292 כַּלָּה \= *one closed in,* or *reserved* (sc. for her husband); Ges al. *one crowned* (cf. Aram. etc. כלילא *crown*), but form much against this; NöZMG 1886, 737 ‘ ventures no explanation ’).

> \[Str3618] † כַּלָּה **n.f.** daughter\-in\-law, bride;—abs. ´כ [Je 2:32](https://ref.ly/Jer2:32) \+ 14 t.; sf. כַּלָּתוֺ [Gn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Gen11:31) \+ 5 t.; כַּלָּתָהּ [Ru 1:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:22) \+ 2 t., etc.; pl. sf. כַּלּֽתֶיהָ [Ru 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:6), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:8), כַּלּוֺתֶיהָ [1:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:7), כַּלּוֺתֵיהֶם [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14); —**1\.** *daughter\-in\-law,* in ref. to husband's father [Gn 38:11](https://ref.ly/Gen38:11), [38:16](https://ref.ly/Gen38:16), [38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24) (J), cf. [1 Ch 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:4), [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), Ez [22:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:11), [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6), [Gn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Gen11:31) (P), [Lv 18:15](https://ref.ly/Lev18:15), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Lev20:12) (both H); husband's mother [Ru 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:7), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:8), [1:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:22), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:20), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:22), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:15). **2\.** *bride,* usu. **a.** just before marriage [Is 49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18), [61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10), [62:5](https://ref.ly/Isa62:5), [Je 2:32](https://ref.ly/Jer2:32) (all in sim., etc.), ‖חָתָן [7:34](https://ref.ly/Jer7:34), [16:9](https://ref.ly/Jer16:9), [25:10](https://ref.ly/Jer25:10), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Jer33:11), [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); Ct [4:8](https://ref.ly/Song4:8), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Song4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Song4:11), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1). **b.** also just after marriage \= *young wife* [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14); rd. הַכַּלָּה likewise [2 S 17:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:3) (for MT הַכֹּל), LXX We Dr Klo Kit Bu.

> \[Str3623] † \[כְּלוּלָה] **n.f.** betrothal;—only pl. כְּלוּלֽתָיִח (cf. *‘ espousals ’*) [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2) *thy betrothal\-time* (‖נְעוּרַיִח).

> \[Str3636] † כְּלָלָ **n.pr.m.** one of those who took strange wives [Ezr 10:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:30), LXX Χαηλ, A Χαληλ, LXXL Χαλαμαναι.

## כָּלַם (id: BDB04495)

\[Str3637] † \[כָּלַם] **vb.** only **Niph. Hoph.** be humiliated, **Hiph.** humiliate (NH Hiph., *id.;* Aram. כְּלַם Aph. Ithpe.; Ar. كَلَمَ is *wound;* كَلَّمَ also *speak to, converse with*);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 2 fs. וְנִכְלַמְתְּ consec. [Je 22:22](https://ref.ly/Jer22:22) \+ 2 t.; 1 S. נִכְלַמְתִּי [Je 31:19](https://ref.ly/Jer31:19), [Ezr 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:6), נִכְלָמְתִּי [Is 50:7](https://ref.ly/Isa50:7); 3 pl. נִכְלְמוּ [Is 45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּכָּלֵם [Nu 12:14](https://ref.ly/Num12:14); 2 fs. תִּכָּֽלְמִי [Is 54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4); 3 mpl. יִכָּֽלְמוּ [Is 41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11) \+ 5 t.; 2 mpl. תִּכָּֽלְמוּ [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17); *Imv.* mpl. הִכָּֽלְמוּ Ez [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32); *Inf. cstr.* הִכָּלֵם [Je 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jer3:3), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12); *Pt.* נִכְלָם [Psalm 74:21](https://ref.ly/Ps74:21); mpl. נִכְלָמִים [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5) \+ 2 t.; fpl. נִכְלָמוֺת Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27); —*be humiliated, ashamed, put to shame, dishonoured, confounded:* **1\.** *be humiliated, ashamed,* before men [Nu 12:14](https://ref.ly/Num12:14) (E), [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5) \= [1 Ch 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:5), [2 S 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:4), cf. [Psalm 74:21](https://ref.ly/Ps74:21); before enemies (by defeat, etc.) [Is 45:17](https://ref.ly/Isa45:17) (‖בושׁ), [50:7](https://ref.ly/Isa50:7), [54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4) (‖בושׁ, חפר); before God, sq. לְ inf., וְנִכְלַמְתִּי אֱלֽהַי לְהָרִים פָּנַי אֵלֶיְ [Ezr 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:6) (‖בושׁ); [Je 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jer3:3), Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27), [16:54](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:54) (‖נָשָא כְלִמָּה), [43:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:10), [43:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:11) (all \+ מִן caus.) [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61), so prob. Levites, at Hezekiah's reforms [2 Ch 30:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:5). **2\.** *be put to shame, dishonoured, confounded,* by judgments of ´י, all ‖בושׁ: [Je 22:22](https://ref.ly/Jer22:22) (מִן of cause), Ez [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32) (*id.*), [Je 31:19](https://ref.ly/Jer31:19) (כִּי of cause), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12), [Is 41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11), [45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) (\+ הָלַח בַּכְּלִמָּה), [Psalm 35:4](https://ref.ly/Ps35:4), [69:7](https://ref.ly/Ps69:7); \+ חפר [Psalm 40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15), [70:3](https://ref.ly/Ps70:3). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הִכְּלִמוֺ [1 S 20:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:34); 3 mpl. sf. הֶכְלַמְנוּם (Ges§ 53, 3 R. 6\) [1 S 25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7); *Impf.* יַכְלִים [Pr 28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7); 2 ms. sf. וַתַּכְלִימֵנוּ [Psalm 44:10](https://ref.ly/Ps44:10); 2 mpl. sf. תַּכְלִימוּנִי [Jb 19:3](https://ref.ly/Job19:3), ־מוּהָ [Ru 2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15); *Inf. cstr.* הַכְלִים [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15), [Pr 25:8](https://ref.ly/Prov25:8); *Pt.* מַכְלִים Ju [18:7](https://ref.ly/Judg18:7) (but v. infr.), מַכְלִם [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3); —**1\.** *put to shame* \= *insult, humiliate,* c. acc. [1 S 20:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:34), [25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7) (cf. Hoph. v:[15](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:15)) [Ru 2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15), [Jb 19:3](https://ref.ly/Job19:3); *humiliate* by rebuke [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3); *humiliate* by defeat [Pr 25:8](https://ref.ly/Prov25:8), [Psalm 44:10](https://ref.ly/Ps44:10); *cause shame to* [Pr 28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7); — Ju [18:7](https://ref.ly/Judg18:7) is crpt. (see Be VB GFM; Be prop. מַחְסוֺר כל־דבר there was no *lack of anything,* for MT ´מַכְלִים ד; GFM conjectures מְכַלֵּא מִדָּבָר *there is no one to restrain* (us) *from anything* in the land). **2\.** *exhibit shame* [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15) (‖בוֺשׁ). **Hoph.** *Pf.* **1\.** 1 pl. לאֹ הָכְלַמְנוּ [1 S 25:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:15) *we were not insulted, humiliated* (cf. **Hiph. 1**). **2\.** 3 pl. חָכְלְמוּ [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3) *they were put to shame, dishonoured, confounded* (‖בושׁ).

> \[Str3639] † כְּלִמָּה **n.f.** insult, reproach, ignominy;—abs. ´כ [Is 45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) \+ 9 t.; cstr. כְּלִמַּת [Je 20:11](https://ref.ly/Jer20:11) \+ 3 t.; sf. כְּלִמָּתִי [Jb 20:3](https://ref.ly/Job20:3) \+ 3 t., etc., pl. כְּלִמּוֺת [Mi 2:6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6), [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6); —**1\.** specif., *insult, reproach,* ´לאֹ יִסַּג כ [Mi 2:6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6) *reproaches do not cease;* מוּסַר כְּלִמָּתִי [Jb 20:3](https://ref.ly/Job20:3) \= *my beshaming* (*insulting*) *correction,* i.e. the correction which insults me. **2\.** in gen., *reproach, ignominy,* opp. כָּבוֺד [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3); ´הלך בַּכּ [Is 45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16) *go into ignominy* (\+ בּוֺשׁ, נִכְלַם); oft. ‖בּשֶׁת [Is 30:3](https://ref.ly/Isa30:3), [61:7](https://ref.ly/Isa61:7), [Psalm 44:16](https://ref.ly/Ps44:16), subj. of כִּסָּה [Je 3:25](https://ref.ly/Jer3:25) *our ignomity covereth us,* so under fig. of garment, after לָבַשׁ [Psalm 35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26) (\+ בושׁ, חפר in va), [109:29](https://ref.ly/Ps109:29); ‖חֶרְפָּה [Psalm 69:8](https://ref.ly/Ps69:8) (subj. of כִּסְּתָה פָּ\+ני), [Je 51:51](https://ref.ly/Jer51:51) (*id.;* בושׁ in ‖ cl.), [Psalm 71:13](https://ref.ly/Ps71:13) (\+ בושׁ in ‖ cl.); ‖בּשֶׁת \+ חֶרְפָּה [69:20](https://ref.ly/Ps69:20); ‖אִוֶּלֶת [Pr 18:13](https://ref.ly/Prov18:13); ‖רֹק [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6); עוֺלָם ´כ [Je 20:11](https://ref.ly/Jer20:11) (בושׁ in ‖ cl.); כְּלִמָּתֵח Ez [16:63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63) (*id.* ‖); oft. in Ezek. ´נשא כ *bear ignominy*, Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52), [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (‖בושׁ), v:[54](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:54) (נִכְלַם in cl.); [32:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:24), [32:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:25), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30) (all \+ אֶת־יוֺרְדֵי בוֺר *with those who go down to the pit;* ref. to ignominious death), [36:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:7), [39:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:26) (si vera 1\. \= *bear the humiliating sense* of underserved kindness from ´י; but txt. dub., Hi Co, q.v., \[´כ] נשׁו; Sm Da defend); נאש כְּלִמַּת הַגּוֺיִם, i.e. caused by the nations, 34:39, [36:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:6), also v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:15) (‖חֶרְפַּת עַמִּים), prob. also [Psalm 89:51](https://ref.ly/Ps89:51) (rd. כְּלִמַּת, cf. VB Che Bae); כְּלִמָּתָם יְתוֺעֲבוֺתָם אֲשֶׁר עָשוּ ´וְנ Ez [44:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:13).

SchrCOT; but txt. Dub. v. Co; Targum ומדי, whence MezStadt Ḥarran 34 כָּל־מָדַי *all Media;* JKi Hi Co כְּלִמּוּד (רְכֻלָּתֵח) Asshur was *as thine apprentice* (v. sub למד) *in trading;* but sense not very prob.

H, so Norzi; Baer כַּלְֶ\+נה) **n.pr.loc.** in Babylonia, [Gn 10:10](https://ref.ly/Gen10:10) (J), LXX Χαλαννη; DlPa 225 prop. identify. with Bab. *Kul\-unu* \= *Zirlab* (conquered by Sargon in 710: COT:[Gn 10:10](https://ref.ly/Gen10:10): [Am 6:2](https://ref.ly/Amos6:2)), but dubious, and site of Zirlab unknown.

Ez [27:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23), LXX om. in [Am 6:2](https://ref.ly/Amos6:2), Χαλαννη [Is 10:9](https://ref.ly/Isa10:9); perh. \= Kullani (WklGesch. Bab. 225\) i.e. (TomkinsPSBA Jan. 1883, 61\) Kullanhou, near Aleppo, conquered by Tiglath\-Pileser III in 738 (COTii, 195\); or (Di) Kunulua (KG217 KBi, 107\), SE. of Antioch (cf. Dr:[Am 6:2](https://ref.ly/Amos6:2)).

> \[Str3643] כמוהם [Je 41:17](https://ref.ly/Jer41:17) Kt, v. כִּמְהָם.

> \[Str3645] † כְּמוֺשׁ **n.pr.div.** Chemosh (כמש MI3. 5\. 9\. 12\. 13\. 14\. 18\. 19\. 32\. 23 also עשתר כמש l17 and n.pr.m. כמשמלך l1; As. *Kammusunadbi,* a king of Moab Schr.:COT i, 281; \= KAT 2, 288; cf. further BaeRel 13 f. 238, 256 NöZMG 1888, 471; LXX Χαμώς);—god of the Moabites to whom Solomon erected a high place [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7), [11:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:33), [2 K 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:13), [Je 48:7](https://ref.ly/Jer48:7) (Kt כמישׁ), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Jer48:13). Moab is ´עַם־כ [Nu 21:29](https://ref.ly/Num21:29) (ode) *people of Chemosh,* and Moabites his sons and daughters, cf. [Je 48:46](https://ref.ly/Jer48:46). He is said to be also the God of the Ammonites Ju [11:25](https://ref.ly/Judg11:25) (probably an error BaeRel 15 GFM).

## I כָּמַר (id: BDB04515)

\[Str3648] † I. \[כָּמַר] **vb. Niph.** grow warm and tender, be or grow hot (NH *id.* Pi. *heat* fruit in the ground, making it ripe, over\-ripe, tender; Aram. כְּמַר *id.,* and more gen. *make warm* (one's flesh, or food); cf. *kemr, fermentation,* etc., in mod. Syria WetzstZPV xiv (1891\), 6\)—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נִכְמְרוּ [Gn 43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30) \+ 2 t., נִכְמָרוּ [La 5:10](https://ref.ly/Lam5:10); —**1\.** *grow warm* and *tender,* fig., subj. רחמים; ´רַחֲמָיו אֶל־אָחִיונ [Gn 43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30) (J), so רַחֲמֶיהָ עַל־בְּנָהּ ´נ [1 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:26); (רַחֲמָי We) נִחוּמָי ´יַחַד נ [Ho 11:8](https://ref.ly/Hos11:8) (‖עָלַי לִבִּינֶהְפַּךְ). **2\.** *be* or *grow hot,* ´עוֺרֵנוּ כְתַנּוּר נ´מִפְּנֵי וגו [La 5:10](https://ref.ly/Lam5:10) *our skin has become hot like a furnace,* because of the famine.

* **Associated Passages:** Hosea 11:8; 1 Kings 3:26; Genesis 43:30; Lamentations 5:10

> \[Str4365] † מִכְמֹ֫רֶת **n.f.** id.; עַל־פְּנֵי מַיִם ´וּפֹרְשֵי מ [Is 19:8](https://ref.ly/Isa19:8) (‖מַשְׁלִיכֵי בַיְאוֺר חַכָּה).

> \[Str3649] † \[כֹּ֫מֶר] **n.m.** (idol\-) priest (so NH כּוּמָר; Aram. ܟܽܘܡܪܳܐ, כּוּמְרָא, is also priest of God; Nerab כמר HalRev. Sem. 1896, 280, 282; Nab. כמר *priest* CISii, No. 170, Teima כמרא *id.* CISii, No. 113 a, b, b *bis*; so כמר Neo\-Pun. EutZMG. 1875, 238, 239 BergerJAs Apr.–June, 1887, 465; perh. In [Ho 4:4](https://ref.ly/Hos4:4) Beck (in Wü142) We prop. וְעַמִּי כִּכְמָרָיו (for MT וְעַמְּ כִּמְרִיבֵי).

## II כֵּן (id: BDB04526)

\[Str4366] II. כֵּן **adv.** So (PH. NH Targum *id.;* Syr. ܟܢ is *then,* Ar. لَّكٍ, لَّكِنَّ, *but:* prob. from the demonstr. √ *ka,* found in כִּי etc., Köii. 1, 254\); (הִיא) כֶּן־הוּא [Gn 44:10](https://ref.ly/Gen44:10) \+ 4 t.:—*so* (i.e. usu., as has been described or commanded, with ref. to what has preceded), mostly of manner, but sts. also of quantity, quality, or degree: **1\. a.** Ju [5:31](https://ref.ly/Judg5:31) כן יאבדו *so* perish thy enemies, [Jb 5:27](https://ref.ly/Job5:27) הִיאכֶּ־ן, [8:13](https://ref.ly/Judg8:13) כן ארחות כל שׁכחי אל, [Pr 6:29](https://ref.ly/Prov6:29), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Prov8:13), [2 S 16:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:23), [Is 36:6](https://ref.ly/Isa36:6), [47:15](https://ref.ly/Isa47:15) כן היו לך, [Nu 13:33](https://ref.ly/Num13:33) וכן היינו בעיניהם; [Nu 9:16](https://ref.ly/Num9:16) כָן יהיה תמיד [Ex 10:14](https://ref.ly/Exod10:14), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Exod10:14) לא היה כן ארבה´כמהו וג (i. e. in such numbers), [1 K 10:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:12) כן עצי אלמגים לא בא, v:[20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:20), [2 Ch 1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12), [1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12), Ju [21:14](https://ref.ly/Judg21:14) להם כן ולא מצאו and they did not suffice for them *so* (viz. in such numbers of them as there were); [1 K 6:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:26) וכן הכרוב השׁני, Ez [41:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:7); ´וכן ל [Ex 27:11](https://ref.ly/Exod27:11), [1 K 10:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:29), Ez [40:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:16), [2 Ch 35:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:12), cf. [1 Ch 23:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:30); ´כן ל [Ex 25:33](https://ref.ly/Exod25:33) (cf. [26:24](https://ref.ly/Exod26:24)), [Jos 21:40](https://ref.ly/Josh21:40). **b.** the force of כֵּן has sts. to be elicited from the context : [1 K 2:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:7) כי כן קרבו אלי (sc. with kindness such as that enjoined in va), [20:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:40) כן משׁפטך, [Je 14:10](https://ref.ly/Jer14:10) לנועכן אהבו (i.e. not less than ´י has withdrawn from them v:[9](https://ref.ly/Jer14:9), Gf Ke; Gie prop. אָכֵן), [Psalm 61:9](https://ref.ly/Ps61:9) שִׁמְ כֵּן אֲזַמְּרָה (Hi כְּצִדְקְ, implied in v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ps61:6)–[8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8)), [63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3) חֲזִיתְִ כֵּן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ (sc. with the longings of v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2)), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps63:5) אֲבָרֶכְ בְחַיָּיכֵּן (sc. כְּרֹב חַסְדְּ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps63:4)), [65:10](https://ref.ly/Ps65:10) כִּי כֵן תְּכִינֶהָ (i.e. so generously, v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps65:10)), [90:12](https://ref.ly/Ps90:12) לִמְנוֺת יָמֵינוּ כֵּן הוֺדַע (Hi ´כְּיִרְאַת י v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps90:11)), [127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2) כֵּן יִתֵּן לִידִידוֺ שֵׁנָא (sc. as abundantly; but Che אָכֵן), [Pr 24:14](https://ref.ly/Prov24:14) (see v:[13](https://ref.ly/Prov24:13)), [Is 52:14](https://ref.ly/Isa52:14) כֵּן מִשְׁחַת מֵאִישׁ מַרְאֵהוּ (sufficiently to justify שׁממו עליך רבים). **c.** כן occurs freq. in partic. phrases, as (*a*) with הָיָה, esp. ויהי כן and it was *so* [Gn 1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), [1:9](https://ref.ly/Gen1:9), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11) \+ , [2 K 2:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:10) if thou seest me taken from thee יהי־לך כן let it be to thee *so* (sc. as thou desirest), [7:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:20), with עשה (esp. וַיַּעַש, וַיַּעֲשוּ) [Gn 29:28](https://ref.ly/Gen29:28), [42:20](https://ref.ly/Gen42:20), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Gen45:21) \+ oft., Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16) וְכֵן תַּעֲשוּמִמֶּנִּי תִּרְאוּ, \+ ´ל [Gn 42:25](https://ref.ly/Gen42:25) ויעש להם כן, [Ex 22:29](https://ref.ly/Exod22:29), [23:11](https://ref.ly/Exod23:11) ´כן תעשה ל, [Dt 22:3](https://ref.ly/Deut22:3), [2 S 12:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:31), [1 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:8) (cf. [6:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:33), [7:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:18)); [Gn 29:26](https://ref.ly/Gen29:26) לא יֵעָשֶה כן במקומנו, [34:7](https://ref.ly/Gen34:7), [2 S 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:12); rarer usages, דִּבֶּר כֵּן [Ex 6:9](https://ref.ly/Exod6:9), אמר כן [1 K 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:8), כֵּן אמרתם Ez [11:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:5), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:10), ´אָמֵן כֵּן יאֹמַר י [1 K 1:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:36) (cf. [Je 28:6](https://ref.ly/Jer28:6)), וגם שׁאול אבי יֹדֵעַ כֵּן [1 S 23:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:17); אָהֵב כֵּן (idiom.) to love (it) *so,* † [Je 5:31](https://ref.ly/Jer5:31), [Am 4:5](https://ref.ly/Amos4:5); (*b*) [Gn 50:3](https://ref.ly/Gen50:3) ימלאו ימי החנֻטים כי כן (cf. Ju [14:10](https://ref.ly/Judg14:10), [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18), [Est 2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12)); (*c*) alone, אִם כֵּן if it be *so* † [Gn 25:22](https://ref.ly/Gen25:22), [43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11); † כִּי־כֵןוַיִּרְאוּ [1 S 5:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:7), יָדַעְתִּי כִּי־כֵן [Jb 9:2](https://ref.ly/Job9:2); (*d*) לא כן not *so* (viz. as has been described or implied), with a subst. [Nu 12:7](https://ref.ly/Num12:7) לא כן עבדי משׁה, [2 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:21), [23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5), [Psalm 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ps1:4), [Jb 9:35](https://ref.ly/Job9:35) b כי־לא־כן אנכי עמדי not *so* am I with myself (i.e. I am not conscious of being one who would fear him, va), with a vb. [Dt 18:14](https://ref.ly/Deut18:14) ואתה ´לא כן נתן לך י (not *so,*—viz. as implied in va), [2 S 18:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:14), [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) לא כן יְדַמֶּה,לא כן יחשׁב, absol. [Gn 48:18](https://ref.ly/Gen48:18) לא כן אבי, [Ex 10:11](https://ref.ly/Exod10:11).

[Am 5:14](https://ref.ly/Amos5:14) אתּכם כאשׁר ´אמרתם יהי כן י, [Ex 10:10](https://ref.ly/Exod10:10); (*b*) [Gn 18:5](https://ref.ly/Gen18:5) כן תעשה כאשׁר דברת, [Ne 5:12](https://ref.ly/Neh5:12).—Occasionally in poetry כאשׁר is not expressed : [Is 54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9) (De), [55:9](https://ref.ly/Isa55:9) [Je 3:20](https://ref.ly/Jer3:20), [Jb 7:9](https://ref.ly/Job7:9), [Ho 11:2](https://ref.ly/Hos11:2) קראו להם כן הלכו מפניהם (of degree: *so* \= in the same proportion), [Psalm 48:6](https://ref.ly/Ps48:6) המה ראו כן תמהו (i.e. in the same measure that they saw); [Je 33:22](https://ref.ly/Jer33:22) (acc. to many, but dub.; rd. prob. כאשׁר Gie) it is represented by אשׁר (so [Is 54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9) Hi Ew Di): Ju [5:15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15) כ is not expressed.—[Na 1:12](https://ref.ly/Nah1:12) is prob. corrupt; [Zc 11:11](https://ref.ly/Zech11:11) for כן עניי rd. כְּנַעֲנֵי.—כֹּה, כָּכָה, כָּזאֹת are syn., but differ considerably in usage. **3\.** With prepositions:—**a.** אַחַר־כֵּן, אַחֲרֵי־כֵן, בָּשָׁן, lit. *after so,* i.e. *afterwards:* v. אַחַר. **b.** † בְּכֵן (late), lit. *in such circumstances,* i.e. *thereupon, then,* [Ec 8:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:10), [Est 4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16) (Targum בְּכֵין oft. for אָז; e.g. [Ex 15:1](https://ref.ly/Exod15:1); SyrJerus. ܒܟܢ \= τότε). **c.** † כֵּן כְּמ־וֺ [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6), acc. to some, *like so,* i.e. (Vrss Rabb) *in like manner,* or (De) *like this* (accompanied by a contemptuous gesture) \= like a mere nothing: but v. IV. כֵּן. **d.** לָכֵן:200 *according to such conditions, that being so, therefore* [Nu 16:11](https://ref.ly/Num16:11), [1 S 27:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:6), [Psalm 16:9](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9), [73:6](https://ref.ly/Ps73:6), [73:10](https://ref.ly/Ps73:10); esp. in proph., where it often introduces, after statement of the grounds, a divine declaration or command : Ju [10:13](https://ref.ly/Judg10:13), [2 K 1:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:6), [Am 4:12](https://ref.ly/Amos4:12), [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Isa7:14), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16), [16:7](https://ref.ly/Isa16:7), [27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9), [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Jer8:10) etc.; Special usages:—(*a*) idiom., in conversation, in reply to an objection, to state the ground upon which the answer is made; [Gn 4:15](https://ref.ly/Gen4:15) *therefore*—this being so—whoso killeth Cain, etc., [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), Ju [8:7](https://ref.ly/Judg8:7), [11:8](https://ref.ly/Judg11:8), [1 S 28:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:2), [1 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:19), [Jb 20:2](https://ref.ly/Job20:2) (LXX in Gn K Jb, not perceiving the idiom, renders οὐχοὕτως (as though לא־כן): so also strangely, elsewhere, as [1 S 3:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:14), [2 K 1:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:4), [1:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:6), [21:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:12)). (*b*) inferring the cause from the effect, or developing what is logically involved in a statement, [Is 26:14](https://ref.ly/Isa26:14) b (cf. ## I כֵּן (id: BDB04527)

\[Str3651,3653–54] I. כֵּן v. כון; III. כֵּן v. I. כנן; IV. כֵּן v. II. כנן, below

* **Associated Articles:** I כנן (ID: `BDB04534@BDBHebrewLexicon`); II כּוּן (ID: `BDB04382@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## כָּנָה (id: BDB04528)

\[Str3655] † \[כָּנָה] **vb. Pi.** betitle, title, give an epithet or cognomen (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּנָא, ܟܢܳܐ; Ar. كَنَى)—**Pi.** *Impf.* 1 s. sf. בִשְׁמְֶָ אֲכַנְּ וָאֶקְרָא לְ [Is 45:4](https://ref.ly/Isa45:4) *I have called thee by thy name, giving thee a title* (of honour; cf. for construction Dr§ 163\); 3 ms. וּבְשֵׁם יִשְרָאֵל יְכַנֶּה [44:5](https://ref.ly/Isa44:5), *and with the name Israel he titles* (himself), is hardly poss.; abs. *he betitles,* or *makes use of a title,* is unlikely; rd. prob. **Pu.,** v. infr., in bad sense \= *give a flattering title:* וְאֶל־אָדָם לאֹ אֲכַנֶּה [Jb 32:21](https://ref.ly/Job32:21) *and unto man I do not give flattering titles* (‖אַל־לנָא אֶשָּא פְנֵי אִישׁ); abs. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Job32:22). **Pu.** *Impf.* יְכֻנֶּה [Is 44:5](https://ref.ly/Isa44:5) *he is betitled* (v. supr.; so Targum BI Che Du; ‖יקרא בשׁם יעקב, etc.)

> \[Str3657] כַּנָּה [Psalm 80:16](https://ref.ly/Ps80:16) v. sub כנן p. 488\.

> \[Str3656] † כַּנֶּה **n.pr.loc.** appar. in Mesopot., ´וָעֶדֶן חָרָן וְכ Ez [27:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23), LXX Χαναα; identif. dub.; \= כַּלְנֶה Thes and most; Co *rds.* כַּלְנֶה; MezStadt Ḫarrân 34 prop. בְּנֵי עֶדֶן.

> \[Str3674] כְּנָוֺת v. כְּנָת.

> \[Str3078,3204] כָּנְיָהוּ v. יְהוֺיָכִין, and יְכוֺנְיָה sub כון. p. 467

> \[Str3654] כִֶּנָּם, כִּנִּם v. IV. כֵּן sub II. כנן. below

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 45:4; Isaiah 44:5; Job 32:21; Psalms 80:16; Ezekiel 27:23; Job 32:22

## I כנן (id: BDB04534)

I. כנן (√ of foll.; parallel form of כון; *be firm, substantial*).

> \[Str3653] † III. כֵּן **n.m.** base, pedestal, office (NH כַּנָּה; Aram. כַּנְתָּא, ܟܰܢܳܐ)—abs. ´כ [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23), [1 K 7:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:31) (but v. infr.); sf. כַּנּוֺ [Ex 30:18](https://ref.ly/Exod30:18) \+ 11 t.; כֶַּ\+נְּ [Gn 40:13](https://ref.ly/Gen40:13); כַּנִּי [41:13](https://ref.ly/Gen41:13); —**1\.** lit. *base, pedestal,* [1 K 7:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:31) (like) the work of *a pedestal* (Th VB), acc. to StaZAW iii, 1883, 161, 162 מַעֲשֵה־כֵן is in wrong place, being orig. part of a gloss to v:[35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:35); —´כ in v:[29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:29) \= *thus,* or is txt. err.;—כֵּן־תָּרְנָם [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23) *the base* (*support*or *socket*) *of their mast* (so Thes and most); esp. of base of laver of tabernacle [Ex 30:18](https://ref.ly/Exod30:18), [30:28](https://ref.ly/Exod30:28), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Exod31:9), [35:16](https://ref.ly/Exod35:16), [38:8](https://ref.ly/Exod38:8), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39), [40:11](https://ref.ly/Exod40:11), [Lv 8:11](https://ref.ly/Lev8:11) (all P). **2\.** *office, place* [Gn 40:13](https://ref.ly/Gen40:13), [41:13](https://ref.ly/Gen41:13) (both E); hence (late) עַל־כַּנּוֺ *in his place* [Dn 11:20](https://ref.ly/Dan11:20), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Dan11:21), [11:38](https://ref.ly/Dan11:38) (i.e. in his stead, as his successor, cf. Germ. *an seiner Stelle*); עַל om. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Dan11:7).

> \[Str3663] † כְּנַנְיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (´י *is firm*);—**1\.** a Levite [1 Ch 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:22) LXX Κωνενια, A Χωνενια, LXXL Ιεχονιας; \= כְּנַנְיָה v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:27), LXX Ιεχονιας, A Χενενιας, LXXL Χονενιας. **2\.** an Izharite [1 Ch 26:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:29), LXX Χωνεν(ε)ια(ς).

> \[Str3662] † כְּנָנִי **n.pr.m.** a Levite [Ne 9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4), א:c. a A Χανανι, LXXL Χωνενιας (B om.)

## II כנן (id: BDB04539)

II. כנן (√ of foll.; meaning dubious; v. conj. in Köii. 1, 100 Anm.)

> \[Str3654] † IV. כֵּן (?), כִּנִּם, כִּנָּם **n.\[m.]**, mng. dub.; either gnat, gnats, gnat\-swarm (so LXX Vulgate PhiloVita Mos. i, p. 97, OrigenHom. [4:6](https://ref.ly/Exod4:6) in Ex., and most moderns), or \[louse,] lice (so SyrVer Targum JosAnt. ii. 14\. 13 BochHieroz. II, ii, 572 f., as NH כִּנָּה, pl. כִּנִּים \= *maggots,* and esp. *lice*)—abs. perh. כֵּן [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6) (v. infr.); pl. כִּנִּים [Ex 8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14), [Psalm 105:31](https://ref.ly/Ps105:31), כִּנִּם [Ex 8:12](https://ref.ly/Exod8:12), [Ex 8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14) has, also, כִּנָּם prob. a mere Mas. device for כִּנִּם (cf. Di), on account of preceding וַתְּהִי (on which see Ges§ 145\. 4 DaSynt. § 116\); Sam. has כנים throughout:—of Egyptian plague [Ex 8:12](https://ref.ly/Exod8:12), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Exod8:13), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14) (all P), hence [Psalm 105:31](https://ref.ly/Ps105:31); כְּמוֺ־כֵן יְמוּתוּן [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6) *like a gnatswarm* (?) *shall they die* (cf. Di Du; Weir Che rd. כנים, cf. Buhlp. 355; \> Brd De *like this*).
> 
>  *Note.*—NöM 54 BaNB § 15, ES 53 BuhlGes HWB ed. 12 connect this word etymol. With Aram. כלמתא *vermin,* Ba and Buhl also with As. *kalmatu, id.* \[DlHWB 333], and even with Aram. קלמתא, ܩܠܡܬܐ, Ar. قَمْلٌ, etc., Eth. ቍመል፡ *louse;* but these connexions, as well as that with NH כְּנִימָה, all involving radical מ, are most improb.

> \[Str3657,3661] † כַּנָּה [Psalm 80:16](https://ref.ly/Ps80:16) **n.f.** (si vera l.) support (of tree), i.e. *root, stock* (fem. of III. כֵּן; cf. Syr. ܟܰܢܳܐ m. *id.,* ChePsalms 397, so Ges); al. take as vb., but LXX κατάρτισαι is ag. gramm. (should be כֹּנְנָה), and √ כנן \= كَنَّ *protect* (Hu Ri De; rd. then כֹּ֫נָּה) is improb. Txt. dubious.

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 8:13; Exodus 8:12; Psalms 105:31; Exodus 4:6; Exodus 8:14; Psalms 80:16; Isaiah 51:6


## כָּנַס (id: BDB04542)

\[Str3664] † \[כָּנַס] **vb.** gather, collect (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּנַס (v. also כנשׁ); Eth. ከነሰ፡ in der. conj. *assemble for worship,* etc.; Ar. كَنَسَ is *sweep, sweep away, destroy*)—**Qal** (late) *Pf.* 1 s. כָּנַסְתִּי [Ec 2:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:8); *Imv.* כְּנוֺס [Est 4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16); *Inf. cstr.* [1 Ch 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:2) \+ 3 t.; *Pt.* כֹּנֵס [Psalm 33:7](https://ref.ly/Ps33:7); —*gather* people [1 Ch 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:2), [Est 4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16); כַּנֵּד מֵי הַָ\+יּם נֹתֵן בְּאוֺצָרוֺת תְּהוֺמוֺת ´כ [Psalm 33:7](https://ref.ly/Ps33:7) (rd. perh., for כַּנֵּד, כַּנּאֹד *as* in *a wine skin,* v. נאד, נד); *gather, collect* portions of harvest for priests and Levites [Ne 12:44](https://ref.ly/Neh12:44); stones [Ec 3:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:5) (opp. השׁליך); silver and gold \= *amass* wealth [Ec 2:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:8); abs. v:[26](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:26) (‖אסף). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 s. וְכִנַּסְתִּ֫י אֶתְכֶם Ez [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:21) *and I will gather you together* (for punishment; Co tr. to v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) and rds. for וְהִנַּחְתִּ֫י; ‖קבץ v:[19](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:19), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20)); of restoration וְכִנַּסְתִּים אֶל־אַדְמָתָם [39:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:28), and יְכַנֵּס [Psalm 147:2](https://ref.ly/Ps147:2). **Hithp.** *Inf. cstr.* כְּהִתְכַּנֵּסוְהַמַּסֵּכָה צָרָה [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20) *and the covering is (too) narrow when one gathers oneself together* (‖מֵהִשְתָּרֵעַקָצַר הַמַּצָּע), i.e. the bed is too short to stretch oneself at full length, and when one (perforce) draws up the feet, the covering becomes too narrow.

> \[Str4370] † \[מִכְנָס] **n.m.**:Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18) only du. (or pl.) drawers (connex. with above √ כנס obscure; Di der. fr. כנס \= גנז *cover up, hide* (cf. Du [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20)), which Thes also comp.)—only cstr. מִָכְנְסֵי—a priestly garment of linen מִכְנְסֵי פִשְׁתִּים Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18); בָּד(הַ)מִכְנְסֵי [Ex 28:42](https://ref.ly/Exod28:42), [39:28](https://ref.ly/Exod39:28), [Lv 6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4) (all P).

> \[Str3672] † כִּנֶּ֫רֶתֵ, כִּֽנְַַרוֺת **n.pr.loc.** in Galilee (Naphtali), כִּנֶּרֶת [Dt 3:17](https://ref.ly/Deut3:17), [Jos 13:27](https://ref.ly/Josh13:27), [Nu 34:11](https://ref.ly/Num34:11); כִָּ\+נּרֶת [Jos 19:35](https://ref.ly/Josh19:35); כִּֽנְַַרוֺת (so Baer; כִּֽנְרוֺת Norzi) [Jos 11:2](https://ref.ly/Josh11:2), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3), [1 K 15:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:20); —**1\.** a city [Jos 19:35](https://ref.ly/Josh19:35) (P; LXX B Κενερεθ, A Χενερεθ, LXXL Χενερεθ, as always exc. [Jos 13:27](https://ref.ly/Josh13:27)), so prob. [11:2](https://ref.ly/Josh11:2) (D ?; B Κενερωθ, A Χενερεθθι), [Dt 3:17](https://ref.ly/Deut3:17) (B Μαχαναρεθ;—Μαχ\- \= ´מִבּ). **2\.** יָם־כִּנֶּרֶת, lake near the city, [Nu 34:11](https://ref.ly/Num34:11) (P; LXX B Χεναρα, A Χενερεθ), [Jos 13:27](https://ref.ly/Josh13:27) (P; LXX B Χενερεθ; A LXXL Χενερωθ), יָם כִּֽנְַַרוֺת [Jos 12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3) (D; LXX B Χενερεθ; A Χεννερεθ); כָּל־כִּֽנְַַרוֺת [1 K 15:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:20) \= all the territory about the city and lake, cf. πᾶσαν τὴνγῆν Χενερεθ LXXL,—Targum has גִּינֵסַר, גְּנֵיסַר, גִּנֹּסַר, cf. τὸ ὕδωρ τοῦ Γεννησαρ 1 Makk. 11:67; Γεννησαρετ [Mk 6:53](https://ref.ly/Mark6:53), [Mt 14:34](https://ref.ly/Matt14:34), [Lu 5:1](https://ref.ly/Luke5:1); etymol. connexion with כִּנֶּרֶת disputed by GASmGeogr. 443 n. BuhlGeogr. 113 n.—On the lake and surrounding region v. GASmGeogr. Ch. xxi. BDPal 254 BuhlGeogr. 113 f. 225 FurrerZPV. 1879 (ii.), 52–74 Freiib. 1886 (ix.), 81–145 van Kasterenib. 1888 (xi.), 212–248\.

> \[Str3674] † \[כְּנָת] **n.** \[of men, but **f.** in form, Ges§ 122, 4\. b] associate, colleague (loan\-wd. fr. BAram. כְּנָת; Syr. ܟܢܳܬ, cf. also SchwallyIdiot. 46; on format. v. LagBN 82\);—only Aram pl. שְׁאָר כְּנָוֺתָו֯ [Ezr 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:7) *the rest of his associates.*

> \[Str3676] כֵּס [Ex 17:16](https://ref.ly/Exod17:16), v. כִּסֵּא. below

> \[Str3677] † כֶּ֫סֶא [Pr 7:20](https://ref.ly/Prov7:20), כֶּ֫סֶה [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4) **n.\[m.]** full moon (cf. Aram. ܟܶܣܐܴܐ; orig. dubious, cf. LagSymn. i. 93; perh. As. loan\-word; cf. As. *kusêu, headdress* or *cap,* \= *agû, id.,* and also *full moon* (as tiara of moon\-god ?), DlHWB, sub *kusêu, kubšu, agû*; yet v. Brock)—´לְיוֺם הַכּ [Pr 7:20](https://ref.ly/Prov7:20); as a feast\-day, ´בַּכּ [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4) (opp. בַּחֹדֶשׁ, *at the new moon*).

## I כָּסָה (id: BDB04561)

\[Str3680] † I. \[כָּסָה] **vb. cover** (NH כָּסָה Pi; Aram. כְּסָא chiefly Pa; ܟܣܳܐ Pa. *hide, cover;* ܟܣܳܝܳܐ *covering,* ܬܰܟܣܺܝܬܳܐ *garment;* Ar. كَسَا (كسو) *clothe,* كِسَآءٌ *garment;* As. *kusû,* cover: *kusîtu,* garment DlHWB 342\)—**Qal,** only *Pt. act.* כֹּסֶה;—**1\.** *conceal* shame [Pr 12:16](https://ref.ly/Prov12:16); knowledge [Pr 12:23](https://ref.ly/Prov12:23). **2\.** *pass.* (cstr.) כְּסוּי חֲטָאָה [Psalm 32:1](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1) *covered* in respect of sin (by God, which he thus puts out of sight) (‖נְשוּי־פֶּשַׁע). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִכְסָתָּה, *covered,* with waves [Je 51:42](https://ref.ly/Jer51:42); *Inf. cstr.* הִכָּסוֺת Ez [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:8), (blood) not to be covered. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. כִּסָּה [Nu 9:15](https://ref.ly/Num9:15) \+ 12 t.; sf. כִּסָּהוּ [Lv 17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13), [Nu 17:7](https://ref.ly/Num17:7); כִּסָּמוֺ [Ex 15:10](https://ref.ly/Exod15:10); 3 fs. כִּסְּתָה [Gn 38:15](https://ref.ly/Gen38:15) \+ 5 t.; 1 s. כִּסִּיתִי [Psalm 32:5](https://ref.ly/Ps32:5) \+ 3 t.; כִּסֵּתִי Ez [31:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:15) (del. Co); כִּסֵּיתִי Ez [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:7) \+ 13 t. mountains with water [Gn 7:19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Gen7:20) (P); field with nettles [Pr 24:31](https://ref.ly/Prov24:31); abs., windows of temple Ez [41:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:16). **2\.** sq. בְּ, *be clothed,* with sackcloth [1 Ch 21:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:16); name with darkness [Ec 6:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:4). **Hithp.** *Impf.* וַיִּתְכַּס [Is 37:1](https://ref.ly/Isa37:1) \= [2 K 19:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:1); 3 fs. תִּכַּסֶּה [Pr 26:26](https://ref.ly/Prov26:26); —*cover, clothe oneself,* abs. (of veil) [Gn 24:65](https://ref.ly/Gen24:65) (J); with בְּ, of new garment [1 K 11:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:29); sackcloth [2 K 19:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:1), [19:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:2) \= [Is 37:1](https://ref.ly/Isa37:1), [37:2](https://ref.ly/Isa37:2); fig. of works [Is 59:6](https://ref.ly/Isa59:6), of hatred with guile [Pr 26:26](https://ref.ly/Prov26:26); with acc. [Jon 3:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:5) (שַקִּים).

## II כסה (id: BDB04566)

II. כסה (√ of foll.; prob. *bind;* cf. As. *kasû*, *take captive*, **Pi.** *fetter, kasîtu, kisittu, a fetter;* ZehnpfundBAS i, 596 DlBaer's Ezech. xii, HWB 342\).

> \[Str3704] † \[כֶּ֫סֶת] **n.f.** band, fillet (\= charm or amulet acc. to Ephr. Syr., φυλακτήρια acc. to ὁ Ἑβραῖος of Hexapl., cf. RSJPh xiii, 286; in NH כסת usu. \= *cushion, bolster, pillow*), only pl. מְתַפְּרוֺת כְּסָתוֺת עַ־ל Ez [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18) women *sewing bands upon* elbows; c. sf. 2 fpl. כִּסְתוֺתֵיכֶ֫נָה v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:20).

> \[Str3677] כֶּסֶה v. כֶּסֶא

> \[Str3678] כִּסֵּה v. כִּסֵּא

> \[Str5478] כַּסּוּחָה [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25) v. סוּחָה. p. 691

> \[Str3681–82] כָּסוּי, כְּסוּת v. sub I. כסה. above

## כָּסַח (id: BDB04572)

\[Str3683] † \[כָּסַח] **vb.** cut off or away, a plant (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּסַח, ܟܣܰܚ (for Heb. זָמַר); Ar. كَسَحَ *sweep off, away, destroy, do away with;* cf. Sab. כסח *overpower, conquer,* HomZMG 1892, 532\)—only **Qal** *Pt. pass.*, fs. כְּסוּחָה [Psalm 80:17](https://ref.ly/Ps80:17), *cut away*, of Isr. under fig. of vine; mpl. קוֺצִים כְּסוּחִים [Is 33:12](https://ref.ly/Isa33:12) *thorns cut away*, fig. of peoples destroyed by divine judgment.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 33:12; Psalms 80:17


## כָסַל (id: BDB04573)

\[Str3688] † \[כָסַל] **vb.** be or become stupid (NH Aram. in derive.; Ar. كَسِلَ *be sluggish*, so N Syr. in deriv.; orig. mng. possibly *thick, plump, fat;* hence in good sense: כְּסָלִים *loins*, כֶּסֶל, כִּסְלָה *confidence;* in bad sense: כֶּסֶל, כִּסְלָה, כְּסִילוּת *stupidity, folly,* כְּסִיל *stupid fellow*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִכְסָלוּ *they become stupid* [Je 10:8](https://ref.ly/Jer10:8) (‖יִבְעֲרוּ *they become brutish*).

> \[Str3689] † כֶּ֫סֶל **n.m. 1\.** loins, **2\.** stupidity, **3\.** confidence (NH *id. lion;* Aram. כִּסְלָא *id.*);—**1\.** *loins* כָּסֶל [Jb 15:27](https://ref.ly/Job15:27); pl. כְּסָלִים [Lv 3:4](https://ref.ly/Lev3:4), [3:10](https://ref.ly/Lev3:10), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Lev3:15), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Lev4:9), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Lev7:4); sf. כְּסָלַי, [Psalm 38:8](https://ref.ly/Ps38:8). **2\.** *stupidity, folly* ´כ [Ec 7:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:25) (‖סִכְלוּת), כֵּסֶל [Psalm 49:14](https://ref.ly/Ps49:14). **3\.** *confidence,* sf. כִּסְלִי [Jb 31:24](https://ref.ly/Job31:24); כִּסְלְֶָ [Pr 3:26](https://ref.ly/Prov3:26); כִּסְלוֺ [Jb 8:14](https://ref.ly/Job8:14); כִּסְלָם [Psalm 78:7](https://ref.ly/Ps78:7).

> \[Str3690] † כִּסְלָה **n.f.** (GieZAW i, 1881, 304\). **1\.** stupidity [Psalm 85:9](https://ref.ly/Ps85:9) (but rd. לִבָּם לֽה LXX Bae Che). **2\.** confidence, sf. כִּסְלָתְֶָ [Jb 4:6](https://ref.ly/Job4:6).

> \[Str3684] I. כְּסִיל:70 **n.m.** stupid fellow, dullard, fool;—´כ [Psalm 49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11) \+ 44 t.; pl. כְּסִילִים [Psalm 94:8](https://ref.ly/Ps94:8) \+ 25 t.;—‖בער [Psalm 49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11), [92:7](https://ref.ly/Ps92:7), [94:8](https://ref.ly/Ps94:8), elsewh. only in Wisd Lt.; he hates knowledge [Pr 1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22); delights not in understanding [18:2](https://ref.ly/Prov18:2); it is his sport to do mischief [10:23](https://ref.ly/Prov10:23); his heart proclaimeth אִוֶּלָת [12:23](https://ref.ly/Prov12:23); his mouth poureth it forth [15:2](https://ref.ly/Prov15:2), and feedeth on it [15:14](https://ref.ly/Prov15:14).

> \[Str3687] † כְּסִילוּת **n.f.** stupidity;—[Pr 9:13](https://ref.ly/Prov9:13) ´אֵשֶׁת כ, *the woman Stupidity,* in antithesis with חָכְמוֺת the Supreme Wisdom personified as a woman.

> \[Str3685] † II. כְּסִיל **n.m.** Orion (relation to above √ obscure);—[Am 5:8](https://ref.ly/Amos5:8), [Jb 9:9](https://ref.ly/Job9:9); also תְּפַתֵּחַ ´משְׁכוֺת כ [38:31](https://ref.ly/Job38:31) *the cords of Orion wilt* (*canst*) *thou let out ?* (appar. some mythological allusion, v. Di Che, to giant bound in skies; cf. Hom. ’Ωρίωνα δοκεύει, and σθέηος Ὠρίωνος); both times associated with the Pleiades; sf. כְּסִילֵיהֶם *their Orions* [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10), Orion and other constellations of the same brilliancy.

> \[Str3686] † III. כְּסִיל **n.pr.loc.** in S of tribe of Judah [Jos 15:30](https://ref.ly/Josh15:30), prob. corrupt for בְּתוּל [Jos 19:4](https://ref.ly/Josh19:4); see בְּתוּאֵל.

Tabor, [Jos 19:12](https://ref.ly/Josh19:12); \= תָּבוֺר [Jos 19:22](https://ref.ly/Josh19:22), [1 Ch 6:62](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:62); \= mod. *Iksâl* RobBR iii. 182, Memi. 365 BuhlG. § 113, p. 216\.

> \[Str3695] † כַּסְלֻחִים **n.pr.gent.** [Gn 10:14](https://ref.ly/Gen10:14) (LXX D Χασμωνιειμ, LXXL Χασλωνιειμ E Χαλοειμ) \= [1 Ch 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:12) (A Χασλωνιειμ, LXXL Χασλωειμ), among the sons of מִָצְרַיִם not identif.; conject. in Thes Di.

> \[Str3696] כִּסְלֽת תָּבוֺר v. כְּסֻלּוֺת supr.

Ges§ 56\);—only *Impf.* 3 ms. sf.:—יְכַרְסְמֶנָּה חֲזִיר מִיָּ֫עַר [Psalm 80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14) *teareth it off* (sc. the vine, fig. of Israel).

> \[Str4373] † מִכְסָה **n.f.** computation (f. of מֶכֶס); hence—**a.** *number,* [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4) (P). **b.** *valuation, worth,* [Lv 27:23](https://ref.ly/Lev27:23) (P).

## כָּעַס (id: BDB04596)

\[Str3707] † כָּעַס **vb.** be vexed, angry (NH כָּעַס, Aram. כְּעַם (not Syr.));—**Qal** *Pf.* ´כ [Psalm 112:10](https://ref.ly/Ps112:10), [Ec 5:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:16); *Impf.* וַיִּכְעַס [Ne 3:33](https://ref.ly/Neh3:33), [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10); 1 s. אֶכְעַס Ez [16:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:42); *Inf.* לִכְעוֺם [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9).—**1\.** *be vexed, indignant* [Psalm 112:10](https://ref.ly/Ps112:10), [Ne 3:33](https://ref.ly/Neh3:33), [Ec 5:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:16). **2\.** *be angry* Ez [16:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:42), [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9), c. אֶל Pers. [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10). **Piel.** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. כִּעֲסַתָּה [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6); 3 mpl. sf. כִּעֲסוּנִי [Dt 32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21) *provoke to anger.*

 **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִכְעִיס [Ho 12:15](https://ref.ly/Hos12:15), [1 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:30); sf. הִכְעִיסוֺ [2 K 23:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:26) \+ 4 t. Pf.; הַכְעִסֵו֯נִי [Je 25:7](https://ref.ly/Jer25:7); (scribal error for תַּכְעִיסוּני acc. to SS); *Impf.* וַיַּכְעֵס [1 K 22:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:54), [2 Ch 28:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:25); 3 fs. sf. תַּכְעִיסֶנָּה [1 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:7) \+ 6 t. Impf.; *Inf. cstr.* הַכְעִיס [1 K 16:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:13) \+ 5 t.; sf. סֵנִי(י)הַכְעִ [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) \+ 11 t., \+ 6 t. sfs.; *Pt.* pl. מַכְעִיסִים [Je 7:19](https://ref.ly/Jer7:19) \+ 4 t.—**1\.** *vex,* c. acc. pers [1 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:7), Ez [32:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:9). **2\.** *vex, provoke to anger,* esp. of provoking Yahweh by workship of other gods Ju [2:12](https://ref.ly/Judg2:12), [1 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:9), [14:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:15), [16:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:33), [22:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:54), [2 K 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:11), [23:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:19), [2 Ch 28:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:25), [Ne 3:37](https://ref.ly/Neh3:37), [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Jer7:19), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Jer11:17), [32:29](https://ref.ly/Jer32:29), [32:32](https://ref.ly/Jer32:32), [44:3](https://ref.ly/Jer44:3), Ez [8:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:17), [16:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:26), [Is 65:3](https://ref.ly/Isa65:3), [Ho 12:15](https://ref.ly/Hos12:15); c. בּ instr. בְּתוֺעֵבֹת [Dt 32:16](https://ref.ly/Deut32:16); בְּהַבְלֵיהֶם [Dt 32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21), [1 K 16:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:13), [16:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:26); בחטאתם [1 K 16:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:2); (יו)יד (י)במעש [Dt 31:29](https://ref.ly/Deut31:29), [1 K 16:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:7), [Je 25:6](https://ref.ly/Jer25:6), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Jer25:7), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Jer32:30), [44:8](https://ref.ly/Jer44:8); ידיהם (ה)בכל מעש [2 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:17) \= [2 Ch 34:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:25); בבמות [Psalm 78:58](https://ref.ly/Ps78:58); בפסיליהם [Je 8:19](https://ref.ly/Jer8:19); במעלליהם [Psalm 106:29](https://ref.ly/Ps106:29); cf. the phrases (ו)להכעיס ´בעיני י (ה)עשה הרע *do the evil in the eyes of Yahweh to provoke* (him) *to anger* [Dt 4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Deut9:18), [31:29](https://ref.ly/Deut31:29), [1 K 16:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:7), [2 K 17:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:17), [21:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:6) \= [2 Ch 33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6); so מכעסים [2 K 21:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:15), [Je 32:30](https://ref.ly/Jer32:30); (ים)אשׁר הכעיס כעס [1 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:30), [21:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:22), [2 K 23:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:26).—The phrase is characteristic of D Je and the compiler of Kings; see DrIntr. 191; [Dt 4:25](https://ref.ly/Deut4:25) Holzeini. Hex. 287\. 

> \[Str3708] † כַּ֫עַס **n.m.** vexation, anger;—´כ [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) \+ 13 t.; כָּעַס [Pr 21:19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19), [Ec 1:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:18); sf. כַּעְסִי [1 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16), כַּעַסְ [Psalm 85:5](https://ref.ly/Ps85:5), כַּעְסוֺ [Pr 12:16](https://ref.ly/Prov12:16), [1 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:30); *pl.* כְּעָסִים [2 K 23:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:26).—*vexation:* **1\.** of men, esp. caused by unmerited treatment, [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6), [1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16), [Pr 12:16](https://ref.ly/Prov12:16), [17:25](https://ref.ly/Prov17:25), [21:19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Prov27:3), [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9). **2\.** *vexation, anger of* Yahweh (caused esp. by worship of other gods) [1 K 15:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:30), [21:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:22), [2 K 23:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:26); עִמָּנוּ ´כ [Psalm 85:5](https://ref.ly/Ps85:5) *anger* (*of Yahweh*) *with us;* בָּנָיו ´כ [Dt 32:19](https://ref.ly/Deut32:19) *anger against his sons,* אוֺיֵב ´כ v:[27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) *anger against the enemy* (both obj. gen. see Di; RV *provocation of,* cf. Dr); קָרְבָּנָם ´כ Ez [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28) *provocation of their offering* (so RV but Co del., rightly). **3\.** *vexation, grief* [Psalm 6:8](https://ref.ly/Ps6:8), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Ps10:14), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ps31:10), [Ec 1:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:18), [2:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:23), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:3), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:10), also כעס [5:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:16) acc. Ew aI.

> \[Str3708] † כַּ֫עַש **n.m.** (dialectic variation of כַּעַם, only in Jb);—´כ [Jb 17:7](https://ref.ly/Job17:7), כָּעָש [Jb 5:2](https://ref.ly/Job5:2); sf. כַּעְשִי [Jb 6:2](https://ref.ly/Job6:2); כַּעַשְ [Jb 10:17](https://ref.ly/Job10:17); —**1\.** *vexation, grief* of men [Jb 5:2](https://ref.ly/Job5:2) (cf. [Pr 12:16](https://ref.ly/Prov12:16), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Prov27:3)), [6:2](https://ref.ly/Job6:2), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Job17:7). **2\.** *vexation, anger,* of God; c. כַּעַשְ עִמָּדִי [Jb 10:17](https://ref.ly/Job10:17) *thine anger with me.*

Nu 7; [1 K 7:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:50) \= [2 Ch 4:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:22), [2 K 25:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:14) \= [Je 52:18](https://ref.ly/Jer52:18), [Je 52:19](https://ref.ly/Jer52:19), [2 Ch 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:14). † **c.** *hollow* of sling, [1 S 25:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:29). † **d.** כַּפּוֺת תְּמָרִים [Lv 23:40](https://ref.ly/Lev23:40) \= of huge *hand\-shaped branches* (or *fronds*) *of palm\-trees.* **e.** כַּפּוֺת הַמַּנְעוּל Ct [5:5](https://ref.ly/Song5:5) *the* (bent) *handles of the bolt.*

> \[Str3712] † כִּפָּה **n.f.** branch frond (prob. of palm tree, cf. כַּף **3 d**);—´כ [Is 9:13](https://ref.ly/Isa9:13), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Isa19:15) fig. of nobles, rulers (both opp. אַגְמוֺן *rush,* i.e. high and low; ‖רֹאשׁ וְזָנָב); c. sf. כִּפָּתוֺ [Jb 15:32](https://ref.ly/Job15:32) (of wicked under fig. of palm\-tree).
