## כפר (id: BDB04613)

I כפר (of foll.; orig. mng. dub., but most prob. *cover,* cf. Ar. كَفَرَ *cover, hide;* \> RS who thinks of Aram. כָפַר, ܟܦܰܪ Pa. *wash away, rub off* whence כֹּפֶר,כִּפֶּר of *washing away, obliteration* of sin : NH כִּפֶּר, Aram. כַּפֵּר and derive.; Ar. كَفَّارَةٌ *an expiation* (see RSOTJC 438; 2\. 381 Kn on [Lv 4:20](https://ref.ly/Lev4:20) RiBegr. Der Sühne WeComp. 335 f. SmAT Rel. Gesch. 321 NowArch. ii, 192 DrDt 425 SchmollerSt,Kr. 1891, 205 ff. LagBN 230 ff.)).

497:a. I. כפר, on orig. mng. cf. now DrHast. DBPROPITIATION; also As. *kuppuru, purify* (and deriv. *takpirtu*) ZimKAT 3\. 601 f. MorgensternSin in Bab. Rel. (MVG. 1905, 3\) 44, 122

> \[Str3724] † I. כֹּפֶר **n.m.** the price of a life, ransom (ποινή, *wergild*);—´כ [Ex 21:30](https://ref.ly/Exod21:30) \+ 10 t.; sf. כָּפְרְ [Is 43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3); כָפְרוֺ [Psalm 49:8](https://ref.ly/Ps49:8); —**1\.** *a price for ransom of a life* [Jb 33:24](https://ref.ly/Job33:24), [36:18](https://ref.ly/Job36:18); עַל ´כ [Ex 21:30](https://ref.ly/Exod21:30) (Covt. code; ‖פִּדְיֹן נַפְשׁוֺ); נפשׁ ´כ [Pr 13:8](https://ref.ly/Prov13:8); לְ ´כ *ransom for* [Pr 6:35](https://ref.ly/Prov6:35), [21:18](https://ref.ly/Prov21:18), [Nu 35:31](https://ref.ly/Num35:31), [35:32](https://ref.ly/Num35:32) (P); כָּפְרוֺ *his ransom* [Psalm 49:8](https://ref.ly/Ps49:8) (‖פדה ); כָּפְרְ *thy ransom* [Is 43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3) ( ‖תַּחְתֶּיְ ); כֹּפֶר alone [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [Am 5:12](https://ref.ly/Amos5:12). **2\.** in the ritual of P נַפְשׁוֺ ´כ [Ex 30:12](https://ref.ly/Exod30:12) is a half shekel of the sanctuary paid by each male above twenty years at the census in order that there might be no plague upon them. It was offered to Yahweh, לְכַפֵּר על *to atone for them.*

> \[Str3722] † כִּפֶּר **vb. Pi.** etc. **denom.** cover over (fig.), pacify, make propitiation;—**Pi.** *Pf.* כִּפֶּר [Ex 30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10) \+ 31 t.; 2 ms. sf. כִּפַּרְתָּהוּ Ez [43:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:20); 3 mpl. יְכַפְּרוּ Ez [43:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:26); 2 mpl. כִּפַּרְתֶּם Ez [45:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:20); *Impf.* יְכַפֵּר [Ex 30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10) \+ 10 t. ; יְכַפֶּ־ר [Lv 7:7](https://ref.ly/Lev7:7), [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8); sf. יְכַפְּרֶנָּה [Pr 16:14](https://ref.ly/Prov16:14); 1 s. אֲכַפֵּר [2 S 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:3); אֲכַפְּרָה [Gn 32:21](https://ref.ly/Gen32:21), [Ex 32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30), etc.; *Imv.* כַּפֵּר [Dt 21:8](https://ref.ly/Deut21:8) \+ 4 t.; *Inf.* כַּפֵּר [Ex 30:15](https://ref.ly/Exod30:15) \+ 28 t.; sfs. כַּפְּרִי Ez [16:63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63); כַּפֶּרְ [Ex 29:36](https://ref.ly/Exod29:36); כַּפְּרָהּ [Is 47:11](https://ref.ly/Isa47:11); —**1\.** *cover over, pacify, propitiate;* אֲכַפָּרָה פָנָיו בַּמִּנְחָה [Gn 32:21](https://ref.ly/Gen32:21) *let me cover his face by the present* (so that he does not see the offence, i.e. *pacify,* him; E; RSOTJC, 2d ed., 381 ‘ wipe clean the face, ’ blackened by displeasure, as the Arabs say ‘ whiten the face ’); לאֹ תוּכְלִי כַּפְּרָהּוְתִפֹּל עָלַיִח הֹוָה [Is 47:11](https://ref.ly/Isa47:11)  *and disaster will fall upon thee, thou wilt not be able to propitiate it* (by payment of a כֹּפֶר, see [Is 43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3)); pacify the wrath of a king [Pr 16:14](https://ref.ly/Prov16:14) (e.g. by a gift). **2\.** *cover over, atone for sin,* without sacrifice : **a.** man as subj., בַּמָּה אֲכַפֵּר [2 S 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:3), *with what shall I cover over* (viz. the bloodguiltiness of the house of Saul, says David. The answer is by a death penalty of seven sons of the guilty house); חטאתכם בְּעַד [Ex 32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30) *on behalf of your sins* (JE; Moses, by intercession); c. על of persons [Nu 17:11](https://ref.ly/Num17:11), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Num17:12) (P; by incense), [25:13](https://ref.ly/Num25:13) (P when Phinehas slays the ringleaders). **b.** with God as subj., c. acc. Pers., *cover,* i.e. treat as covered, view propitiously, Yahweh's land [Dt 32:43](https://ref.ly/Deut32:43) (song); לְ Pers. [Dt 21:8](https://ref.ly/Deut21:8) (bloodguiltiness flows away in the stream), Ez [16:63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63); בְּעַד of person [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18); c. acc. of *the sin* [Psalm 65:4](https://ref.ly/Ps65:4), [78:38](https://ref.ly/Ps78:38), prob. also [Dn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24) ( ‖חטאתח֯תם ); עַל of sin, [Psalm 79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9), [Je 18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23) ( ‖מחה ). It is conceived that God in his sovereignty may himself provide an atonement or covering for men and their sins which could not be provided by men. **3\.** *cover over, atone for sin and persons by legal rites,* in the codes of H, P, and Ez : abs. הַכֹּהֵן ´וכ *and the priest shall make atonement* [Lv 16:32](https://ref.ly/Lev16:32); **a.** c. acc. of sacred places (by the great sin\-offering of the day of atonement), [Lv 16:20](https://ref.ly/Lev16:20), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33); also Ez [43:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:20), [43:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:26), [45:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:20) (by the blood of the sin\-offering ‖חִטֵּא, טִהַר). **b.** usually c. עַל (I) of things, e.g. of the altar to which the blood of the sin\-offering was applied [Ex 29:36](https://ref.ly/Exod29:36), [29:37](https://ref.ly/Exod29:37), [30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10), [Lv 8:15](https://ref.ly/Lev8:15) ( ‖קִדַּשׁ ), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Lev16:18); and specifically the horns of the altar [Ex 30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10); the holy place of the tabernacle [Lv 16:16](https://ref.ly/Lev16:16) (by the great sin\-offering, because of (מִן ) the uncleannesses of the children of Israel and because of their transgressions); for the leprous house by ceremony of purification [Lv 14:53](https://ref.ly/Lev14:53) ( ‖טִהַר ); for the goat לעזאזל [Lv 16:10](https://ref.ly/Lev16:10) (which was presented before Yahweh to consecrate him for the bearing away of the sins of the people). (2\) of persons, נפשׁתיכם ע־ל, *for your persons, yourselves,* e.g. by the payment of atonement\-money כֶּסֶף הַכִּפֻּרִים at the census [Ex 30:15](https://ref.ly/Exod30:15), [30:16](https://ref.ly/Exod30:16); by the קרבן of the spoils [Nu 31:50](https://ref.ly/Num31:50); by the blood upon the altar [Lv 17:11](https://ref.ly/Lev17:11); in the ritual עֲלֵיהֶם, עָלָיו by ministry of priest through the blood of the sin\-offering [Lv 4:20](https://ref.ly/Lev4:20), [4:31](https://ref.ly/Lev4:31), [8:34](https://ref.ly/Lev8:34), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Lev10:17), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Lev12:7), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Lev14:19), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Lev14:31), [16:30](https://ref.ly/Lev16:30), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Lev23:28), [Nu 8:12](https://ref.ly/Num8:12), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Num8:21), [15:25](https://ref.ly/Num15:25), [15:28](https://ref.ly/Num15:28), [15:28](https://ref.ly/Num15:28), [28:22](https://ref.ly/Num28:22), [28:30](https://ref.ly/Num28:30), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Num29:5), [2 Ch 29:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:24), [Ne 10:34](https://ref.ly/Neh10:34); of the trespass\-offering [Lv 5:16](https://ref.ly/Lev5:16), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Lev5:18), [5:26](https://ref.ly/Lev5:26), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Lev7:7), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21), [19:22](https://ref.ly/Lev19:22), [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8); the whole burnt\-offering [Lv 1:4](https://ref.ly/Lev1:4), [14:20](https://ref.ly/Lev14:20), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Lev16:24); by the oil used in purifying a leper [Lv 14:18](https://ref.ly/Lev14:18), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Lev14:29); by the תרומה Ez [45:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:15); by the priestly ministry in general [1 Ch 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:34); by the substitution of the Levites for the firstborn [Nu 8:19](https://ref.ly/Num8:19). Underlying all these offerings there is the conception that the persons offering are covered by that which is regarded as sufficient and satisfactory by Yahweh. (The purpose of the covering is stated [Lv 16:30](https://ref.ly/Lev16:30) תִּטְהָרוּיְכַפֵּר ´עֲלֵיכֶם לְטַהֵר אֶתְכֶם מִכֹּל חַטּאֹתֵיכֶם לִפְנֵי י \= *shall atonement be made for you to cleanse you, from all your sins shall ye be clean before Yahweh,* and [Nu 8:21](https://ref.ly/Num8:21) וַיְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיהֶם לְטַהֲרָם *and* (*Aaron*) *made atonement for them to cleanse them.*) **c.** the need of the atonement is expressed by מִן:מֵחטאתו *because of his sin* [Lv 4:26](https://ref.ly/Lev4:26), [5:6](https://ref.ly/Lev5:6), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Lev5:10), [16:34](https://ref.ly/Lev16:34); מטמאתו [Lv 14:19](https://ref.ly/Lev14:19), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Lev16:16); מזוב [Lv 15:15](https://ref.ly/Lev15:15), [15:30](https://ref.ly/Lev15:30); מֵאֲשֶׁר חָטָא [Nu 6:11](https://ref.ly/Num6:11); also עַל עַל־חַטָּאתוֺ *on account of his sin* [Lv 4:35](https://ref.ly/Lev4:35), [5:13](https://ref.ly/Lev5:13), [19:22](https://ref.ly/Lev19:22); על־שׁגגתוֺ [Lv 5:18](https://ref.ly/Lev5:18). **d.** c. בְּ instr. בְּאֵיל [Lv 5:16](https://ref.ly/Lev5:16), [19:22](https://ref.ly/Lev19:22); [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8); *with* a trespass\-offering [Lv 7:7](https://ref.ly/Lev7:7); כִּיהּהַדָּם הוּא בַּנֶּפֶשׁ יְכַפֵּר [Lv 17:11](https://ref.ly/Lev17:11) *for it is the blood with the living being that covers over* (H, see נפשׁ **3** (a); RV *by reason of the life* after De Di Kn Bähr Kau and most moderns; AV follows LXX Vulgate. Targum. so Ges Ew§ 282 a. Anm. 1 :  *‘ for the soul ’*); c. בְּ loc. בַּקֹּדֶשׁ [Lv 6:23](https://ref.ly/Lev6:23), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Lev16:17), [16:27](https://ref.ly/Lev16:27). **e.** c. בַּעַד Pers., *on behalf of* [Lv 9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Lev16:6), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Lev16:11), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Lev16:17), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Lev16:24) (by Aaron), Ez [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17) (by the prince). **Pu.** *Pf.* כֻּפַּר [Ex 29:33](https://ref.ly/Exod29:33), [Is 28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18) (but rd. תֻּפַר,—√ I. פרר,—so Targum. Hu We Che SS al. v. BrMP 209\); *Impf.* יְכֻפַּר [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33) \+ 3 t.; 3 fs. תְּכֻפָּר [Is 6:7](https://ref.ly/Isa6:7) *be covered over, atoned for.* **1\.** apart from the ritual, חטאתך תכפר [Is 6:7](https://ref.ly/Isa6:7) thy sin *shall be covered over* ( ‖וְסָר עֲוֺנְֶ; by the touch of the live coal from the altar); לָכֶם אִם־יְכֻפַּר הֶעָוֺן הַזֶּה [Is 22:14](https://ref.ly/Isa22:14) *surely this iniquity shall not be covered over;* c. בְּ instr. בזאת יכפר עון [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9) by *this* shall the iniquity *of Jacob be covered over* (‖הָסר חטאתו namely by the destruction of idolatrous objects); עון בחסד יאמת יכפר [Pr 16:6](https://ref.ly/Prov16:6) *by mercy and fidelity iniquity is covered over.* **2\.** c. לְ *for* whom, לארץ לא יכפר לדם [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33) *for the land atonement cannot be made, in view of the blood* shed in it, except by the blood of the shedder of blood; in the ritual of P, c. בְּ instr. אֲשֶׁר כֻּפַּר בָּהֶם [Ex 29:33](https://ref.ly/Exod29:33) *wherewith atonement was made* (ram of consecration).
> 
> **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְבַּפֵּר [1 S 3:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:14); c. בְּ instr. יִתְכַּפֵּר עֲוֺן בֵּית־עֵלִי בְּזֶבַח וּבְמִנְחָה אִ־ם *the iniquity of the house of Eli shall not be covered by peace offering or minchah* (in other words there was no atonement for it; cf. Pu. [Is. 22:14](https://ref.ly/Isa22:14)). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנִכַּפֵּר לָהֶם הַדָּם [Dt 21:8](https://ref.ly/Deut21:8) *and the blood shall be covered for them.*

> \[Str3725] † כִּפֻּרִים **n.pl.abstr.** atonement, only in P: ´הַטַּאת הַכּ *sin\-offering of the atonement* [Ex 30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10), [Nu 29:11](https://ref.ly/Num29:11); ´כ(ה)יוֺם *day of* (*the*) *atonement* [Lv 23:27](https://ref.ly/Lev23:27), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Lev23:28), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Lev25:9); ´אֵיל הַכּ [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8); ´עַל־הַכּ [Ex 29:36](https://ref.ly/Exod29:36); ´כֶּסֶף הכ [Ex 30:16](https://ref.ly/Exod30:16) *money of atonement.*

> \[Str3727] † כַּפֹּרֶת **n.** propitiatory, late techn. word from כּפר *cover over sin:* the older explan. *cover, lid* has no justification in usage; LXX ιλαστήριον; only P : [Ex 25:17](https://ref.ly/Exod25:17), [25:18](https://ref.ly/Exod25:18), [25:19](https://ref.ly/Exod25:19), [25:20](https://ref.ly/Exod25:20), [25:20](https://ref.ly/Exod25:20), [25:21](https://ref.ly/Exod25:21), [25:22](https://ref.ly/Exod25:22), [26:34](https://ref.ly/Exod26:34), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Exod30:6), [31:7](https://ref.ly/Exod31:7), [35:12](https://ref.ly/Exod35:12), [37:6](https://ref.ly/Exod37:6), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Exod37:7), [37:8](https://ref.ly/Exod37:8), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Exod37:9), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Exod37:9), [39:35](https://ref.ly/Exod39:35), [40:20](https://ref.ly/Exod40:20), [Lv 16:2](https://ref.ly/Lev16:2), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Lev16:2), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Lev16:13), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15), [Nu 7:89](https://ref.ly/Num7:89), and [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11); it was a slab of gold 2 ½ cubits X 1 ½ c. placed on top of the ark of the testimony. On it, and a part of it, were two golden cherubim facing each other, whose outstretched wings came together above and constituted the throne of Yahweh. When the high priest entered the Holy of Holies on the day of atonement it was necessary that this highest place of atonement should be enveloped in a cloud of incense. The blood of the sin\-offering of the atonement was then sprinkled on the face of and seven times before it. The temple proper, as distinguished from porch etc., was called ´בֵּית הַכּ [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11).

## I כָּרָה (id: BDB04641)

\[Str3738] † I. כָּרָה verb. Dig (NH *id.;* Aram כְּרָא, Eth. ከረየ፡ Ar. كَرَا (c. و and esp. ي Dozyii. 461\); Syr. ܟܪܳܐ is *be short, cut off* (i.e. *rounded off* ?), Ar. كُرَةٌ *ball;* Targum. כְּרֵי *heap* DalmGr. 109; Syr. ܟܰܪܝܳܐ Mish. כרי *id.;* As. *karê* large vessels for holding corn, etc., DlHWB 353, cf. *kirû* (dub.) WklTel Am. Vocab.; BaZMG 1887, 615 conjectures *be round* as orig √ mng., and comp. Ar. كَرِيَتِ, السَّاقُ *the leg is round*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. כָּרָה [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14), [Psalm 7:16](https://ref.ly/Ps7:16); 2 ms. כָּרִיתָ [Psalm 40:7](https://ref.ly/Ps40:7); 1 s. בָּרִיתִי [Gn 50:5](https://ref.ly/Gen50:5); 3 pl. כָּרוּ [Je 18:20](https://ref.ly/Jer18:20) \+ 3 t.; sf כָּרוּהָ [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִכְרֶה [Ex 21:33](https://ref.ly/Exod21:33); 3 mpl. וַיִּכְרוּ [Gn 26:25](https://ref.ly/Gen26:25); *Pt.* כֹּרֶה [Pr 16:27](https://ref.ly/Prov16:27), [26:27](https://ref.ly/Prov26:27); —*dig* a grave, קֶבֶר [Gn 50:5](https://ref.ly/Gen50:5) (J), cf. [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14); a well, בְּאֵר [Gn 26:25](https://ref.ly/Gen26:25) (J), [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) (song in JE); a pit, בּוֺר [Ex 21:33](https://ref.ly/Exod21:33); fig. of plotting against others [Psalm 7:16](https://ref.ly/Ps7:16); so sq. שׁוּחָה [Je 18:20](https://ref.ly/Jer18:20), [18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22), sq. שִׁיחָה [57:7](https://ref.ly/Ps57:7), [119:85](https://ref.ly/Ps119:85); sq. שַׁחַת [Pr 26:27](https://ref.ly/Prov26:27); hence כֹּרֶה רָעָה [Pr 16:27](https://ref.ly/Prov16:27) *one digging a calamity;* לִי ´אָזְנַיִם כ [Psalm 40:7](https://ref.ly/Ps40:7) ears *hast thou dug* (with allusion to the cavity of the ear) *for me,* thou hast given me the means of hearing and obeying thy will.—On [Psalm 22:17](https://ref.ly/Ps22:17) verse. II. כּוּר. **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. עַד יִכָּרֶה שַׁהַּת [Psalm 94:13](https://ref.ly/Ps94:13) *until the pit be digged* for the wicked, fig. of judgment.

> \[Str3741] † \[כָּרָה] **n.f.** cistern (or well), only pl. cstr. In נְוֺת כְּרֹת רֹעִים [Zp 2:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:6) pastures of (\= with) *wells of shepherds,* but text dub.; ´כ a gloss acc. to Köii. 176, (§ 94 a) Anm.; LXX. κρήτὴ We (כרת נות רעים והיתה, as LXX. *kereth* \= Philistia; v. further כְּרֵתִי; BöhmeZAW vii. 1887, 212 views כְּרֹת, plausibly, as erron. variant of preceding נְוֺת, so Schwallyib. x. 1890, 185, 186; Rosthstein in KauAT.

> \[Str4379] † \[מִכְרֶה] **n.m.** pit (?) only cstr. מִכְרֵדה־מֶלַךְ [Zp 2:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:9) *salt\-pit* (but not certain).

[Ho 3:2](https://ref.ly/Hos3:2), [Dt 2:6](https://ref.ly/Deut2:6); sq. \-עַל *trade in, make trade of* [Jb 6:27](https://ref.ly/Job6:27), [40:30](https://ref.ly/Job41:6).

## III כָּרָה (id: BDB04645)

\[Str3739] † III. \[כָּרָה] **vb.** give a feast (lit. perhaps *bring* (guests), *invite;* cf. As. *karû, bring, kirêtu, feast, kireti iškun, he gave a feast,* Dl :HWB 352\);—only **Qal** *Impf.*. c. acc. cogn., לָהֶם כֵּרָה גְדוֺלָהוַיִּכְרֶה [2 K 6:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:23) *and he gave a great feast for them* (the context requires this sense, but text dubious, NöZMG 1886, 724, cf. Klo).

> \[Str3740] † כֵּרָה **n.f.** a feast, [2 K 6:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:23) (si vera l.; v. foregoing).

> \[Str3742–43] כְּרוּב:91 **n.m.** cherub;—´כ [Ex 25:19](https://ref.ly/Exod25:19) \+ 26 t.; pl. כְּרוּבִים [Psalm 99:1](https://ref.ly/Ps99:1) \+ 33 t.; כְּרֻבִים [Ex 25:18](https://ref.ly/Exod25:18) \+ 29 t.; (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּרוּבָא, ܟܪܽܘܒܳܐ √ dub.; As *karâbu* \= *be gracious to, bless* DlHWB 350, but adj. *karûbu* is *great, mighty,* Idib. 352; on poss. connex. with כְּרוּב cf. Dl in BaerEzech. Xiii.; As. *kirubu* \= *šêdu* (name of winged bull in Assyr.; v. LenOrigines 1\. 118, Eng. Tr. 126 DlPar 154\) has not been verified, cf. v. F.:ZA i. 68 f. BudgeExpos. Apr. May, 1885 TeloniZa vi, 124 ff.; the older view, connecting ´כ with γρύ Psalm and deriving from Pers. *giriften, griffen,* lacks evidence and probability.—Possibly the *thunder\-cloud* underlies the conception);—**1\.** the living chariot of the theophanic God; possibly identified with the storm\-wind [Psalm 18:11](https://ref.ly/Ps18:11) \= [2 S 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:11) וַיִּדְכַּב עַל־כְּרוּב *and he rode upon a cherub* (‖ flew swiftly on the wings of the wind).**2\.** as the guards of the garden of Eden [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) (J).**3\.** as the throne of Yahweh Sabaoth, in phrase ישֵׁב הַכְּרוּבִים (צְבָאוֺת) ´י *Yahweh Sabaoth throned on the cherubim* [1 S 4:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:4), [2 S 6:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:2) \= [1 Ch 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:6); the context shews that the cherubim of the ark of the covenant are referred to, and it is probable that the same reference to, and it is probable that the same reference is in [2 K 19:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:15) \= [Is 37:16](https://ref.ly/Isa37:16), [Psalm 80:2](https://ref.ly/Ps80:2), [99:1](https://ref.ly/Ps99:1). **4\.** P gives an account of: **a.** two cherubim of solid gold upon the slab of gold of the כַּפֹּרֶת facing each other with wings outstretched above, so as to constitute a basis or throne on which the glory of Yahweh appeared, and from whence He spake [Ex 25:18](https://ref.ly/Exod25:18); –22, [37:7](https://ref.ly/Exod37:7); ”9, [Nu 7:89](https://ref.ly/Num7:89); **b.** numerous cherubim woven into the texture of the inner curtains of the tabernacle and the veils [Ex 26:1](https://ref.ly/Exod26:1), [26:31](https://ref.ly/Exod26:31), [36:8](https://ref.ly/Exod36:8), [36:35](https://ref.ly/Exod36:35) **5\.** K and Ch describe the cherubim of the temple: **a.** two gigantic images of olive wood plated with gold, ten cubits high, standing in the דְּבִיר facing the door, whose wings five cubits each, extended, two of them meeting in the middle of the room to constitute the throne, two of them extending to the walls [1 K 6:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:23); –[28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:28), [8:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:6); –[7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:7), [2 Ch 3:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:10); –[13](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:13), [5:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:7); –[8](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:8); Ch (doubtless influenced by Ez) represents them as the chariot of Yahweh [1 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18); **b.** images of cherubim were carved on the gold plated cedar planks which constituted the inner walls of the temple, and upon the olive wood doors [1 K 6:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:29); –[35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35), [2 Ch 3:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:7); and on the bases of the portable lavers, interchanging with lions and oxen [1 K 7:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:29); –[36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:36); Ch also represents that they were woven in the veil of the דְּבִיר [2 Ch 3:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:14). **6\.** Ezekiel describes the cherubim: **a.** as four living creatures, each with four faces, lion, ox, eagle, and man, having the figure and hands of men, and the feet of calves. Each has four wings, two of which are stretched upward, meeting above and sustaining the throne of Yahweh; two of them stretched downwards so as to cover the creatures themselves. The cherubim never turn but go straight forward, as do the wheels of the cherubic chariot, and they are full of eyes and are like burning coals of fire, Ez [1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:5); –[28](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:28), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:3) [10:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:1); –[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:20), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:22); the king of Tyre is scornfully compared with one of these, and is assigned a residence in Eden and the mountain of God Ez [28:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:14); –[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:16); **b.** Ez knows of no cherubic statues in the new temple, but represents the inner walls of the temple as carved with alternating palm trees and cherubim, each with two faces, the lion looking on one side, the man on the other. It is evident that the number and the form of the cherubim vary in the representations (cf. Ez [41:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:18); –[25](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:25)). It is probable that the שְרָפִים of [Is 6:2](https://ref.ly/Isa6:2); –[6](https://ref.ly/Isa6:6) are another form of the cherubim. The Apoc. of the seals Rev 4–6 combines them in four ζῷα.

RSOTJC 249, 2d ed.262\) לַכָּרִי וְלָרָצִים [2 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:4) cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:19); הכרי [2 S 20:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:23) Kt (\< Qr הַכְּרֵתִי which We Dr Bu prefer).

## כָּרַע (id: BDB04668)

\[Str3766] † כָּרַע bow down (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּרַע; Ar. كَرَعَ *put one's mouth* into water, or water\-vessel; i.e. kneel to drink ? denom. fr. foll. ?—**Qal** *pf.* 3 m. ´כ [Gn 49:6](https://ref.ly/Gen49:6) \+ 6 t.; 3 mpl. כָּֽרְעוּ Ju [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6) \+ 4 t.; *Impf.* יִכְרַע Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5) \+ 3 t.; 1 s. וָאֶכְרְעָה [Ezr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:5); 3 mpl. יִכְרְעוּן [Jb 31:10](https://ref.ly/Job31:10); 2 mpl. תִּכְרָעוּ [Is 65:12](https://ref.ly/Isa65:12); 1 pl. וְנִכְרָעָה [Psalm 95:6](https://ref.ly/Ps95:6) \+ 6 t. Impf.; *Inf. cstr.* כְּרֹעַ [1 K 8:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:54); *pt.* כֹּרֵעַ [Est 3:5](https://ref.ly/Esth3:5); pl. כֹּרְעִים [Est 3:2](https://ref.ly/Esth3:2); f. כֹּרְעוֺת [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4).—**1\.** *bow* על ברכים to drink Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6), in supplication to Elijah [2 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:13); in the worship of God [1 K 8:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:34), [Ezr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:5); c. ברכים subj. and ל of God [1 K 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:18), [Is 45:23](https://ref.ly/Isa45:23); without ברכים c. לִפְנֵי [Psalm 22:30](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30), [72:9](https://ref.ly/Ps72:9); ‖השׁתחוה [2 Ch 7:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:3), [29:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:29), [Psalm 95:6](https://ref.ly/Ps95:6), worship of God, but [Est 3:2](https://ref.ly/Esth3:2), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Esth3:2), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Esth3:5) in obeisance to Haman. **2\.** *bow down,* of the couching lion [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9), [Nu 24:9](https://ref.ly/Num24:9) (both poetry). **3\.** preg. c. על, *bow down over* (in order to lie with) a woman [Jb 31:10](https://ref.ly/Job31:10). **4\.** *bow down,* of a woman in childbirth [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), so of animals [Jb 39:3](https://ref.ly/Job39:3); idols, removed by enemies [Is 46:1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Isa46:2); בִּרְכַּיִם כֹּרְעוֺת [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4) *tottering* (feeble) knees; of enemies in death, נפל\[ו]כרע *bow and fall down* Ju [5:27](https://ref.ly/Judg5:27), [5:27](https://ref.ly/Judg5:27), [5:27](https://ref.ly/Judg5:27); [Psalm 20:9](https://ref.ly/Ps20:9) preg. Without נפל [2 K 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:24), [Is 65:12](https://ref.ly/Isa65:12), c. תַּחַת [Is 10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4).— **Hiph.** *pf.* 3 ms. הִכְרִיעַ [Psalm 78:31](https://ref.ly/Ps78:31); 2 fs. sf. הִכְרַעְתִּנִי Ju [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35); *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּכְרִיעַ [2 S 22:40](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:40) \= [Psalm 18:40](https://ref.ly/Ps18:40); *Imv.* sf. הַכְרִיעֵהוּ [Psalm 17:13](https://ref.ly/Ps17:13); *Inf. abs.* הַכְדֵעַ Ju [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35).—**1\.** cause to *bow in grief* Ju [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35), [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35). **2\.** cause to *bow down in death* [Psalm 17:13](https://ref.ly/Ps17:13), [78:31](https://ref.ly/Ps78:31), c. תַּחַת [2 S 22:40](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:40) \= [Psalm 18:40](https://ref.ly/Ps18:40).

(\= 129\.97 lbs.) acc. to older (Bab.) standard, later 49\.11 kg. (\= 108\.29 lbs.) and less; v. BenzArch. 187 ff. 194 NowArch. i. 208 f.—See also שֶׁקֶל.

## כרש (id: BDB04675)

כרש √ of foll. (Ar. كَرِشَ *be wrinkled*).

> \[Str3770] † \[כָּרֵש] **n.\[m.]** belly (NH כֶּרֶס *id.;* Aram. כַּרְסָא ܟܪܣܐ *id.;* Mand. כראס, כארסא, *uterus,* NöM 151, 157; Ar. كَرِشٌ Eth. ክርሽ፡ *wrinkled* or *folded stomach* of cud\-chewing animals; cf. LagBN 20, 44; As. *karšu, body, belly*);—only sf. כְּרֵשוֺ [Je 51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34) of Nebuchadrezzar under fig. of sea\-monster.

> \[Str3566] כֹּרֶשׁ **n.pr.m.** V. כּוֺרֶשׁ. p.468

> \[Str3771] † כַּרְשְׁנָא **n.pr.m.** a Pers. prince [Est 1:14](https://ref.ly/Esth1:14).

* **Associated Passages:** Esther 1:14; Jeremiah 51:34

## כָּשָה (id: BDB04687)

\[Str3780] † \[כָּשָה] **vb. be sated, gorged** with food (cf. Ar. كَشِيءَ *be filled with food;* As. *kissatum, sustenance, provender, food,* ZephnpfBAS i. 503\);—only **Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. שָׁמַנְתָּ עָבִיתָ כָּשִיתָ [Dt 32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15) *thou grewest fat, becamest thick, wast gorged !* fig. of Isr. as fat beast (cf. Dr).

> \[Str3570] כֻּשִׁיִּים כֻּשִׁית v. כּוּשִׁי. p. 469

## כָּשַׁל (id: BDB04689)

\[Str3782] † כָּשַׁל **vb. stumble, stagger, totter** (NH *id.;* Syr. \[ܟܫܰܠ] *give offence* (occasion for stumbling), σκανδαλιζω, (chiefly Ethpe. Aph.), v. PS Brock Schwally; Ar. كَسِلَ is *be heavy, sluggish*);—**Qal** *Pf.* ´כ [Ne 4:4](https://ref.ly/Neh4:4) \+, ([Ho 5:5b](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5) rd. יִכָּשֵׁל, cf. We), כָּֽשְׁלָה [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), [59:14](https://ref.ly/Isa59:14); כָּֽשְׁלוּ [Je 46:6](https://ref.ly/Jer46:6) \+ , כָּשַׁלְנוּ [Is 59:10](https://ref.ly/Isa59:10), etc.; *Impf.* 3 mpl. יכשָׁולו [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16) Kt (but rd. Hiph., q.v., so Qr); יכשׁלו [Na 3:3](https://ref.ly/Nah3:3) Kt

 (but rd. Pf. וְכָֽשְׁלוּ so Qr); *Inf. abs.* כָּשׁוֺל [Is 40:30](https://ref.ly/Isa40:30) (c. Niph. Impf.); *Pt. act.* כּוֺשֵׁל [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27) \+ 2 t., כּשֵׁל [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), [Psalm 105:37](https://ref.ly/Ps105:37); fpl. כּשְׁלוֺת [Is 35:3](https://ref.ly/Isa35:3); —**1\.** *stumble* at, over, something, c. ב (lit. *by means of*), [Na 3:3](https://ref.ly/Nah3:3), ´גִּבּוֺר בְּגִבּוֺר כ [Je 46:13](https://ref.ly/Jer46:13) (\+ נָפַל), cf. [Lv 26:37](https://ref.ly/Lev26:37); ´וּנְעָרִיס בָּעֵץ כ [La 5:13](https://ref.ly/Lam5:13) i.e. *stagger* (of toil in captivity); abs. [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27) ( ‖עָיֵף), כָּֽשְׁלוּ וְנָפָלוּ [Je 46:6](https://ref.ly/Jer46:6), cf. ´הִרְבָּה כּוֺשֵָׁל גַּם־נָפַל אישׁ וגו v:[16](https://ref.ly/Jer46:16) (strange; Gie foll. in part LXX SyrVer, עֶרְבְּ כָּשַׁל וְנָפַל *thy mixed people*—cf. [25:20](https://ref.ly/Jer25:20), etc.—*hath stumbled and hath fallen*); oft. fig. of overthrow, through divine judgment, c. בְּ *at,* (v. supr.) [Je 6:21](https://ref.ly/Jer6:21), so [Is 8:15](https://ref.ly/Isa8:15) (\+ נָפַל etc.) acc. to Ges Hi Bhul Che Di; \< בָּם \= *among them,*—Isr. and Judah,—as ב in v:[16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16), so Ew De Du EV; [Ho 4:5](https://ref.ly/Hos4:5), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Hos4:5), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Hos14:2) (בַּעֲוֺנְֶֽ, בְּ prob. instr.); abs. of Babylon, under fig. of זָדוֺן, ונפל ´כ [Je 50:32](https://ref.ly/Jer50:32); ונפל ´כ [Psalm 27:2](https://ref.ly/Ps27:2), [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3) (both ‖נפל); of אֱמֶת [Is 59:14](https://ref.ly/Isa59:14); in metaph. of anxiety, distress [Is 59:10](https://ref.ly/Isa59:10), [Psalm 107:12](https://ref.ly/Ps107:12). **2\.** *totter,* of knees, lit. מִצּוֺם ´בִּרְכַּי כ [Psalm 109:24](https://ref.ly/Ps109:24) *my knees totter from fasting;* fig. [Is 35:3](https://ref.ly/Isa35:3) (‖יָדַיִם רָפוֺת) כּוֺשֵׁל *tottering one,* lit. [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4), [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), [Psalm 105:37](https://ref.ly/Ps105:37); hence fig., *fail,* of strength (כַּךְַ) [Ne 4:4](https://ref.ly/Neh4:4), [Psalm 31:11](https://ref.ly/Ps31:11); —Inf. abs. כָּשׁוֺל, v. sub Niph. **Niph.** *Pf.* consec. וְנִכְשַׁל [Dn 11:9](https://ref.ly/Dan11:9), וְנִכְשְׁלוּ v:[33](https://ref.ly/Dan11:33), וְנִכְשָׁלוּ v:[14](https://ref.ly/Dan11:14); *Impf.* יִכָּשֵׁל Ez [33:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:12) \+ [Ho 5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5) b (LXX. as va, so We; MT כָּשַׁל), 2 ms. תִּכָּשֵׁל pr [4:12](https://ref.ly/Hos4:12), בִּכָּֽשְׁלוּ Pr [24:17](https://ref.ly/Prov24:17) (yet v. Ges§ 51, R. 1\), בְּהִכָּֽשְׁלָם [Dn 11:34](https://ref.ly/Dan11:34); *Pt.* נִכְשָׁל [Zc 12:8](https://ref.ly/Zech12:8); pl. נִכְשָׁלִים [1 S 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:4); —**1\.** *stumble* (\= **Qal**) [Na 2:6](https://ref.ly/Nah2:6), [Is 63:13](https://ref.ly/Isa63:13), [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); usu. fig.: **a.** of misfortune [Pr 24:17](https://ref.ly/Prov24:17) (‖נפל), neg. [4:12](https://ref.ly/Prov4:12). **b.** of divine judgement (c. בְּ *at*) [Pr 4:19](https://ref.ly/Prov4:19), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Prov24:16), neg. Ez [33:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:12); see also [Ho 14:10](https://ref.ly/Hos14:10) (בְloc.), [Je 20:11](https://ref.ly/Jer20:11), [Dn 11:14](https://ref.ly/Dan11:14), [11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35), יִכָּֽשְׁלוּ וְיאֹבְדוּ מִפָּנֶיְ [Psalm. 9:4](https://ref.ly/Ps9:4); esp. \= *be overthrown,* of nations, armies, etc., [Ho 5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5) (c. בַּעֲוֺנָם, cf. [14:2](https://ref.ly/Hos14:2) **Qal**), [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15) ‖נפל, cf. [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12) (‖ *id.*), [Dn 11:19](https://ref.ly/Dan11:19) ( \+ נפל), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Dan11:33) (בְ instr.), v:[34](https://ref.ly/Dan11:34), v:[41](https://ref.ly/Dan11:41). **2\.** *be tottering, feeble,* כָּשׁוֺל יִכָּשֵָׁלוּ [Is 40:30](https://ref.ly/Isa40:30) (opp. יַחֲלִיפוּ כֹחַ v:[31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31)); so pt. [1 S 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:4), [Zc 12:8](https://ref.ly/Zech12:8). **Pi.** only apparent, תְּכַשְּׁלִי Ez [36:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:14), rd. תְּשַׁכְּלִי with Qr Vrss EV Sm Co Da (v. also **Hiph.** *ad fin.*). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִכְשִׁיל [La 1:14](https://ref.ly/Lam1:14) הִכְשַׁלְתֶּם [Mal 2:8](https://ref.ly/Mal2:8); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַכְשִׁילְ [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8); 2 fs. תַּכְשִׁלִי Ez [36:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:15) (but del. LXX Co; rd. תשׁכלי, as v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:14) other Vrss Sm Da); 3 mpl. יַכְשִׁילוּ [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16) Qr (Kt v. sub **Qal**), וַיַּכְשִׁילֻהוּ [Psalm 64:9](https://ref.ly/Ps64:9), וַיַּכְשִׁלוּם [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15) (so Targum; Gie, after LXX. SyrVer, וַיִּכְשְׁלוּ, \[\< וַיִּכָּֽשִׁלוּ]); *Inf. cstr.* הַכְשִׁיל [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8), sf הַכְשִׁילוֺ [2 Ch 28:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:23); —**1\.** *cause to stumble,* fig.: **a.** \= *bring injury* or *ruin to,* abs [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16); of punishment [Psalm 64:9](https://ref.ly/Ps64:9) (indef. Subj.; si vera l.; text prob. corrupt, v. Che Bae). **b.** \= *overthrow,* of nation, army, etc., [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8) (sq. לִפְנֵי אוֺיֵב) cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8) (opp. עזר); *cause overthrow* [2 Ch 28:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:23).—On Ez [36:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:15) v. supr. **c.** morally, [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15) (בְּ loc.), [Mal 2:8](https://ref.ly/Mal2:8) (בְּ instr.). **2\.** *make feeble, weak,* הִכְשִׁיל כֹּחִי [La 1:14](https://ref.ly/Lam1:14) (of God's dealing with Jerus.) **Hoph.** only *Pt.* והיו מֻכְשָׁלִים לְפָנֶיְ [Je 18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23) either *and let them be ones who have stumbled before thee,* i.e. regard them as such (Hi Gf VB), or *and let them be ones overthrown before thee* (Gie; so Qr acc. to Gf). So also in Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) acc. to Co, cf. Sm Da (MT מִכְשֹׁלִים, v. מִכְשׁוֺל). 

> \[Str3781] † כַשִּׁיל **n.\[m.]** axe (acc. to Vrss and context; NH *id.;* Targum [Je 46:22](https://ref.ly/Jer46:22); prob. fr. *felling;* Aram. loan\-word acc. to Frä74; but word not common in Aram.)—וְכַילַפּוֺת יַהֲלֽמוּן ´בְּכ [Psalm 74:6](https://ref.ly/Ps74:6).

> \[Str3783] † כִּשָּׁלוֺן **n.\[m.]** astumbling; fig. \= calamity [Pr 16:18](https://ref.ly/Prov16:18).

> \[Str4383] † מִכְשׁוֺל **n.m.**:[Je 6:21](https://ref.ly/Jer6:21) a stumbling, means or occasion of stumbling, stumbling\-block;—´מ abs. [Is 8:14](https://ref.ly/Isa8:14) \+ 3 t., מִכְשֹׁל [Lv 19:14](https://ref.ly/Lev19:14); cstr. מִכְשׁוֺל [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31) \+ 6 t. Ez; pl. מִכְשֹׁלִים [Je 6:21](https://ref.ly/Jer6:21) \+ Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) MT (Co al. מֻכְשָׁלִים, v. *infr.*);—**1\.** *stumbling,* ´צוּר מ [Is 8:14](https://ref.ly/Isa8:14) (fig) *a rock of stumbling* (i.e. over which one stumbles); lit. הַרְבֵּה הַמִּכְשֹׁלִים Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) *an abundance of stumblings, falls* (si vera l.; LXX. οἱ ἀσθενοῦντες, appar. reading pt., cf. Sm; so Co Da (as a possibility), i.e. מֻכְשָׁלִים **Hoph.** *Pt.* of כשׁל \= *those who have stumbled or been overthrown*). **2\.** *means, or occasion of stumbling, stumbling\-block;* **a** lit. [Lv 19:14](https://ref.ly/Lev19:14). **b.** fig. of misfortune, calamity [Psalm 119:165](https://ref.ly/Ps119:165); in divine judgment [Je 6:21](https://ref.ly/Jer6:21), Ez [3:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:20), as a hindrance to restoration of people [Is 57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14). **c.** in ethical sense עֲוֺנָם ´מ Ez [7:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:19) *a stumbling\-block of* (i.e. occasioning) *their iniquity,* so [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:4), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:7), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:30), [44:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:12) (mostly with ref. to idols.). **d.** לֵב ´מ [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31) *a stumbling\-block of heart* i.e. ground for remorse.

> \[Str4384] † מַכְשֵׁלָה **n.f. 1\.** overthrown mass. **2\.** stumbling\-block;—**1\.** fig. of kingdom [Is 3:6](https://ref.ly/Isa3:6). **2\.** pl. הַמַּכְשֵׁלוֺת [Zp 1:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3) *stumbling\-blocks*\=idols (cf. מִכְשׁוֺל **2 c**), but rather dub.; We thinks a gloss. SchwallyZAW x. (1890\), 169 rds. וְכִשַּׁלְתִּי *and I will cause* the wicked to *stumble.*

## כשׁף (id: BDB04694)

I כשׁף (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. كَسَفَ *cut off, cut up,* Syr. ܐܶܬܟܰܫܰܦ *to pray* (lit. prob. *to cut oneself,* v. [1 K 18:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:28)): so RSJ Phil. xiv. 125, 126 NöZMG 1886, 723; acc. to RS כֶּשֶׁף is prop. herbs etc. *shredded* into a magic brew).

> \[Str3785] † \[כֶּ֫שֶׁף] **n.m.**:[2 K 9:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:22) **sorcery,** only **pl.** (As. *kisûpu, id.*);—כְּשָׁפִים [Mi 5:11](https://ref.ly/Mic5:11), [Na 3:4](https://ref.ly/Nah3:4); sf. כְּשָׁפַיִח [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12); כְּשָׁפֶיהָ [Na 3:4](https://ref.ly/Nah3:4), [2 K 9:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:22); —*sorceries:* **1\.** lit. מִיָּדֶיְ ´וְהִכְרַתִּי כ [Mi 5:11](https://ref.ly/Mic5:11), said of Isr.; ´רֹב כ [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12) ‖חֲבָרַיִח, said of Babylon. **2\.** fig. of seductive and corrupting influences: of Jezebel [2 K 9:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:22) ‖זְנוּנִים; of Nineveh personif. as harlot [Na 3:4](https://ref.ly/Nah3:4) (‖ *id.*), called ´בַּעֲלַת כ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Nah3:4) (following זוֺנָהמֵרֹב זְנוּנֵי).

> \[Str3784] † כִּשֵּׁף **vb. Pi. denom.** practice sorcery (As. *kašâpu, id.);—Pf.* 3 ms.: וְעוֺנֵן וְנִחֵשׁ וְכִשֵּׁף וְעָשָה אוֺב וְיִדְּעוִֺ\+ני [2 Ch 33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6) (of Manasseh); elsewh. only *Pt.* as subst. ms. *sorcerer,* in Israel, מְכַשֵּׁף [Dt 18:10](https://ref.ly/Deut18:10) (following קֹסֵם קְסָמִים מְעוֺנֵן וּמְנַחֵשׁ); fs. מְכַשֵּׁפָה \= *sorceress* [Ex 22:17](https://ref.ly/Exod22:17) (E); mpl. מְכַשְּׁפִים [Mal 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mal3:5) (named with adulterers and falseswearers); of diviners, or astrologers in Egypt (חַרְטֻמִּים q.v.) [Ex 7:11](https://ref.ly/Exod7:11) (P; ‖חֲכָמִים); in Babylon [Dn 2:2](https://ref.ly/Dan2:2) (\+ חַרְטֻמִּים, אַשָּׁפִים, כַּשְדִּים).

> \[Str3786] † \[כַּשָּׁף] **n.m.** sorcerer, only pl. sf. (As. *kassapu, id.; kassaptu, sorceress*);—אַל־תִּשְׁמְעוּ, וְאֶל־כַּשָּֽׁפֵיכֶָם אֶל־נְבִיאֵיכֶם וְאֶל־קֹסְמֵיכֶם וְאֶל חֲלֽמֹתֵיכֶם וְאֶל־עְֹנַנֵיכֶם

* **Associated Passages:** Nahum 3:4; Malachi 3:5; Exodus 22:17; 1 Kings 18:28; Daniel 2:2; Isaiah 47:9; 2 Chronicles 33:6; Exodus 7:11; Deuteronomy 18:10; Micah 5:11; Isaiah 47:12; 2 Kings 9:22


## כשׁף (id: BDB04698)

II כשׁף (√ of following).

> \[Str407] † אֶַכְשָׁף **n.pr.loc.** in (Northern) Canaan, with a king [Jos 11:1](https://ref.ly/Josh11:1), [12:20](https://ref.ly/Josh12:20); situated on the border of Asher [19:25](https://ref.ly/Josh19:25), Egypt. *'A\-k\-sap* WMMAs. u. Eur. 181; LXX B Αζειφ A Ἀχσαφ, LXXL (Ἀ) χασαφ, etc.; site dub.; mod. *Iksâf or Kesâf,* RobBR iii. 55, c. 17 m. E. of Tyre, and nearly 3 m. SW. of the great bend of the Liṭâny, is phonetically suitable, but much too far NE. for [Jos 19:25](https://ref.ly/Josh19:25); poss. there were two *Akšaphs;* cf. Di:[Jos 11:1](https://ref.ly/Josh11:1) BuhlGeogr.237\. Vid. further KrallTyrus U. Sidon 10, LagOnom. 218\. 91, 3; 2d ed. 239\.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 19:25; Joshua 11:1; Joshua 12:20


## כָּשֵׁר (id: BDB04700)

\[Str3787] † כָּשֵׁר **vb.** be advantageous, proper, suitable, succeed, late Aram. (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּשָׁר, ܟܫܰܪ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְכָשֵׁר הַדָּבָר לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶח [Est 8:5](https://ref.ly/Esth8:5) *and the thing be proper in the view of the king; Impf.* יִכְשָׁר֙ [Ec 11:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:6) thou knowest not whether this *shall succeed,* or this. **Hiph.** *Inf. cstr.* וְיִתְרוֺן הַכְשֵׁיר חָכְמָה [Ec 10:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:10) *an advantage for giving success is wisdom.*

> \[Str3788] † כִּשְׁרוֺן **n.\[m.]** skill, success;—**1\.** *skill,* בְּחָכְמָה וּבְדַעַת וּבְכִשְׁרָוֺן [Ec 2:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:21); cstr. כָּל־כִּשְׁרוֺן הַמַּעֲשֶה [4:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:4) *all skill of work* \= skilful work. **2\.** *success, profit,* וּמַה־כִּשְׁרוֺן לִבְעָלָיהָ [Ec 5:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:10) *and what profit have the owners of it* ?

> \[Str3574] † \[כּוֺשָׁרָה] **n.f.** prosperity (cf. ܟܽܘܫܳܪ *id.*). Only pl. intens. בַּכּוֺשָׁרָוֺת [Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7) he bringeth out captives *into prosperity.*

> \[Str3601] † כִּישׁוֺר **n.\[m.]** distaff (etym. Dub.; perh., if meaning correct, fr. כשׁר (cf. Sta§ 216 LagBN 182\) \= *be straight,* because it stands erect, De Str; SyrVer ܟܰܫܺܝܪܽܘܬܳܐ *industry;* whence Targum כּוּשְׁרָא, and likewise Targum כּוּנְשְׁרָא, also [Pr 3:8](https://ref.ly/Prov3:8), (for Heb. שׁר), where Levy *wirbelsäule,* Strad loc. Jastr *navel*; but Str doubts etymological connexion with כישׁור);—only in יָדֶיהָ שִׁלְּחָה בַכִּישׁוָֺר [Pr 31:19](https://ref.ly/Prov31:19) (‖:פָּֽלָךְ *whirl* of spindle).—As above De Now Str RV SS al.; \> Ki AW Thes RobGes *whirl* of spindle (AV *spindle*); vid. פֶּלֶךְ.

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 4:4; Proverbs 31:19; Ecclesiastes 5:10; Esther 8:5; Psalms 68:7; Ecclesiastes 11:6; Ecclesiastes 2:21; Ecclesiastes 10:10; Proverbs 3:8

## כָּתַר (id: BDB04721)

\[Str3803] † \[כָּתַר] **vb.** surround (in **Pi.**), (Aram. כַּתַּר Pa., *wait, hope* for; ܟܰܬܰܪ Pa. *wait, await, remain:* i.e. perh. *surrounded expectantly*, v. [Jb 36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2) infr.)—**Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. כִּתְּרוּ Ju [20:43](https://ref.ly/Judg20:43) of *surrounding* an enemy; sf. כִּתְּרוּנִי [Psalm. 22:13](https://ref.ly/Ps22:13) (in fig.; ‖סְבָבוּנִי); *Imv.* כַּתַּר־לִי [Jb 36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2) *wait, I pray* (as in Aram.) **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. בִּי יַכְּתִּרוּ צַדִּיקִים [Psalm. 142:8](https://ref.ly/Ps142:8) dub., *throw out crowns* (Ges§ 53 g), i.e. appear with crowns (denom. fr. כֶּתֶר; but this very late—only in Est.); fig. for *triumph*, because of me; but Gr Che יִתְפָּֽאֲרוּ (v. I. פאר); יַכְתִּרוּ דָעַת [Pr 14:18](https://ref.ly/Prov14:18) dub., Thes De al. *throw out knowledge as a crown*, make knowledge their crown (denom. fr. כֶּתֶר; but v. supr.), Now, *encompass knowledge*, i.e. possess it (‖נָֽחֲלוּ); sense good, but meaning of ´כ without ‖. *Pt.* מַכְתִּיר *surrounding* (as **Pi.**) Hb [1:4](https://ref.ly/Hab1:4) (c. acc. of enemy).

> \[Str3804] † כֶּ֫תֶר **n.m.** crown ((perh. Pers. loan\-word LagGes Abh. 207\); NH *id.;* Aram. כִּתְרָא; Ar. كَ/ِت;رٌ *the higher hump* of a camel);—always cstr., in combin מַלְכוּת ´כ *royal crown* [Est 1:11](https://ref.ly/Esth1:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Esth2:17) (both of queen), [6:8](https://ref.ly/Esth6:8) (of king).

> \[Str3805] כֹּתֶ֫רֶת, כּוֺתֶ֫רֶת **n.f.** capital of pillar (as *surrounding, crowning* its top ?);—abs. כֹּתֶרֶת [1 K 7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16), [7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16) \+ 10 t.; הַכּוֺתֶרֶת [Je 52:22](https://ref.ly/Jer52:22) c.; pl. abs., כֹּתָרֹת [1 K 7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16) \+ 7 t., כֹּתָרוֺת [2 Ch 4:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:12), [4:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:12), [4:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:13);—*capitals* of pillars יָכִין and בֹּעַז [1 K 7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16), [7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16), [7:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:16) \+ 12 t. 1 K 7 \= [2 Ch 4:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:12), [4:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:12), [4:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:13), [2 K 25:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:17), [25:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:17), [25:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:17) \= [Je 52:22](https://ref.ly/Jer52:22), [52:22](https://ref.ly/Jer52:22), [52:22](https://ref.ly/Jer52:22).

## כָּתַשׁ (id: BDB04724)

\[Str3806] † \[כָּתַשׁ] **vb.** pound, pound fine, bray (NH *id.;* Aram כְּתַשׁ; Syr. ܟܬܰܫ is *strive, contend;* Zinj. כרש *break in pieces, shatter*, DHMSendsch. 58\);—**Qal** *Impf.* אִם־תִּכְתּוֺשׁ אֶת־הָאֱוִיל בַּמַּכְתֵּשׁ [Pr 27:22](https://ref.ly/Prov27:22) *if thou shouldest bray the fool in the mortar.*

> \[Str4388–89] † מַכְתֵּשׁ **n.m.** mortar (place of pounding, braying; cf. Palm. n.pr.m. מכתש \= *contundens* VogNo. 97\)—abs. ´מ [Pr 27:22](https://ref.ly/Prov27:22) (v. foregoing); hollow resembling a mortar Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19) (whence came forth a water\-spring; cf. GFM), [Zp 1:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:11) \= a part of Jerusalem, cf. SchwallyZAW x. (1890\), 173 f.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 15:19; Proverbs 27:22; Zephaniah 1:11


## כָּתַת (id: BDB04726)

\[Str3807] † \[כָּתַת] **vb.** beat, crush by beating (NH *id.;* Aram. כְּתַת);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. וְכַתּוֺתִ֫י [Psalm 89:24](https://ref.ly/Ps89:24); *Impf.* 1 s. וָאֶבֹּת [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21); *Imv.* mpl. כֹּ֫תּוּ [Jo 4:10](https://ref.ly/Joel4:10); *Pt. pass.* כָּתוּת [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14), [Lv 22:24](https://ref.ly/Lev22:24); —**1\.** *beat or crush fine,* of a potter's vessel [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14), the golden calf [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21) (\+ טָחוֺן הֵיטֵב); a sacrificial vicitim [Lv 22:24](https://ref.ly/Lev22:24) (i.e. its testicles; \+ מָעוּח נָתוּק כָּרוּת); fig. of enemies [Psalm 89:24](https://ref.ly/Ps89:24). **2\.** *beat, hammer* (ploughshares into swords, cf. **Pi. 2**) [Jo 4:10](https://ref.ly/Joel4:10). **Pi** *Pf.* 3 ms. כִּתַּת [2 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:4), [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7); 3 mpl. וְכִּתְּתוּ consec. [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4) \+ 2 t.;—as **Qal 1\.** *beat* or *crush fine* [2 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:4), [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7) (of images); fig. of devastating the land [Zc 11:6](https://ref.ly/Zech11:6). **2\.** *beat, hammer* (swords into ploughshares, cf. **Qal 2**) [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4) \= [Mi 4:3](https://ref.ly/Mic4:3). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. וְכֻתְּתוּ [2 Ch 15:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:6) *and they were beaten in pieces,* one nation against another. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיַּכְּתוּ [Dt 1:44](https://ref.ly/Deut1:44) *beat in pieces* an enemy, sf. וַיַּכּוּם וַיַּכְּתוּם [Nu 14:45](https://ref.ly/Num14:45). **Hoph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וּשְׁאִיָּה יֻכַּת־שָׁעַר [Is 24:12](https://ref.ly/Isa24:12) *and to ruins is the gate crushed;* 3 mpl. יֻכַּתּוּ [Mi 1:7](https://ref.ly/Mic1:7) (of idol\-images); fig. of warriors [Je 46:5](https://ref.ly/Jer46:5); of frail man [Jb 4:20](https://ref.ly/Job4:20).

> \[Str3795] † כָּתִית **adj.** beaten;—only in combin. שֶׁמֶן כָּתִית *beaten oil,* i.e. oil made by beating or pounding the olives in a mortar; esp. fine and costly (cf. LevyNHWB ii. 443 sub כָּתִית);—[1 K 5:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:25), [Ex 29:40](https://ref.ly/Exod29:40), [Nu 28:5](https://ref.ly/Num28:5); לַמָּאוֺר ´שֶׁמֶן זַית זָךְ כ [Ex 27:20](https://ref.ly/Exod27:20) *pure beaten olive oil for the lamp* \= [Lv 24:2](https://ref.ly/Lev24:2) (Hex only H.P).

> \[Str4386] † \[מְכִתָּה] **n.f.** the crushed or pulverized \= coll. crushed fragments;—sg. sf. מְכִתָּתוֺ [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14) (result of כָּתוּת, va).
